### [87] [Stochastic Reset Pathfinding: Path-Level Regret for Cascading Bandits over Graph Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15440)
*Guni Sharon,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机重置路径规划（SRP）——一种在已知有向图上、边成功概率未知且静态的情境下的情节式学习问题。每情节中，智能体选定源到目标路径，若执行中边失败则重置到起点。作者将最优策略归结为开环策略，并将其纳入组合级联老虎机（CCB）框架；提出基于UCB和汤普森采样的Log-Dijkstra元算法（PathUCB和PathTS），给出PathUCB的路径级遗憾界，并实验验证了理论结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 量子中继网络中的纠缠分发、闪电网络的支付路由、不可靠网格网络中的投递等实际场景存在“全局重置”结构（失败后回到起点），但缺乏统一的理论框架来分析这类问题的最优策略和遗憾上界。

Method: 将SRP形式化为组合级联老虎机（CCB）问题，证明最优策略为开环策略；提出Log-Dijkstra元算法框架，实例化为PathUCB（上置信界）和PathTS（汤普森采样）；在路径级别推导PathUCB的遗憾界，将其分解为次优路径的复杂度C(π)，该复杂度同时考虑了每条边的前缀和后缀可靠性。

Result: 理论上：PathUCB的路径级遗憾界比边级CCB界对结构化图更紧致。实验上：在量子网络、分层DAG、网格世界和Erdos-Renyi图上验证理论；PathTS通常表现最佳；但存在对抗性实例导致PathTS不收敛，这与组合汤普森采样在乘法奖励问题上的已知指数级障碍一致。

Conclusion: 推荐将PathTS作为实际默认算法，同时需警惕存在导致其不收敛的对抗性实例。

Original Abstract: We introduce Stochastic Reset Pathfinding (SRP), an episodic learning problem on a known directed graph with unknown stationary edge success probabilities. In each episode, the agent commits to a source-to-goal path, and any edge failure during execution resets it to the source. SRP captures settings such as entanglement distribution in quantum repeater networks, payment routing on the Lightning Network, and delivery in unreliable mesh networks. We show that the global-reset structure makes the optimal policy open-loop, placing SRP within the combinatorial cascading bandit (CCB) framework. We propose a Log-Dijkstra meta-algorithm with UCB (PathUCB) and Thompson Sampling (PathTS) instantiations. Our main technical result is a path-level regret bound for PathUCB that decomposes regret over suboptimal paths via a per-path complexity C(pi) combining each edge's prefix and suffix reliability. The bound is complementary to the edge-level CCB bound and more informative on structured graphs with polynomially many source-to-goal paths. Experiments on quantum-network, layered-DAG, grid-world, and Erdos-Renyi domains support the theory and show that PathTS typically achieves the best empirical performance among the algorithms tested. We then exhibit an adversarial instance on which PathTS fails to converge, consistent with a known exponential obstruction for combinatorial Thompson Sampling on multiplicative-reward problems. We recommend PathTS as the practical default while cautioning that adversarial instances exist.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了随机重置路径规划（Stochastic Reset Pathfinding, SRP）——一个在已知有向图上的情节式学习问题，其中边的成功概率是未知且静态的。在每个情节中，智能体选定一条从源节点到目标节点的路径，执行过程中若任意边失败，则智能体被重置回源节点。SRP可以捕捉量子中继网络中的纠缠分发、闪电网络上的支付路由以及不可靠网格网络中的投递等场景。我们证明，全局重置结构使得最优策略是开环的，从而将SRP纳入组合级联老虎机（CCB）框架。我们提出了一种Log-Dijkstra元算法，并实例化为PathUCB（上置信界）和PathTS（汤普森采样）。我们的主要技术结果是PathUCB的路径级遗憾界，该界通过每条路径的复杂度C(π)将遗憾分解到次优路径上，其中复杂度C(π)结合了每条边的前缀和后缀可靠性。该界是对边级CCB界的补充，并且在具有多项式数量源到目标路径的结构化图上更具信息量。在量子网络、分层有向无环图、网格世界和Erdos-Renyi图上的实验支持了理论分析，并表明PathTS在测试的算法中通常达到最佳经验性能。随后我们展示了一个对抗性实例，在该实例上PathTS无法收敛，这与组合汤普森采样在乘法奖励问题上已知的指数级障碍一致。我们推荐PathTS作为实际默认算法，同时警告存在对抗性实例。

</details>


### [88] [Who Became Financially Vulnerable After COVID-19? A Population-Level Machine Learning Analysis Using MEPS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15446)
*Alexey Kresin,Zien Cheng,Ammar Ahad,Ebiyomare Kelvin,Manish Sivaratri,Prabhjeet Singh,Omar Aljawfi,Olabisi Ojo,Nawar Shara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用2019和2021年MEPS数据，通过逻辑回归和机器学习模型分析COVID-19前后美国医疗财务脆弱性的变化，发现贫困状况、保险覆盖和处方药支出是主要预测因素，尽管2021年脆弱人群负担有所增加，但预测因素总体保持稳定。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 美国医疗费用负担持续引发关注，COVID-19疫情可能加剧了这一问题。然而，疫情前后医疗财务脆弱性的变化及其主要预测因素是否发生结构性转变尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 使用2019年和2021年医疗支出小组调查（MEPS）数据，将自付医疗支出超过家庭收入10%定义为高财务负担。采用调查加权亚组分析、可解释逻辑回归（估计调整后优势比）、随机森林和梯度提升模型（评估预测性能），并通过时间泛化评估训练于疫情前数据的模型对疫情后数据的预测能力。

Result: 财务脆弱性与贫困状况、保险覆盖和处方药支出强相关；亚组分析显示人口群体间持续存在差异，2021年脆弱人群负担有所增加；但基于疫情前数据训练的模型在疫情后数据上预测性能仅轻度下降，表明主要预测因素在时间上保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 研究提供了COVID-19期间美国医疗财务脆弱性的人群水平评估，展示了将可解释统计建模与机器学习相结合用于人群健康研究的价值，可为未来人群健康监测、风险分层及旨在减少医疗财务障碍的卫生政策研究提供支持。

Original Abstract: The cost of healthcare remains a concern in the United States and may have been influenced by disruptions associated with the COVID-19 pandemic. This study examines healthcare financial vulnerability before and after the pandemic using Medical Expenditure Panel Survey (MEPS) data from 2019 and 2021. High financial burden was defined as out-of-pocket healthcare expenditures exceeding 10% of family income. Survey-weighted subgroup analyses were performed to obtain nationally representative estimates across demographic and socioeconomic groups.
  Descriptive analyses were complemented by interpretable logistic regression and machine learning models. Logistic regression was used to estimate adjusted odds ratios, while random forest and gradient boosting models were used to evaluate predictive performance. Temporal generalization assessed whether models trained on pre-pandemic data remained predictive when applied to post-pandemic observations.
  Financial vulnerability was strongly associated with poverty status, insurance coverage, and prescription drug spending. Subgroup analyses indicated persistent disparities across population groups, with some evidence of increased burden among vulnerable populations in 2021. Despite these differences, models trained on pre-pandemic data exhibited only modest reductions in predictive performance when evaluated on post-pandemic data, suggesting that the principal predictors of healthcare financial vulnerability remained relatively stable over time.
  These findings provide a population-level assessment of healthcare financial vulnerability during the COVID-19 period and demonstrate the value of combining interpretable statistical modeling with machine learning for population health research. The results may support future population health surveillance, risk stratification, and healthcare policy research aimed at reducing financial barriers to care.

中文摘要翻译: 在美国，医疗保健成本始终是一个令人担忧的问题，并可能受到COVID-19大流行相关干扰的影响。本研究利用2019年和2021年医疗支出小组调查（MEPS）数据，考察了大流行前后的医疗财务脆弱性。高财务负担被定义为自付医疗支出超过家庭收入的10%。研究进行了调查加权亚组分析，以获得具有全国代表性的人口统计和社会经济群体估计值。描述性分析辅以可解释的逻辑回归和机器学习模型。逻辑回归用于估计调整后的优势比，随机森林和梯度提升模型用于评估预测性能。时间泛化评估了基于大流行前数据训练的模型在应用于大流行后观测数据时是否仍具有预测能力。财务脆弱性与贫困状况、保险覆盖范围和处方药支出密切相关。亚组分析表明，各人口群体之间存在持续存在的差异，并有证据显示2021年脆弱人群的负担有所增加。尽管存在这些差异，但在大流行后数据上评估时，基于大流行前数据训练的模型预测性能仅出现适度下降，这表明医疗财务脆弱性的主要预测因素随时间推移保持相对稳定。这些发现提供了COVID-19期间医疗财务脆弱性的人群水平评估，并展示了将可解释统计建模与机器学习相结合用于人群健康研究的价值。研究结果可能有助于未来旨在减少医疗财务障碍的人群健康监测、风险分层和医疗政策研究。

</details>


### [89] [LLM4EHR: Aligning Clinical Time Series with Medical Event Sequences via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15447)
*Jingteng Li,Alexander Capstick,Louise Rigny,Iona Biggart,Neil J Sebire,Payam Barnaghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM4EHR，一种结合领域自适应大语言模型和Transformer时间序列编码器的临床基础模型，通过正则化对比学习对齐ICU EHR中的临床事件和时间序列，提升下游任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有临床基础模型未能充分挖掘EHR中临床事件与时间序列之间的共享时间结构，导致模型鲁棒性和适应性不足，下游任务性能受限。

Method: LLM4EHR模型：结合领域自适应大语言模型（处理临床事件）与Transformer时间序列编码器（处理时序数据），通过正则化对比学习目标进行时序对齐预训练，使时间序列表示以临床事件嵌入为条件进行学习。

Result: 消融实验表明LLM4EHR学习到的时间序列嵌入在多种下游临床任务中表现优异；经验证该模型能学到可迁移的临床时间序列嵌入，通过k-shot适应部署到新队列。

Conclusion: LLM4EHR通过时序对齐学习更鲁棒、泛化的临床表示，为构建更具通用性和高性能的临床基础模型提供了可行方向。

Original Abstract: Recent research in clinical machine learning, focusing on outcome predictions in intensive care unit (ICU), has shifted from bespoke supervised models to foundation models, utilising modern representation learning methods. Here, foundation models are pre-trained on mixtures of complex clinical data modalities, useful for various downstream tasks. Existing works often utilise Electronic Health Records (EHR) to provide rich and diverse patient observations to train clinical foundation models. However, existing methods do not sufficiently explore the shared temporal structures between clinical events and time series (TS) observations recorded in EHRs. This limitation potentially leads to less robust and adaptive clinical foundation models, resulting in reduced performance on downstream tasks. To fully exploit this temporal structure, we propose LLM4EHR, a new clinical foundation model trained on ICU EHR data. Combining domain adapted large language models with a transformer TS encoder, we pre-trained LLM4EHR by temporally aligning the EHR events and TS. For this, we propose a regularised contrastive objective to learn robust EHR TS representations conditioned on EHR event embeddings produced by the domain adapted LLM. Supported by an ablation study, we find that learnt EHR TS embeddings from LLM4EHR improve performance on various downstream clinical tasks with competitive performance. Further, we empirically demonstrate that LLM4EHR learns transferable clinical TS embeddings that can be deployed to new cohorts via k-shot adaptation. These findings provide a step towards building more generalisable and performant clinical foundation models.

中文摘要翻译: 近期临床机器学习研究（聚焦重症监护病房结果预测）已从定制化监督模型转向基础模型，利用现代表示学习方法。基础模型在复杂临床数据模态的混合数据上预训练，适用于多种下游任务。现有工作常利用电子健康记录提供丰富多样的患者观测数据来训练临床基础模型。然而，现有方法未能充分探索EHR中记录的临床事件与时间序列观测之间的共享时间结构。这一局限性可能导致临床基础模型鲁棒性和适应性不足，从而降低下游任务性能。为充分利用这种时间结构，我们提出LLM4EHR——一种在ICU EHR数据上训练的新型临床基础模型。通过结合领域自适应大语言模型与Transformer时间序列编码器，我们通过对齐EHR事件与时间序列进行预训练。为此，我们提出一种正则化对比学习目标，使模型以领域自适应LLM生成的临床事件嵌入为条件，学习鲁棒的EHR时间序列表示。消融研究支持表明，LLM4EHR学习到的EHR时间序列嵌入在各种下游临床任务上表现出竞争性性能提升。进一步，我们通过实验证明LLM4EHR能学到可迁移的临床时间序列嵌入，可通过k-shot适应部署到新队列。这些发现为构建更泛化且性能更优的临床基础模型迈出了重要一步。

</details>


### [90] [Relevant and Irrelevant: A Renormalization Group Analysis of Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15449)
*Parviz Haggi-Mani,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Wilson重整化群理论的语言，将Transformer的注意力机制视为对已训练MLP残差栈不动点的微扰，并探究其是相关、边缘还是无关算子。通过推导不动点位移公式，在合成马尔可夫链序列上验证了四个可检验预测，发现注意力相关性取决于数据生成过程的谱结构而非架构本身。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究对注意力机制的作用理解多停留在经验层面，缺乏一个统一的理论框架来预测注意力在何种条件下对模型性能有实质性贡献。作者希望用重整化群理论来形式化地刻画注意力机制相对于MLP基线的"相关性"。

Method: 将Transformer的注意力机制视为对已训练MLP残差栈不动点的微扰算子，利用Wilson重整化群理论框架，推导不动点位移公式，得到关于不动点几何、有效秩轮廓、层级特异性和微扰衰减谱的四个可检验预测。在合成马尔可夫链序列（可控相关长度）上进行验证实验。

Result: (1)长相关下，注意力是强相关算子，能弥补MLP无法弥合的残差损失差距，推动表示空间中的相变，有效秩在层1跳变至高于输入维度并稳定在高维平台；(2)短相关下，注意力是无关算子，Transformer收敛到与MLP相同的损失和不动点几何，但微扰衰减更快；(3)相变由第一层第一个注意力头主导，其表示偏移量是后续任何头的4倍以上；(4)长相关下注意力选择性保留慢马尔可夫模态（衰减长度动态范围5.4倍 vs MLP的1.3倍），短相关下无谱选择性。

Conclusion: 注意力的相关性不是架构的属性，而是数据生成过程谱结构的属性；一阶RG微扰框架能够预测性地解释这种差异。

Original Abstract: Using the language of Wilsonian renormalization group theory (RG), we treat the Transformer's attention mechanism as a perturbation of the trained MLP residual-stack fixed point and ask whether it constitutes a relevant, marginal, or irrelevant operator. We derive a fixed-point shift formula and obtain four testable predictions for the fixed-point geometry, effective rank profile, layer specificity, and perturbation decay spectrum. Testing these on synthetic Markov chain sequences with controlled correlation length, we find: (1) For large chains(long correlation), attention is strongly relevant: it closes a residual loss gap the MLP cannot bridge and drives a phase transition in representation space, with effective rank jumping above input dimensionality at layer 1 and stabilizing at a high-dimensional plateau. (2) For short chains(short correlation), attention is irrelevant: the Transformer converges to the same loss and fixed-point geometry as the MLP, though it contracts perturbations faster. (3) The transition is dominated by the first-layer head (L0H0), which accounts for more than 4 times the representational shift of any subsequent head, consistent with the prediction that the relevant operator acts before the MLP begins integrating out positional variation. (4) Perturbation decay experiments reveal a regime reversal: in the long correlation regime the Transformer selectively preserves slow Markov modes (5.4 times the dynamic range in decay length vs. 1.3 times for the MLP); in the short correlation regime it suppresses all modes faster than the MLP, with no spectral selectivity. Together, these results show that the relevance of attention is not a property of the architecture but of the spectral structure of the data-generating process, and that a first-order RG perturbation framework provides a predictive account of that difference.

中文摘要翻译: 利用Wilson重整化群理论（RG）的语言，我们将Transformer的注意力机制视为对已训练MLP残差栈不动点的一个微扰，并探究其构成相关、边缘还是无关算子。我们推导了一个不动点位移公式，并得到了关于不动点几何、有效秩轮廓、层级特异性以及微扰衰减谱的四个可检验预测。在具有可控相关长度的合成马尔可夫链序列上对这些预测进行测试，我们发现：(1) 对于长链（长相关），注意力是强相关的：它弥合了MLP无法跨越的残差损失缺口，并在表示空间中驱动了一场相变——有效秩在层1处跳变至高于输入维度，并在一个高维平台上稳定下来。(2) 对于短链（短相关），注意力是无关的：Transformer收敛到与MLP相同的损失和不动点几何，尽管其微扰衰减更快。(3) 该相变由第一层第一个注意力头主导，其表示偏移量是后续任何注意力头的4倍以上，这与相关算子作用于MLP开始整合掉位置变化之前的预测一致。(4) 微扰衰减实验揭示了一个体制反转：在长相关体制下，Transformer选择性地保留了慢马尔可夫模态（衰减长度的动态范围为5.4倍，而MLP为1.3倍）；在短相关体制下，它比MLP更快地抑制所有模态，且无谱选择性。综上，这些结果表明注意力的相关性并非架构的属性，而是数据生成过程谱结构的属性，并且一阶RG微扰框架为这种差异提供了可预测的解释。

</details>


### [91] [Looped Latent Attention: Cross-Loop KV Compression for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15456)
*James O' Neill,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出循环潜在注意力（LLA），一种针对循环权重共享Transformer的KV缓存后训练编码方法，利用循环轴的低秩结构实现21.3倍压缩，提升批处理容量并保持模型质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 循环权重共享Transformer虽减少了参数，但解码时仍需为每个循环步存储独立的K/V缓存，导致显存开销大。作者观察到循环索引的缓存具有高度结构化特性：对于固定token、层和头，K/V向量在循环轴上呈低秩轨迹，因此可以通过低秩编码压缩循环轴上的KV缓存。

Method: 提出循环潜在注意力（LLA），一种后训练缓存编解码器。默认的per-head编解码器压缩循环轴（循环潜在），LLA-2D进一步将头轴也折叠进同一潜在进行极端压缩。编解码器通过教师激活的SVD初始化，再经过KL散度和注意力输出蒸馏微调。解码时只在对齐注意力读取时才重建循环特异的K/V向量。

Result: 在相同缓存预算下，per-head LLA优于头部轴MLA、跨层共享、KV量化和最终循环复用等方法。在Ouro-1.4B上，以21.3倍压缩将4k上下文的批处理容量从32提升至768个序列。在长数学推理中，4倍压缩下MATH-500从0.43提升至0.66，减少了无回答生成。

Conclusion: 循环缓存在循环轴上是低秩的，但不能安全地折叠为单一状态。LLA通过利用循环轴的低秩结构实现了高效的KV缓存压缩，为循环权重共享Transformer的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Looped, weight-tied Transformers reduce parameters by reusing a block, but decoding still stores a separate K/V cache for every recurrence step. We show that this loop-indexed cache is highly structured. For a fixed token, layer and head, K/V vectors trace a short low-rank trajectory across loops, while the head and layer axes remain much flatter. We introduce Looped Latent Attention (LLA), a post-training cache codec that stores compact K and V latents and reconstructs loop-specific K/V vectors only when attention reads them. The default per-head codec compresses recurrence, while LLA-2D also folds heads into one latent for the extreme-compression regime. The codec is initialized from SVD of teacher activations and refined with KL and attention-output distillation. At matched cache budget, per-head LLA outperforms head-axis MLA, cross-layer sharing, KV quantization and final-loop reuse, showing that the recurrent cache is low-rank but not safely collapsible to a single state. The same axis advantage holds on Ouro-2.6B-Thinking and transfers to Huginn-3.5B, where an SVD codec remains near-lossless to 32x in decoder-independent evaluation. The cache reduction is exact. On one H200, the latent-store path increases measured Ouro-1.4B batch capacity at 4k context from 32 to 768 sequences at 21.3x compression. For long math rollouts, on-policy refinement on student-generated prefixes raises MATH-500 at 4x from 0.43 to 0.66 and reduces no-answer generations.

中文摘要翻译: 循环权重共享Transformer通过重用块来减少参数，但解码时仍要为每个循环步存储独立的K/V缓存。我们发现这种循环索引的缓存具有高度结构化特征。对于固定的token、层和头，K/V向量在循环步之间形成一条短的低秩轨迹，而头和层轴则保持得更平坦。我们提出循环潜在注意力（Looped Latent Attention, LLA），一种后训练缓存编解码器，它存储紧凑的K和V潜在表示，并仅在注意力读取时才重建循环特异的K/V向量。默认的per-head编解码器压缩循环维度，而LLA-2D进一步将头折叠进一个潜在表示，用于极端压缩场景。该编解码器通过教师激活的奇异值分解（SVD）初始化，并通过KL散度和注意力输出蒸馏进行精炼。在匹配的缓存预算下，per-head LLA优于头部轴MLA、跨层共享、KV量化和最终循环复用等方法，表明循环缓存是低秩的但不能安全地折叠为单一状态。相同的轴优势在Ouro-2.6B-Thinking上成立，并迁移到Huginn-3.5B，其中SVD编解码器在解码器独立评估中在32倍压缩下仍保持近乎无损。缓存缩减是精确的。在一张H200上，潜在存储路径在4k上下文下将Ouro-1.4B的批处理容量从32个序列提升到768个序列，压缩比为21.3倍。对于长数学推理任务，在学生生成前缀上的在线策略精炼将4倍压缩下的MATH-500从0.43提升至0.66，并减少了无回答生成。

</details>


### [92] [Robust Peak-cost Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15457)
*Shilpa Mukhopadhyay,Sourav Ganguly,Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Honghao Wei,Debdipta Goswami,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究鲁棒峰值代价约束强化学习（RP-CRL），旨在最大化期望奖励的同时控制轨迹中的最大代价。作者发现峰值代价约束MDP不满足零对偶间隙，并提出了基于积分概率度量的鲁棒值估计方法和替代优化框架，在保证约束满足的前提下实现鲁棒奖励性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于期望累积代价的CMDP框架无法有效处理单次大幅违规可能造成灾难性后果的安全关键场景。尽管已有可达性约束RL方法采用拉格朗日方法，但峰值代价约束MDP的对偶性质尚不明确。此外，仿真环境与真实环境之间的动力学差异也需要被考虑。

Method: 1）证明了峰值代价约束MDP可能不存在零对偶间隙；2）提出了鲁棒公式以处理仿真到真实的动力学失配；3）开发了替代优化框架；4）提出了基于积分概率度量（IPM）的鲁棒值估计方法。

Result: 理论结果表明，通过合适的超参数选择，替代解能够达到与原问题相同的鲁棒奖励值，同时约束违反不超过ε。实验证明该方法在动力学扰动下能有效保障安全性，同时保持强大的奖励性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的RP-CRL方法为峰值代价约束下的安全强化学习提供了理论支持与实用算法，能够有效处理动力学不确定性和单次大幅代价违规问题。

Original Abstract: We study robust peak-cost constrained reinforcement learning (RP-CRL), where the objective is to maximize expected reward while controlling the maximum cost encountered along a trajectory. This setting is motivated by safety-critical applications in which a single large violation can be catastrophic and therefore cannot be adequately captured by the standard CMDP framework based on expected cumulative cost. Existing reachability-constrained RL methods adopt Lagrangian-based approaches, yet the underlying duality properties of peak-cost constrained MDPs remain unclear. We show that, unlike standard CMDPs, peak-cost constrained MDPs may not admit zero duality gap. We further consider a robust formulation to address simulator-to-real-world mismatch in the transition dynamics. To solve this problem, we develop a surrogate optimization framework and a robust value estimation method based on integral probability metrics. We prove that, with appropriate hyperparameter choices, the surrogate solution attains the same robust reward value as the original problem while violating the constraint by at most epsilon. Experiments show that the proposed method effectively enforces safety under dynamics perturbations while retaining strong reward performance.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究鲁棒峰值代价约束强化学习（RP-CRL），其目标是在优化期望奖励的同时，控制轨迹中出现的最大代价。该设定受安全关键应用驱动，在这类应用中，单次大幅违规可能造成灾难性后果，因此无法被基于期望累积代价的标准CMDP框架充分捕获。现有可达性约束RL方法采用基于拉格朗日的方法，然而峰值代价约束MDP的潜在对偶性质仍不清楚。我们证明，与标准CMDP不同，峰值代价约束MDP可能不存在零对偶间隙。我们进一步考虑了一种鲁棒公式，以处理转移动力学中从仿真到真实环境的失配。为解决该问题，我们开发了一个替代优化框架和一种基于积分概率度量的鲁棒值估计方法。我们证明，通过合适的超参数选择，替代解能够达到与原问题相同的鲁棒奖励值，同时约束违反不超过ε。实验表明，所提方法在动力学扰动下能有效保障安全性，同时保持强大的奖励性能。

</details>


### [93] [ADS-C: Antidistillation Sampling for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15467)
*Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADS-C，一种针对分类任务的抗知识蒸馏防御方法。该方法通过封闭形式的逐样本边际预算对输出概率施加扰动，在完全保持教师模型准确率（效用成本为零）的前提下，使学生蒸馏模型性能显著下降（CIFAR-100下降17.4个百分点，CIFAR-10下降29.6，Tiny-ImageNet下降13.3），实现了首个零效用成本的抗蒸馏分类防御。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有针对大语言模型的抗蒸馏采样方法（Antidistillation sampling）在迁移到分类任务时存在缺陷：由于分类器过度自信，直接迁移存在一个惰性窗口——扰动阈值以下对攻防双方均无影响，阈值以上则反而加速教师模型性能退化。亟需一种适用于分类任务、且不牺牲教师模型效用的抗蒸馏防御方法。

Method: 提出ADS-C（Antidistillation Sampling for Classification）方法，对教师模型输出的概率分布施加输入相关的梯度定向扰动，但扰动幅度受限于一个封闭形式的逐样本边际预算（per-input margin budget），该预算确保教师模型的每个top-1预测结果在扰动前后不变，从而教师准确率与未防御时完全相同。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、CIFAR-10、Tiny-ImageNet上，ADS-C保持教师准确率不变，但使学生蒸馏精度分别下降17.4、29.6、13.3个百分点。同等学生退化程度下，未修改的防御需牺牲教师27.5、32.9、22.2个百分点的准确率。由于服务标签不变，硬标签攻击者无收益，而防御后的软输出训练的学生模型甚至比硬标签基线还低29.7个百分点。

Conclusion: ADS-C是首个针对分类任务的零效用成本抗蒸馏防御方法，在完全保持教师模型准确率的前提下有效阻止了基于概率向量的知识蒸馏攻击，甚至将蒸馏激励逆转为负向激励。

Original Abstract: Knowledge distillation enables an adversary to replicate a proprietary classifier by querying its prediction interface and training a surrogate on the returned probability vectors. Antidistillation sampling, proposed for large language models, counters this threat with an input-dependent, gradient-directed perturbation of the served distribution; its transfer to classification has not been studied. Adapting the defense to classification, we show its behavior is governed by the distribution of the teacher's per-input confidence margins. Because well-trained classifiers are severely overconfident, the direct transfer exhibits an inert window: below a closed-form-predictable threshold, it affects neither attacker nor defender; beyond it, the defense undergoes a phase transition and degrades the teacher faster than the attacker's student. Temperature softening rescales the transition in closed form, and every temperature configuration lies on the same unfavorable trade-off curve. Our method, ADS-C, composes the perturbation under a closed-form, per-input margin budget that provably preserves every served top-1 prediction, so the defended teacher's accuracy equals the undefended teacher's identically. Under this guarantee the distilled student still loses 17.4 percentage points on CIFAR-100, 29.6 on CIFAR-10, and 13.3 on Tiny-ImageNet; matching this degradation with the unmodified defense costs 27.5, 32.9, and 22.2 points of teacher accuracy. Because served labels are unchanged, a hard-label attacker gains nothing, while the defended soft output trains a student up to 29.7 points below that floor: the incentive to distill served probabilities is not merely removed but reversed. To our knowledge, ADS-C is the first antidistillation defense for classification whose utility cost is exactly zero.

中文摘要翻译: 知识蒸馏使攻击者能够通过查询专有分类器的预测接口并在返回的概率向量上训练替代模型来复制该分类器。针对大语言模型提出的抗蒸馏采样方法通过输入相关的梯度定向扰动来对抗这一威胁，但其向分类任务的迁移尚未被研究。通过将该防御方法适配到分类任务，我们证明其行为由教师模型逐样本置信度边际的分布决定。由于训练良好的分类器严重过度自信，直接迁移存在一个惰性窗口：在一个封闭形式可预测的阈值以下，它对攻击者和防御者均无影响；超过该阈值后，防御发生相变，教师模型退化速度反而快于攻击者的学生模型。温度缩放以封闭形式重新调节该相变点，且每种温度配置都位于同一条不利的权衡曲线上。我们的方法ADS-C在一个封闭形式的逐样本边际预算下构造扰动，该预算可证明地保留了每个服务的top-1预测结果，因此防御后的教师准确率与未防御的教师准确率完全相同。在此保证下，蒸馏后的学生在CIFAR-100上仍损失17.4个百分点，CIFAR-10上损失29.6个百分点，Tiny-ImageNet上损失13.3个百分点；以未修改的防御实现相同的学生退化程度需要付出教师准确率27.5、32.9和22.2个百分点的代价。由于服务的标签不变，硬标签攻击者毫无收益，而防御后的软输出训练出的学生模型比该基线还低29.7个百分点：蒸馏服务概率的激励不仅被消除，而且被逆转。据我们所知，ADS-C是第一个效用成本恰好为零的抗蒸馏分类防御方法。

</details>


### [94] [Deep Learning Approaches for Sleep Apnea Classification from Polysomnographic EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15477)
*Shashank Manjunath,Mukesh Cheemakurthi,Aarti Sathyanarayana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种深度学习架构（Vision Transformers、Graph Attention Networks）和特征表示（原始时域信号、STFT频谱图、相干性图、拓扑数据分析特征）在多通道EEG上自动检测儿童睡眠呼吸暂停的效果，在2410名训练对象和575名测试对象上取得最佳AUC 0.750（基于TDA特征的Vision Transformer），并揭示了不同人口统计学和睡眠分期条件下的性能差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统多导睡眠图监测诊断睡眠呼吸暂停耗时耗力，已有研究表明睡眠呼吸暂停事件的中枢神经系统效应可通过EEG信号检测，但现有工作多使用单一特征类型和不同分类算法，缺乏系统性的跨架构与跨特征表示的比较研究。

Method: 在2410名儿童训练集和575名儿童测试集上（进行年龄和性别匹配），系统比较了Vision Transformers和Graph Attention Networks两种深度学习架构，输入特征包括：原始时域信号、短时傅里叶变换频谱图、基于相干性的图结构、以及两种拓扑数据分析（TDA）特征。

Result: 基于TDA特征的Vision Transformer模型取得最佳测试AUC为0.750。分层分析（年龄、性别、AHI严重程度、睡眠分期N1/N2/N3/REM）揭示了显著的性能差异。

Conclusion: 结果表明基于EEG的自动化阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停筛查具有可行性，同时指出了临床部署面临的关键挑战。

Original Abstract: Sleep apnea diagnosis via polysomnography remains resource intensive and relies on time consuming manual data analysis and scoring. Recent work has demonstrated that central nervous system effects of sleep apnea events can be detected through electroencephalogram (EEG) signals. However, most work uses a single feature type on various datasets combined with different classification algorithms. In this work, we present a comprehensive comparison of deep learning architectures and feature representations for automated sleep apnea detection from multichannel EEG on a single dataset of pediatric subjects. We evaluate Vision Transformers and Graph Attention Networks across distinct signal representations: raw temporal signals, short-time Fourier transform spectrograms, coherence based graphs, and two topological data analysis (TDA) derived features. Using age and sex matching of our train and test sets, we train on 2410 pediatric subjects and test on 575 pediatric subjects. We achieve a best test AUC of 0.750 using a vision transformer based model trained on TDA features. Stratified analysis across patient demographics (age, sex, AHI severity) and sleep stages (N1, N2, N3, REM) reveals significant performance variation. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of EEG based automated OSA screening while highlighting essential challenges for clinical deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 通过多导睡眠图诊断睡眠呼吸暂停仍然资源密集，并且依赖于耗时的手动数据分析和评分。近期研究表明，睡眠呼吸暂停事件的中枢神经系统效应可以通过脑电图信号检测。然而，大多数工作在不同数据集上使用单一特征类型并结合不同的分类算法。在本工作中，我们针对单一儿童数据集上的多通道脑电图，提出了一种深度学习架构与特征表示的系统性比较，用于自动睡眠呼吸暂停检测。我们评估了视觉变换器（Vision Transformer）和图注意力网络（Graph Attention Network）在不同信号表示上的表现：原始时域信号、短时傅里叶变换频谱图、基于相干性的图结构，以及两种拓扑数据分析（TDA）衍生特征。通过对训练集和测试集进行年龄和性别匹配，我们在2410名儿童受试者上训练，并在575名儿童受试者上测试。使用基于TDA特征训练的视觉变换器模型，我们取得了最佳测试AUC为0.750。跨患者人口统计学（年龄、性别、AHI严重程度）和睡眠分期（N1、N2、N3、REM）的分层分析揭示了显著的性能差异。我们的结果证明了基于EEG的自动化OSA筛查的可行性，同时指出了临床部署中存在的关键挑战。

</details>


### [95] [Inpainting Insights: Elevating Visual XAI with Photorealistic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15482)
*Josef Lindl,Mariana Chaves,Damien Garreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式图像修复技术融入LIME方法，通过生成逼真的扰动样本来替代传统扰动方法产生的伪影和分布外样本，从而提升基于扰动的图像可解释性方法的解释质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统扰动方法（如LIME）在图像上替换像素值时会产生不真实的分布外样本和可见伪影，误导模型并降低解释质量，因此需要更符合原始数据分布的扰动方式。

Method: 将生成式图像修复（generative inpainting）技术融入LIME框架，通过生成逼真的扰动样本来替代传统简单的像素替换扰动，使扰动样本更好地对齐原始数据分布。

Result: 生成式修复方法能够产生照片级逼真的扰动样本，更好对齐原始数据分布，并提升解释质量。

Conclusion: 生成式图像修复可以有效改善基于扰动的图像可解释性方法，通过产生更真实的扰动样本来提升解释质量。

Original Abstract: The increasing complexity of state-of-the-art machine learning models has made their behavior progressively harder to interpret, spurring rapid advancements in the field of eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). Among many methods proposed, perturbation-based approaches play a major role. By systematically altering (perturbing) input features, these approaches measure the impact on the model's predictions. For image data, traditional perturbation techniques, often involve replacing pixel values e.g., with a pre-defined color. However, such approaches, but also more refined deterministic techniques, generate unrealistic out-of-distribution samples and often leave visible artifacts, which can mislead the model and compromise explanation quality. In this work, we adjust LIME, a widely used perturbation-based method, to demonstrate how generative inpainting can improve perturbation-based explanations for images. We achieve photorealistic perturbed samples that align better with the original data distribution and enhance explanation quality.

中文摘要翻译: 最先进的机器学习模型日益增长的复杂性使其行为越来越难以解释，这推动了可解释人工智能（XAI）领域的快速发展。在众多提出的方法中，基于扰动的方法扮演着重要角色。通过系统地改变（扰动）输入特征，这些方法衡量其对模型预测的影响。对于图像数据，传统的扰动技术通常涉及替换像素值（例如用预定义颜色）。然而，这类方法，以及更精细的确定性技术，都会生成不真实的分步外样本，并常常留下可见伪影，这可能会误导模型并损害解释质量。在这项工作中，我们调整了LIME（一种广泛使用的基于扰动的方法），以展示生成式图像修复如何改善基于扰动的图像解释。我们实现了照片级逼真的扰动样本，使其更好地对齐原始数据分布，并提升了解释质量。

</details>


### [96] [Diffusion models recover accurate mixture weights despite score function insensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15485)
*Andrew Dennehy,Ramchandran Muthukumar,Rebecca Willett,Nisha Chandramoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于分数的生成模型（如扩散模型）虽然能覆盖多模态分布的所有模态，但可能无法正确学习模态的相对幅度（即混合权重）这一看似矛盾的现象。作者通过将扩散得分匹配（DSM）损失与混合权重估计误差联系起来，提出了扩散得分敏感性指数（DSSI），证明了DSM损失与参数估计误差的同阶关系，并验证了噪声调度对敏感性及模态放大的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解决基于分数的生成模型在理论上能覆盖所有模态但实践中混合权重（模态相对幅度）学习不准确的看似矛盾的现象，并建立理论框架来解释和量化这一行为。

Method: 1. 将扩散得分匹配（DSM）损失与生成样本中混合权重的估计误差建立理论联系。2. 定义扩散得分敏感性指数（DSSI），衡量DSM损失相对于分布参数变化的敏感度。3. 对任意维度的高斯混合分布，证明混合权重估计误差与DSM损失同阶。4. 通过实验验证噪声调度对敏感性和模态放大的影响。

Result: 1. 理论证明：在高斯混合分布下，混合权重估计误差与DSM损失处于同一数量级。2. 实验发现：在基准数据分布的加噪过程中，敏感性会显现出来，且敏感性值能预测训练良好的模型恢复混合权重的能力。3. 噪声调度的选择可以降低扩散敏感性，从而减少模态放大现象。

Conclusion: 本文提出的敏感性框架不仅适用于混合权重，还能推广到目标分布中任何定性参数的恢复问题，为理解和改进基于分数的生成模型中的模态偏差问题提供了理论基础。

Original Abstract: Score-based generative models exhibit a puzzling behavior: they often appear to cover all modes of a target multimodal distribution and yet may fail to learn the correct relative mode amplitudes, which can be interpreted as mixture weights. We resolve this apparent paradox by relating the diffusion score matching (DSM) loss to the error in estimating mixture weights from generated samples. We show that, even when the target score is insensitive to mixture weights, generated samples can recover the weights accurately if the scores at intermediate noise levels are informative about the weights. Accordingly, we define the diffusion score sensitivity index (DSSI) as the variation in the DSM loss relative to changes in a parameter. We then show that the DSSI governs the accuracy with which the parameter of the target distribution can be estimated from generated samples. For Gaussian mixtures in arbitrary dimensions, we prove that the mixture weight estimation errors are on the same order as the DSM loss under mild conditions. Empirically, we show the emergence of sensitivity during the noising process of benchmark data distributions under typical noise schedules, and that these sensitivity values predict how well a well-trained model recovers mixture weights. Furthermore, we show that the choice of noise schedule can reduce diffusion sensitivity, leading to mode amplification. Although we focus on mixture weights, the proposed sensitivity framework governs the recovery of any qualitative parameter of the target distribution.

中文摘要翻译: 基于分数的生成模型表现出一种令人费解的行为：它们通常似乎能够覆盖目标多模态分布的所有模态，然而却可能无法学习正确的模态相对幅度（可解释为混合权重）。我们通过将扩散得分匹配（DSM）损失与生成样本中混合权重的估计误差联系起来，解决了这一表面上的悖论。我们证明，即使目标得分对混合权重不敏感，只要中间噪声水平下的得分包含关于权重的信息，生成的样本仍然可以准确地恢复权重。据此，我们将扩散得分敏感性指数（DSSI）定义为DSM损失相对于参数变化的变异性。然后我们证明，DSSI决定了从生成样本中估计目标分布参数的精度。对于任意维度的高斯混合分布，我们证明在温和条件下，混合权重估计误差与DSM损失处于同一数量级。实验上，我们展示了在典型噪声调度下，基准数据分布的加噪过程中敏感性的出现，并且这些敏感性值能够预测训练良好的模型恢复混合权重的效果。此外，我们证明噪声调度的选择可以降低扩散敏感性，从而导致模态放大。尽管本文聚焦于混合权重，但所提出的敏感性框架适用于目标分布中任何定性参数的恢复。

</details>


### [97] [An Auto-Scaling Approach for Serverless Environments Based on a Multi-Expert Consensus Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15511)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Mehrdad Ashtiani,Amirhossein Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种依赖感知的无服务器自动扩缩容框架，结合图结构瓶颈识别、多模型集成预测和成本感知控制，在真实工作负载下达到99.88%预测精度并降低基础设施成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无服务器计算的自动扩缩容面临动态负载、冷启动延迟和函数间依赖关系的挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 将无服务器应用建模为有向依赖图，用加权度中心性识别关键函数；采用MLP、LSTM、CNN进行轻量级资源需求预测，通过性能加权概率集成（受贝叶斯模型平均启发）融合多模型输出；控制器结合冷启动感知和成本比较选择扩缩容动作。

Result: 监督预测大幅优于无监督聚类；集成模型达到99.88%预测精度；在多种云定价模型下持续降低基础设施成本同时满足性能目标。

Conclusion: 结合依赖分析、多专家预测和成本感知控制为无服务器自动扩缩容提供了一种稳健且实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Serverless computing provides automatic resource management and pay-per-use execution, but effective autoscaling remains challenging because of dynamic workloads, cold-start latency, and dependencies among functions. We present a dependency-aware autoscaling framework that integrates graph-based bottleneck identification, short-term workload forecasting, multi-model consensus, and cost-aware scaling control. Serverless applications are represented as directed dependency graphs, and structurally important functions are identified using weighted degree centrality. Resource demand is predicted using lightweight MLP, LSTM, and CNN models. Their outputs are combined through a performance-weighted probabilistic ensemble inspired by Bayesian model averaging. The controller further incorporates cold-start awareness and cost comparison to select among scale-up, scale-down, and hold actions. Experiments using real workload traces show that supervised forecasting substantially outperforms unsupervised clustering for autoscaling decision generation. The proposed ensemble achieves 99.88 percent prediction accuracy and reduces prediction error compared with representative hybrid forecasting methods. Evaluations across multiple cloud pricing models also demonstrate consistent infrastructure cost reductions while maintaining performance targets. The results show that combining dependency analysis, multi-expert forecasting, and cost-aware control provides a robust and practical solution for serverless autoscaling.

中文摘要翻译: 无服务器计算提供了自动资源管理和按使用付费的执行方式，但由于动态负载、冷启动延迟以及函数间的依赖关系，有效的自动扩缩容仍然充满挑战。我们提出了一种依赖感知的自动扩缩容框架，该框架融合了基于图的瓶颈识别、短期工作负载预测、多模型共识和成本感知的扩缩容控制。无服务器应用被建模为有向依赖图，并通过加权度中心性识别结构上重要的函数。资源需求预测采用轻量级MLP、LSTM和CNN模型，其输出通过一种受贝叶斯模型平均启发的性能加权概率集成方法进行融合。控制器进一步引入冷启动感知和成本比较，在扩容、缩容和保持三种动作之间进行选择。基于真实工作负载轨迹的实验表明，监督式预测在扩缩容决策生成方面显著优于无监督聚类。所提出的集成方法达到了99.88%的预测精度，并与代表性混合预测方法相比降低了预测误差。在多种云定价模型上的评估也表明，在维持性能目标的同时持续降低了基础设施成本。结果表明，将依赖分析、多专家预测和成本感知控制相结合，为无服务器自动扩缩容提供了一种稳健且实用的解决方案。

</details>


### [98] [Cache-Aware Prompt Compression:A Two-Tier Cost Model for LLM API Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15516)
*Yan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生产级LLM部署中提示缓存与提示压缩的成本交互问题，发现缓存命中率远非理想值，并提出缓存感知的提示压缩方法（CAPC），在多种配置和真实工作负载上实现了最低成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有提示压缩文献默认采用查询感知（query-aware）方法，每次调用会生成不同的压缩前缀，从而机械性地使前缀缓存失效，导致缓存命中率下降。然而这些研究通常假设缓存命中率ρ=1.0的理想情况，忽略了实际部署中缓存性能的非理想性。

Method: 提出缓存感知提示压缩（CAPC），核心包括：(1) 采用查询无关（query-agnostic）压缩以保持前缀一致性；(2) 配合显式的缓存控制指令（cache_control）；(3) 引入层级保持比率约束（tier-preserving ratio bound），防止过度压缩导致缓存前缀被推入热层级而降低缓存效率。

Result: (1) 在Sonnet API上发现缓存具有两层架构，约3500 token处存在明显阈值，低于该阈值命中率ρ~0.83；(2) 在真实ρ下，查询感知压缩在高压缩比（r≥6）时优于朴素缓存；(3) CAPC在LongBench-v2的16/16配置中成本最低，比纯缓存节省49%，比查询感知压缩节省64%，比基线节省90%，质量损失在0.05以内；(4) 在三个生产工作负载上均验证了优势，包括tau-bench基准测试中成为成本最低策略且奖励与基线完全相同。

Conclusion: 缓存感知提示压缩（CAPC）是生产级LLM部署中首个同时利用缓存折扣和压缩成本节省的有效策略，在多个真实场景中均实现了最低成本且不牺牲质量，并首次在公开基准上验证了查询感知压缩的负ROI预测。

Original Abstract: Production LLM deployments combine two cost-reduction primitives: prompt caching (a discounted rate for re-used token prefixes) and prompt compression (fewer tokens sent). The compression literature has standardized on query-aware methods that produce a different compressed prefix per query, mechanically invalidating the prefix-strict cache on every call. We characterize this cost empirically on Anthropic's Sonnet 4.6 API and find caching is far from the rho=1.0 ideal the literature assumes: Sonnet's cache has a two-tier architecture with a sharp threshold near 3,500 tokens, below which the hit rate plateaus at rho~0.83 across 30-call sessions. Our cost model predicts, and experiments confirm, that under realistic rho, query-aware compression beats naive caching at high compression ratios (r>=6). We propose Cache-Aware Prompt Compression (CAPC), pairing query-agnostic compression with explicit cache_control plus a tier-preserving ratio bound that prevents over-compression from pushing the cached prefix into the hot tier. CAPC is the cheapest strategy in 16/16 configurations on LongBench-v2, with mean savings of 49% over cache-only, 64% over query-aware compression, and 90% over vanilla, at quality within 0.05 of the uncompressed baseline. We validate CAPC on three production workloads: an enterprise tool-using assistant with a 94k-token schema prefix (51.7% cost reduction at r=3); a graphify knowledge-graph RAG pipeline across two codebases (9.3x vs cache-all on FastAPI, 2.4x on httpx); and the public tau-bench retail benchmark (50 tasks), where CAPC is the cheapest of four strategies with reward exactly equal to vanilla (both 36/50, p=1.00) while query-aware compression is the most expensive at +40.1% over vanilla -- the first production confirmation of the crossover model's negative-ROI prediction on a public benchmark.

中文摘要翻译: 生产级LLM部署结合了两种成本降低原语：提示缓存（对重复使用的令牌前缀提供折扣费率）和提示压缩（减少发送的令牌数量）。压缩文献已标准化采用查询感知方法，这些方法为每次查询生成不同的压缩前缀，从而机械性地使每次调用的前缀缓存失效。我们在Anthropic的Sonnet 4.6 API上对成本进行经验性刻画，发现缓存远非文献所假设的ρ=1.0理想值：Sonnet的缓存具有两层架构，在约3,500令牌处存在一个尖锐阈值，低于该阈值时，在30次调用的会话中命中率稳定在ρ~0.83。我们的成本模型预测并通过实验证实，在现实ρ下，查询感知压缩在高压缩比（r≥6）时优于朴素缓存。我们提出缓存感知提示压缩（CAPC），将查询无关压缩与显式缓存控制加上层级保持比率约束相结合，防止过度压缩导致缓存前缀被推入热层级。CAPC在LongBench-v2的16/16配置中是最便宜的策略，平均比纯缓存节省49%，比查询感知压缩节省64%，比基线节省90%，质量控制在无压缩基线的0.05以内。我们在三个生产工作负载上验证了CAPC：一个拥有94k令牌模式前缀的企业工具使用助手（r=3时成本降低51.7%）；跨越两个代码库的图形化知识图谱RAG管道（FastAPI上比全缓存快9.3倍，httpx上快2.4倍）；以及公开的tau-bench零售基准（50个任务），其中CAPC是四种策略中最便宜的，奖励与基线完全相等（均为36/50，p=1.00），而查询感知压缩是最昂贵的，比基线高出40.1%——这是首次在公开基准上确认交叉模型负ROI预测的生产级验证。

</details>


### [99] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15524)
*Hyunin Lee,Jinglue Xu,Jeffrey Seely,Donghyun Lee,Matei Zaharia,Yujin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归式框架自改进（RHI）方法，通过将agent框架表示为提示级规范，并利用成对反馈迭代优化，在30个合成机器学习研究任务中以少量迭代即可提升低推理能力agent的性能上限，降低高达60%的推理成本，其增益主要来自改进的上下文管理而非更长推理链。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在模型-框架协同进化范式下，框架不仅是推理时的支架，更是数据生成组件，其执行轨迹可塑造未来基础模型。然而持续更新供应商构建的框架成本高昂且劳动密集，因此研究者探究能否以任务特定方式优化用户自建框架，在保持计算轻量且只需少量更新迭代的情况下提升执行轨迹质量。

Method: 提出递归式框架自改进（Recursive Harness Self-Improvement, RHI），将框架表示为agent循环的提示级规范，基于自身修订历史利用成对反馈进行迭代优化。

Result: 在覆盖量化金融、机器人和药学的30个合成机器学习研究任务上，少量RHI迭代即可显著提升低推理能力agent的性能上限，超越对应最大推理能力设置，同时降低高达60%的推理成本；性能提升主要源于通过更有效的agent间信息流改进任务特定上下文管理，而非更长的推理链。

Conclusion: 将RHI的行为形式化为信息论假设，指出其隐式优化目标，表明RHI是模型-框架协同进化范式中持续学习的实用算法。

Original Abstract: Under model--harness co-evolution, harnesses are not merely inference-time scaffolds but data-generating components whose execution traces can shape future foundation models. This motivates harness-in-the-loop learning: optimizing harnesses for both immediate agent performance and the quality of traces used for future model training. However, continually updating provider-built scaffolds is costly and labor-intensive. We therefore investigate whether optimizing user-constructed harnesses in a task-specific manner can improve execution-trace quality while remaining computationally lightweight and requiring only a few update iterations. To this end, we introduce Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI), which represents the harness as a prompt-level specification of the agent loop and iteratively refines it using pairwise feedback over its own revision history. Across 30 synthetic machine-learning research tasks spanning quantitative finance, robotics, and pharmacy, a few RHI iterations suffice to substantially raise the performance ceiling of low-reasoning-effort agents, exceeding the corresponding maximum-reasoning-effort setting while reducing inference cost by up to 60%. We show that these gains arise primarily from improved task-specific context management through more effective inter-agent information flow rather than longer reasoning traces. Finally, we formalize this behavior as an information-theoretic hypothesis for RHI's implicit optimization objective, suggesting RHI as a practical algorithm for continual learning within the paradigm of model--harness co-evolution.

中文摘要翻译: 在模型与框架协同进化（model-harness co-evolution）的范式下，框架不仅仅是推理时的支撑结构（scaffolds），更是数据生成的组件，其执行轨迹可以塑造未来的基础模型。这催生了框架参与式学习（harness-in-the-loop learning）的理念：优化框架既要考虑当前的智能体性能，也要兼顾用于未来模型训练的执行轨迹质量。然而，持续更新供应商构建的框架成本高昂且劳动密集。因此，我们探究是否可以在任务特定方式下优化用户自建的框架，从而在保持计算轻量且只需少量更新迭代的前提下提升执行轨迹质量。为此，我们提出递归式框架自改进（Recursive Harness Self-Improvement, RHI），该方法将框架表示为智能体循环的提示级规范，并基于自身修订历史利用成对反馈进行迭代优化。在覆盖量化金融、机器人和药学的30个合成机器学习研究任务上，少量RHI迭代足以显著提升低推理能力智能体的性能上限，甚至超越对应的最大推理能力设置，同时将推理成本降低高达60%。我们表明，这些性能提升主要源于通过更有效的智能体间信息流（inter-agent information flow）改进了任务特定的上下文管理，而非依赖更长的推理解链。最后，我们将这一行为形式化为RHI隐式优化目标的信息论假设，表明RHI是模型-框架协同进化范式中一种实用的持续学习算法。

</details>


### [100] [Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15525)
*Felippe Alves,Renato Vicente*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了KAN网络的两大核心主张（可解释性与替代前馈网络能力），发现小基KAN具有可迁移的审计接口，但在标准基准测试、质量和延迟方面均不具备一致优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 验证KAN网络的两个核心主张：（1）用可学习的一维边缘函数替代固定节点激活是否能提供显式可解释接口；（2）KAN是否能够有效替代Transformer中的前馈网络。

Method: （1）在六层1000万参数B样条KAN中重建所有前馈边，进行激活分析与剪枝实验；（2）在BabyLM数据集上对比MLP、SwiGLU、分组Chebyshev、有理GR-KAN等网络；（3）进行基压缩（fPCA）和闭式拟合覆盖分析；（4）在Wikitext-103和BLiMP等基准上开展大规模对比实验。

Result: （1）87.8%的边存在非线性（NLS>0.1），仅0.4%不活跃；剪枝20-25%低活性边损失可忽略；（2）近完全的fPCA压缩和高闭式拟合覆盖率是低容量grid-2基的性质，非KAN通用行为；（3）KAN族和门控变体在验证损失上优于GELU MLP，但在标准基准测试（BLiMP、EWoK）上优势消失；（4）参数匹配的MLPEdge在Wikitext-103上不如MLP；2.86亿参数GR-KAN仍低于SwiGLU ClimbMix基线。

Conclusion: 小基KAN提供了实用的、可跨语料迁移的审计接口用于检查学习到的标量变换，但测试的替代方案在基准测试、质量和延迟方面均未表现出对强MLP基线的一致优势。

Original Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace fixed node activations with learned one-dimensional edge functions, offering an explicit interface for interpretation and a possible alternative to transformer feed-forward networks. We test these claims separately. In a six-layer, 10M-parameter B-spline KAN, we reconstruct all 884,736 feed-forward edges: 87.8\% exceed (NLS>0.1) and 0.4\% are inactive. Pruning the lowest-activity 20--25\% causes negligible loss increase, although structured MLP neuron pruning tolerates comparable sparsity. The audit replicates on BabyLM, but grid-size sweeps show that near-total fPCA compression and high closed-form-fit coverage are properties of the low-capacity grid-2 basis, not universal KAN behavior. For replacement, we evaluate MLP, SwiGLU, grouped Chebyshev, and rational GR-KAN networks on BabyLM. The KAN-family and gated variants improve validation loss over the GELU MLP, but this ordering does not transfer to standardized benchmarks: across ten seeds and 59,875 BLiMP pairs, accuracies span 62.4--63.1\%, EWoK remains at chance, and a (+0.7)-point GR-KAN effect on BLiMP reverses on the supplement. Larger tests are also cautionary: parameter-matched MLPEdge underperforms the MLP on Wikitext-103, and 286M-parameter GR-KAN remains below a SwiGLU ClimbMix baseline after stabilization. Thus, small-basis KANs provide a practical, corpus-transferable interface for auditing learned scalar transformations, but the tested replacements show no consistent benchmark, quality, or latency advantage over strong MLP baselines.

中文摘要翻译: Kolmogorov–Arnold网络（KAN）用可学习的一维边缘函数替代了固定的节点激活，从而为模型解释提供了一个显式接口，并可能替代Transformer中的前馈网络。我们分别检验了这些主张。在一个六层、1000万参数的B样条KAN中，我们重建了全部884,736条前馈边：其中87.8%表现出显著非线性（NLS>0.1），0.4%处于非活跃状态。剪除活性最低的20–25%的边仅带来可忽略的损失增加，尽管结构化MLP神经元剪枝也能容忍相当的稀疏度。该审计结果在BabyLM上可复现，但网格尺寸扫描表明，近乎完全的fPCA压缩和高闭式拟合覆盖率是低容量grid-2基的性质，而非KAN的通用行为。在替代性评估方面，我们在BabyLM上对比了MLP、SwiGLU、分组Chebyshev以及有理GR-KAN网络。KAN族和门控变体相比GELU MLP在验证损失上有所改善，但这种排序并未迁移至标准化基准测试：在十种随机种子和59,875个BLiMP测试对的设置下，准确率介于62.4–63.1%之间，EWoK仍处于随机水平，而GR-KAN在BLiMP上（+0.7个百分点的）正向效应在补充测试中发生逆转。更大规模的测试同样具有警示意义：参数匹配的MLPEdge在Wikitext-103上的表现不如MLP，而2.86亿参数的GR-KAN在稳定后仍低于SwiGLU ClimbMix基线。因此，小基KAN为审计学习到的标量变换提供了一个实用的、可跨语料迁移的接口，但所测试的替代方案在标准基准、质量或延迟方面均未显示出对强MLP基线的一致优势。

</details>


### [101] [Publicly-Verifiable Certificates for Statistical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15528)
*Michael Ngo,Michael P. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了非交互式学习证明的新概念——公开可验证的统计有效性证书（pvCSVs），允许学习者在发布假设的同时附带证书，供任意用户根据自身分布高效验证假设的有效性。在自适应统计查询（SQ）算法场景中，构造了样本复杂度仅为O(log k)的pvCSVs（k为查询次数），远优于最优学习算法的Õ(√k)样本复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 拓展Goldwasser等人提出的交互式学习证明框架至非交互式场景，定义一种新的公开可验证机制，使学习结果的验证不依赖于特定验证者，且对用户分布具有鲁棒性。

Method: 在自适应统计查询（SQ）算法框架下构造公开可验证的统计有效性证书（pvCSVs），通过设计高效的非交互式证明系统，使得证书的样本复杂度仅与查询次数的对数相关（O(log k)）。

Result: 对于进行k次自适应查询的SQ算法，所构造的pvCSVs样本复杂度为O(log k)，而最优学习算法的样本复杂度为Õ(√k)，实现了指数级的样本效率提升。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在SQ模型下存在高效的分布鲁棒的非交互式学习证明系统，同时也揭示了该模型的能力与局限性。

Original Abstract: Following Goldwasser, Rothblum, Shafer, and Yehudayoff, who defined a framework for interactive proofs of learning [ITCS'21], we initiate the study of non-interactive proofs of learning. We define and study a new notion: Publicly-Verifiable Certificates of Statistical Validity (pvCSVs), which allow for public, distributionally-robust certification that the result of a learning algorithm is valid. In a pvCSV, a learner publishes a hypothesis $h$ and corresponding certificate $π$; then, any user, who holds a user-specific distribution, can read the pair $(h,π)$ and determine efficiently whether the hypothesis is valid according to the user-specific distribution.
  We construct pvCSVs in the context of Adaptive Statistical Query (SQ) Algorithms. To certify SQ algorithms that makes $k$ adaptive queries, we construct pvCSVs where the sample complexity scales with $O(\log k)$, whereas the sample complexity of the best learning algorithms scale with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{k})$. More generally, we study proof systems for learning in the SQ model, demonstrating the model's strengths as well as its limitations.

中文摘要翻译: 继Goldwasser、Rothblum、Shafer和Yehudayoff定义了交互式学习证明框架[ITCS'21]之后，我们率先开展了非交互式学习证明的研究。我们定义并研究了一个新概念：公开可验证的统计有效性证书（pvCSVs），该证书允许对学习算法的结果进行公开的、分布鲁棒的验证。在pvCSV中，学习者发布一个假设h及其对应的证书π；随后，任何持有用户特定分布的用户都可以读取对(h,π)，并高效地判断该假设是否在用户特定分布下有效。我们在自适应统计查询（SQ）算法的背景下构造了pvCSVs。对于进行k次自适应查询的SQ算法，我们构造的pvCSVs的样本复杂度为O(log k)，而最优学习算法的样本复杂度为Õ(√k)。更广泛地，我们研究了SQ模型中学习任务的证明系统，展示了该模型的能力及其局限性。

</details>


### [102] [From Feasibility to Desirability: Plan, Learn, Adapt (PLA) Framework for Personalized On-Device Itinerary Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15552)
*Himel Dev,Tanmoy Sen,Madhusudan Basak,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PLA三阶段框架，通过轻量规划器集成生成可行候选路线，用Bradley-Terry奖励模型学习用户偏好，并在设备端进行可行性保持的局部优化，在人类对比评估中实现67.8%胜率与100%可行性，生产部署中行程完成率提升91%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统优化方法能保证可行性但无法捕捉主观偏好，学习方法能建模偏好却无法保证可行性，且移动端部署对两者都施加了额外资源约束，亟需一种既能保证可行性又能学习用户偏好的轻量级端侧方案。

Method: PLA三阶段框架：(1) Plan阶段构建轻量规划器异构集成，生成结构多样的可行候选行程；(2) Learn阶段通过成对行程比较拟合紧凑Bradley-Terry奖励模型，捕获行程节奏、地理连贯性、日间平衡等涌现属性；(3) Adapt阶段在设备感知计算预算内进行保持可行性的局部精调，确保每个中间状态均可行的。

Result: 在2519组成对人类对比中（覆盖100+美国城市），奖励引导集成达到67.8%胜率（比最佳单规划器高11.2个百分点），100%可行性；三个前沿LLM在相同约束下可行性为0%；奖励模型跨城市泛化能力达67.6%留一城准确率；在FlyEnJoy生产中行程完成率提升91%，平均端侧延迟109.9ms。

Conclusion: PLA框架证明通过轻量级异构规划器集成与紧凑偏好模型的结合，可以在移动设备上同时实现可行性与个性化偏好学习，且该方案在性能、泛化能力和部署效率上均显著优于现有方法和大语言模型方案。

Original Abstract: Generating personalized trip itineraries is a complex planning task and involves a tension between hard combinatorial feasibility and soft latent desirability. Classical optimization enforces constraints but fails to capture subjective traveler preferences. While learning-based approaches model preferences, they cannot guarantee feasibility. Mobile deployment imposes additional resource constraints on both. To address this, we propose Plan, Learn, Adapt (PLA), a three-stage framework for personalized on-device itinerary generation. The Plan stage builds a heterogeneous ensemble of lightweight planners that produces structurally diverse feasible candidates. From pairwise itinerary comparisons, Learn fits a compact Bradley-Terry reward model that captures emergent schedule properties such as pacing, geographic coherence, and day balance, which per-POI signals miss. Finally, Adapt applies feasibility-preserving local refinement within a device-aware compute budget; every intermediate state is feasible by construction. On 2,519 pairwise human comparisons across more than 100 U.S. cities, the reward-guided ensemble achieves a 67.8% win rate, 11.2 percentage points above the best single planner, with 100% feasibility. Three frontier LLMs, GPT-5, Claude Opus 4.5, and Gemini 3 Pro, achieve 0% feasibility under the same constraints. The reward model generalizes across held-out cities, with a 67.6% mean leave-one-city-out accuracy. In production deployment within FlyEnJoy, PLA increased itinerary completion rates by 91%, with 109.9 ms average on-device latency.

中文摘要翻译: 生成个性化旅行行程是一项复杂的规划任务，涉及硬组合可行性与软潜在偏好之间的紧张关系。经典优化方法能保证约束满足但无法捕捉主观旅行者偏好，而基于学习的方法虽能建模偏好却无法保证可行性。移动端部署对两者都施加了额外的资源约束。为解决这一问题，我们提出了PLA（Plan, Learn, Adapt）——一个用于个性化设备端行程生成的三阶段框架。Plan阶段构建了一个轻量级规划器的异构集成，生成结构多样的可行候选方案。Learn阶段通过成对行程比较拟合一个紧凑的Bradley-Terry奖励模型，捕获诸如行程节奏、地理连贯性和日间平衡等涌现的行程属性，这些属性是单个POI信号无法捕捉的。最后，Adapt阶段在设备感知的计算预算内进行保持可行性的局部精调；每个中间状态在构造上都是可行的。在覆盖100多个美国城市的2519组成人对比较中，奖励引导的集成达到了67.8%的胜率，比最佳单规划器高出11.2个百分点，且可行性为100%。三个前沿大语言模型——GPT-5、Claude Opus 4.5和Gemini 3 Pro——在相同约束下可行性均为0%。奖励模型在留出城市上具有良好的泛化能力，平均留一城市准确率为67.6%。在FlyEnJoy的生产部署中，PLA使行程完成率提升了91%，平均设备端延迟为109.9毫秒。

</details>


### [103] [Hard Rules, Soft Preferences: Bridging Reasoning, Learning, and Optimization for Personalized Packing Checklist Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15562)
*Himel Dev,Madhusudan Basak,Tanmoy Sen,Paromita Shome,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种推理引导的学习框架，通过符号引擎生成合规行李清单、两阶段偏好学习器估计用户偏好、CP-SAT优化器选择最优子集，解决了航空出行打包中个性化与约束兼顾的难题，在实际应用中使清单完成率翻倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有打包助手要么是模板驱动、缺乏个性化的，要么是推荐驱动但未考虑安全规则和容量约束，用户仍需手动处理违规问题。亟需一种既能感知个人偏好和上下文，又能满足安全规则、物品依赖关系和行李限制的智能打包方法。

Method: 提出三阶段推理引导学习框架：(1)符号引擎：生成合规的种子清单，包含显式的依赖结构；(2)两阶段偏好学习器：从用户的添加和移除操作中估计包含效用和优先级效用，同时减轻生存偏差；(3)CP-SAT优化器：选择紧凑且合规的物品子集。该架构实现了约束个性化的一般模式，适用于硬可行性约束与稀疏偏好信号并存的应用场景。

Result: 在604个标注旅行场景（包含29K个包含标签和343K个成对比较）上，符号引擎达到99.7%召回率和0.96准则有效性（前沿LLM为0.78-0.81）；梯度提升树和LambdaMART分别达到0.943 AUC-ROC和0.923 NDCG@5；CP-SAT达到100%约束满足（贪心选择28%，随机选择10%）；在iOS旅行应用FlyEnJoy中部署后，清单完成率翻倍，编辑和完成时间均减少。

Conclusion: 该推理引导学习框架能够有效融合符号推理和数据驱动方法，在满足严格约束的同时实现个性化打包推荐，并且在真实应用中得到验证，显著提升了用户体验和效率。

Original Abstract: Packing for air travel is recurring and error-prone: the checklist must be personal and context-aware, yet feasible under safety rules, item dependencies, and luggage limits. Existing packing assistants are template-driven and generic, or recommendation-driven but unconstrained, leaving users to manually patch regulatory and capacity violations. We propose a reasoning-guided learning framework with three stages: (1) a symbolic engine that generates a regulation-aware seed checklist with explicit dependency structure, (2) a two-stage preference learner that estimates inclusion and priority utilities from user add and remove actions while mitigating survivorship bias, and (3) a CP-SAT optimizer that selects a compact, compliant subset. The architecture instantiates a general pattern for constrained personalization, applicable wherever hard feasibility coexists with sparse preference signals. On 604 labeled trip scenarios, comprising 29K inclusion labels and 343K pairwise comparisons, the symbolic engine attains 99.7% recall and 0.96 rubric validity, compared with 0.78 to 0.81 for frontier LLMs. Gradient-boosted trees and LambdaMART reach an AUC-ROC of 0.943 and an NDCG@5 of 0.923. CP-SAT attains 100% constraint satisfaction, compared with 28% for greedy selection and 10% for random selection. Deployment in FlyEnJoy, a production iOS travel app, doubled checklist completions and reduced editing and completion time.

中文摘要翻译: 航空旅行的打包是一项反复出现且容易出错的任务：清单必须是个性化且上下文感知的，同时必须在安全规则、物品依赖关系和行李限制下可行。现有的打包助手要么是模板驱动且通用的，要么是推荐驱动但无约束的，用户需要手动修补违反规则和容量的问题。我们提出了一种推理引导的学习框架，包含三个阶段：(1)一个符号引擎，生成具有显式依赖结构的、符合规则的种子清单；(2)一个两阶段偏好学习器，从用户的添加和移除操作中估计包含效用和优先级效用，同时减轻生存偏差；(3)一个CP-SAT优化器，选择紧凑且合规的子集。该架构实现了约束个性化的一般模式，适用于硬可行性约束与稀疏偏好信号并存的应用场景。在604个标注旅行场景上（包含29K个包含标签和343K个成对比较），符号引擎达到了99.7%的召回率和0.96的准则有效性，而前沿大语言模型为0.78至0.81。梯度提升树和LambdaMART达到了0.943的AUC-ROC和0.923的NDCG@5。CP-SAT实现了100%的约束满足，而贪心选择为28%，随机选择为10%。在FlyEnJoy（一款生产级iOS旅行应用）中部署后，清单完成率翻倍，编辑和完成时间均有所减少。

</details>


### [104] [Information-Directed Sampling for Causal Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15577)
*Muhammad Qasim Elahi,Murat Kocaoglu,Mahsa Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在不可操控变量的上下文因果Bandit问题，提出了因果汤普森采样和因果信息导向采样的变体，并证明了其亚线性贝叶斯遗憾界，实验表明所提方法优于因果和非因果基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在许多实际应用中，某些变量虽影响奖励且携带因果系统信息，但无法被直接操控。现有因果Bandit方法未充分利用这类不可操控变量，因此需要开发能有效利用不可操控变量共享信息的方法。

Method: 采用贝叶斯公式，以观测分布的条件概率表作为未知参数，在已知无潜在混杂的因果图下，提出了因果汤普森采样和因果信息导向采样的变体。通过共享因果机制，使一种干预下收集的观测数据能更新其他干预的奖励估计。

Result: 对因果汤普森采样建立了熵相关的亚线性贝叶斯遗憾界；对因果信息导向采样推导了熵相关的遗憾界，显式量化了蒙特卡洛近似引入的额外误差，当近似量精确可求时恢复标准亚线性率；提供了蒙特卡洛估计的高概率置信界；合成因果Bandit实验表明所提方法优于因果和非因果基线。

Conclusion: 本文提出的因果Bandit方法能有效利用不可操控变量在不同干预之间共享信息，提升奖励识别的效率，理论上证明了亚线性遗憾界，实验验证了有效性。

Original Abstract: Causal bandits exploit structural relationships among variables to share information across interventions and accelerate the identification of high-reward decisions. In many applications, however, some variables cannot be directly manipulated, even though they influence the reward and provide useful information about the underlying causal system. We study contextual causal bandits with non-manipulable variables, where context variables are observed before action selection and additional variables are observed after each intervention. Assuming a known causal graph without latent confounding, we adopt a Bayesian formulation in which the conditional probability tables of the observational distribution constitute the unknown parameter. This representation allows observations collected under one intervention to update reward estimates for other interventions through their shared causal mechanisms. We develop causal variants of Thompson Sampling and Information-Directed Sampling (IDS) for this setting. For Thompson Sampling, we establish an entropy-dependent sublinear Bayesian regret bound. For IDS, we derive an entropy-dependent regret bound that explicitly quantifies the additional error introduced by Monte Carlo approximation of the expected regret and information gain; when these quantities are available exactly, the bound recovers the standard sublinear IDS rate. We further provide high-probability confidence bounds for the Monte Carlo estimates used by the algorithm. Experiments on several synthetic causal bandit tasks show that the proposed methods outperform causal and non-causal baselines by more effectively exploiting information shared across interventions.

中文摘要翻译: 因果Bandit利用变量之间的结构关系，在干预之间共享信息，从而加速高奖励决策的识别。然而，在许多应用中，某些变量即使会影响奖励并携带关于底层因果系统的有用信息，也无法被直接操控。本文研究存在不可操控变量的上下文因果Bandit问题，其中上下文变量在动作选择前被观测到，而额外的变量在每次干预后被观测到。假设已知无潜在混杂的因果图，我们采用贝叶斯公式，将观测分布的条件概率表作为未知参数。这种表示使得在一种干预下收集的观测数据，能够通过共享的因果机制更新其他干预的奖励估计。我们针对该场景提出了因果汤普森采样和因果信息导向采样的变体。对于因果汤普森采样，我们建立了一个熵相关的亚线性贝叶斯遗憾界。对于因果信息导向采样，我们推导了一个熵相关的遗憾界，显式量化了由蒙特卡洛近似期望遗憾和信息增益所引入的额外误差；当这些量能够精确计算时，该界恢复为标准的信息导向采样亚线性率。我们还为算法使用的蒙特卡洛估计提供了高概率置信界。在多个合成因果Bandit任务上的实验表明，所提方法通过更有效地利用跨干预共享的信息，优于因果与非因果基线。

</details>


### [105] [Rethinking Transfer in Continual Learning: A Replay-Based Realisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15587)
*Yang Meng,Zhenya Liu,Zhuokai Zhao,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迁移选择性回放（TSR）方法，在持续学习中通过选择有益于当前任务的历史数据来回放，在保持稳定性的同时提升前向迁移，并论证了迁移应作为持续学习的一阶目标来理解与设计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有持续学习方法几乎都聚焦于防止灾难性遗忘，而忽视了前向迁移（过去帮助未来）。作者认为在设计迁移机制之前，首先应明确迁移在何种条件下才可能发生，从而将迁移视为持续学习的一阶目标。

Method: 提出Transfer-Selective Replay (TSR)：基于三个可测条件（目标任务有改进空间、可迁移信息在持续优化中存活、回放来自兼容的先前任务），利用零训练任务签名选择对当前任务有益的回放数据，而非无差别回放；同时通过蒸馏保持先前任务的稳定性。

Result: 在标准持续学习协议的低预算设定下，TSR在异构和同构任务流中均一致地提升了前向迁移，同时保持稳定性，优于现有的回放基线方法。

Conclusion: 持续学习应当将前向迁移作为与防止遗忘同等重要的一阶目标来对待，在工程实现之前先理解迁移发生的条件，TSR为此提供了有效的框架和方法。

Original Abstract: Continual learning studies how deployed language models can continually acquire new tasks without expensive retraining from scratch. Existing methods, whether rehearsal-based (replaying stored past data) or rehearsal-free (regularising or isolating parameters), overwhelmingly target one objective: preventing catastrophic forgetting. Forward transfer, the past helping the future, has meanwhile been pursued almost exclusively through parameter reuse, with no explicit account of when transfer should be expected at all. We begin one step earlier: before designing a transfer mechanism, we ask when transfer should exist at all. We answer with a framework of three measurable conditions: the target task must leave room for improvement beyond its own limited supervision, transferable information must survive continued optimisation, and replay must come from compatible previous tasks. We instantiate this view as Transfer-Selective Replay (TSR), which selects replay data predicted to benefit the incoming task rather than replaying past examples indiscriminately. Selection is guided by a zero-training task signature, while distillation preserves stability on previous tasks. Under the standard continual learning protocol in the low-budget regime, TSR consistently improves forward transfer while maintaining stability, outperforming existing replay baselines across heterogeneous and homogeneous task streams. More broadly, the results argue for treating transfer as a first-class objective of continual learning, to be understood before it is engineered.

中文摘要翻译: 持续学习研究的是已部署的语言模型如何在不进行昂贵重新训练的情况下持续获取新任务。现有的方法，无论是基于回放（存储过去数据并重放）还是无回放（正则化或隔离参数），绝大多数都只针对一个目标：防止灾难性遗忘。与此同时，前向迁移——过去帮助未来——几乎完全通过参数复用来实现，且没有明确解释迁移应该在何时发生。我们提前一步：在设计迁移机制之前，首先追问迁移究竟在何时存在。我们用一个包含三个可测条件的框架来回答：目标任务必须在其有限的监督之外留有改进空间，可迁移的信息必须在持续优化中存活，回放必须来自兼容的先前任务。我们将这一观点实例化为迁移选择性回放（TSR），该方法选择预测对当前任务有益的回放数据，而不是无差别地回放过去的样本。选择过程由一个零训练的任务签名引导，而蒸馏则保持先前任务的稳定性。在标准持续学习协议的低预算设定下，TSR在保持稳定性的同时一致地提升了前向迁移，在异构和同构任务流上均优于现有的回放基线。更广泛地说，实验结果论证了应将迁移视为持续学习的一阶目标，在工程实现之前先理解其本质。

</details>


### [106] [Field-Aware RankMixer with Dual-Stream Bilinear Fusion for the Tencent UNI-REC Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15590)
*Yufeng Zhang,Zhengqi Xu,Jiajun Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FA-RankMixer模型，通过双流双线性融合（深度RankMixer流+浅层MLP流）联合建模多域用户行为序列与多字段特征，用于目标广告pCVR预测，在KDD Cup 2026腾讯UNIREC挑战赛中获得第九名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法难以有效联合建模多域用户行为序列与非序列多字段特征，且长行为序列中近期与早期兴趣的区分建模不足，需设计更优的跨域特征交互与融合机制以提升pCVR预测精度。

Method: 1) 目标感知DIN模块从多行为域提取用户兴趣，并对最长行为序列分别建模近期与早期兴趣；2) 基于特征字段和行为域构造语义Token，使用RankMixer块进行跨Token交互；3) 浅层MLP流与深度RankMixer流构成双流结构，通过分组双线性模块融合两流表示。

Result: 在KDD Cup 2026腾讯UNIREC挑战赛官方榜单上排名第九。

Conclusion: FA-RankMixer通过双流架构与分组双线性融合有效提升了多域pCVR预测性能，验证了所提方法在工业级推荐场景下的有效性。

Original Abstract: This paper presents our solution to the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UNIREC Challenge. The task requires joint modeling of multi-domain user behavior sequences and non-sequential multi-field features for target-ad pCVR prediction. We develop a Field-Aware RankMixer (FA-RankMixer) with dual-stream bilinear fusion. The model first applies target-aware DIN modules to extract user interests from multiple behavior domains. It also models recent and earlier interests separately for the longest behavior sequence. The model then forms semantic tokens based on feature fields and behavior domains and uses RankMixer blocks for cross-token interaction. A shallow MLP stream complements the deep RankMixer stream, and a group-wise bilinear module fuses their representations. Our final solution ranks ninth on the official leaderboard. Our code is available at https://github.com/PixelCookie-zyf/TAAC-2026-SeRankMixer.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了我们在KDD Cup 2026腾讯UNIREC挑战赛中的解决方案。该任务要求对多域用户行为序列与非序列多字段特征进行联合建模，以实现目标广告的pCVR（转化率后验预测）预估。我们开发了字段感知的RankMixer（FA-RankMixer）模型，并采用双流双线性融合策略。该模型首先应用目标感知的DIN模块从多个行为域中提取用户兴趣，同时对最长行为序列分别建模近期与早期兴趣。然后，模型基于特征字段和行为域构建语义Token，并利用RankMixer块进行跨Token交互。浅层MLP流作为深度RankMixer流的补充，并通过分组双线性模块融合两流的表征。我们的最终方案在官方排行榜上位列第九。代码已开源：https://github.com/PixelCookie-zyf/TAAC-2026-SeRankMixer。

</details>


### [107] [Do Generative Models Keep Time? A Time-Aware Evaluation of Synthetic Sequential Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15606)
*Kiwan Kwon,Kangmin Kim,Hojin Lee,Yeseong Jung,Hyeongwoo Kong,Vamsi K. Potluru,Saerom Park,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据属性分类的时间保真度评估协议，用于检测合成时序表格数据在时间维度上的失败模式（如时间戳倒流、重复、实体轨迹异常），并通过实验证明传统静态分布评估与时间评估结果存在显著差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有合成时序表格数据的评估方法将所有记录池化为静态分布，无法检测时间戳倒流/重复、实体路径异常等时间维度上的失败模式，因此需要一种能够衡量时间保真度的评估协议。

Method: 首先根据四个属性（时间表示方式、观测是否等间隔采样、轨迹是否相互依赖、模式如何链接实体及其历史）对数据集进行分类，确定适用的评估维度；然后测量时间戳有效性、对齐时间点的横截面结构、实体内部动态以及时变关系结构；最后在轨迹层面而非孤立行层面重新评估效用与隐私。

Result: 在8个生成模型、13个数据集（覆盖6个领域）上的实验表明，传统评估与时间评估的排名存在显著不一致，且失败模式与模型架构相关而非随机出现。

Conclusion: 时间保真度必须在时间轴本身上直接测量，而不能从池化的记录分布中推断得出。

Original Abstract: Synthetic sequential tabular data are increasingly used for privacy-preserving data sharing, yet a generator can reproduce every marginal and every foreign-key relationship while emitting timestamps that run backwards or repeat, and while sending entities along paths that no real entity followed. Conventional tabular evaluation, which pools records into static distributions, is blind to such failures. We present a taxonomy-guided evaluation protocol for temporal fidelity, in which the applicable measurements are determined by the data rather than fixed in advance. Each dataset is first characterized along four properties: how time is represented, whether observations are regularly sampled, whether trajectories are mutually dependent, and how the schema links entities to their histories. These properties determine which evaluation dimensions are meaningful. The protocol then measures timestamp validity, cross-sectional structure at aligned time points, within-entity dynamics, and time-varying relational structure, and recasts utility and privacy evaluation over trajectories rather than isolated rows. We apply the protocol to eight generative models across thirteen datasets spanning six domains. Rankings under conventional evaluation disagree substantially with those obtained under temporal evaluation, and the resulting failures are architecture-coherent rather than random. Temporal fidelity must therefore be measured on the time axis itself, rather than inferred from pooled record distributions.

中文摘要翻译: 合成序列表格数据越来越多地被用于隐私保护的数据共享，然而生成器在重现每一个边缘分布和每一个外键关系的同时，可能生成时间戳倒流或重复的时间序列，并让实体沿着真实实体从未走过的路径移动。传统的表格数据评估将记录池化为静态分布，对这些失败模式视而不见。本文提出了一种基于分类学指导的时间保真度评估协议，其中适用的测量指标由数据本身决定，而非预先固定。每个数据集首先沿着四个属性进行刻画：时间如何表示、观测是否等间隔采样、轨迹是否相互依赖、以及模式如何将实体与其历史记录关联起来。这些属性决定了哪些评估维度是有意义的。该协议随后测量时间戳有效性、对齐时间点上的横截面结构、实体内部动态以及时变关系结构，并在轨迹层面而非孤立行层面重新定义效用与隐私评估。我们在涵盖六个领域的十三个数据集上，将该协议应用于八个生成模型。传统评估下的排名与时间评估下的排名存在显著差异，且产生的失败模式具有架构一致性而非随机性。因此，时间保真度必须在时间轴本身上直接测量，而不能从池化的记录分布中推断得出。

</details>


### [108] [ASK-NN: An Asymmetric Nearest-Neighbor Test that detects Distribution Drifts in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15607)
*Sergey Zakharov,Rodion Oblovatny,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种非对称双样本检验统计量ASK-NN，基于有向k近邻图，用于检测LLM生成文本中的幻觉和人工痕迹，性能优于现有基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LLM生成的幻觉和人工文本表现为提示与响应隐藏状态分布之间的偏差，但提示（参考样本）和响应（查询样本）长度不对称，传统对称检验方法不适用，因此需要非对称的检验统计量。

Method: 提出ASK-NN统计量，基于有向k近邻图，计算参考样本中最近邻仍为参考样本的点数，并在置换零假设下推导其精确有限样本条件均值和方差，同时建立渐近正态性和相合性。

Result: 在合成基准、人工文本检测和基于Token级隐藏状态的LLM幻觉检测任务上，ASK-NN与核方法和基于图的基线方法具有竞争力，且计算高效、易于实现。

Conclusion: ASK-NN是一种有效、计算高效且易于实现的非对称双样本检验方法，适用于LLM幻觉检测等分布偏差检测场景。

Original Abstract: Hallucinations and artificial text in LLM-generated outputs often appear as distributional deviations between prompt and response hidden-state distributions. Since prompts or retrieved contexts typically serve as reference samples and responses as query samples, with major differences in length, these asymmetries motivate the use of change test statistics that treat the two samples differently. We consider an asymmetric two-sample test ASK-NN based on the directed k-nearest-neighbor graph. Our statistic counts reference points whose nearest neighbor in the pooled sample is also a reference point. Under the permutation null, it admits an exact finite-sample conditional mean and variance; we further establish asymptotic normality and consistency under fixed alternatives. ASK-NN is computationally effective and easy to implement. Empirically, it is competitive with kernel and graph-based baselines on synthetic benchmarks, artificial-text detection, and LLM hallucination detection from token-level hidden states.

中文摘要翻译: LLM生成输出中的幻觉和人工文本通常表现为提示与响应隐藏状态分布之间的分布偏差。由于提示或检索到的上下文通常作为参考样本，响应作为查询样本，且两者长度存在显著差异，这种不对称性促使我们采用对两个样本区别对待的变更检验统计量。我们考虑一种基于有向k近邻图的非对称双样本检验ASK-NN。该统计量计算参考样本中其最近邻（在混合样本中）仍是参考样本的点数。在置换零假设下，该统计量具有精确的有限样本条件均值和方差；我们进一步建立了其在固定备择假设下的渐近正态性和相合性。ASK-NN计算高效且易于实现。在合成基准测试、人工文本检测以及基于Token级隐藏状态的LLM幻觉检测任务上，ASK-NN与核方法和基于图的基线方法表现相当。

</details>


### [109] [Neural Non-Equilibrium Hamiltonian Monte Carlo for Corrected Boltzmann Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15682)
*Moxian Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经非平衡哈密顿蒙特卡洛（NHMC），一种“先训练后校正”的学习型哈密顿采样器，通过最小化路径空间KL散度训练神经网络生成非平衡路径，再利用非平衡功进行统计校正，实现从非归一化玻尔兹曼密度中采样。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从非归一化玻尔兹曼密度采样需要能全局移动概率质量且保留足够路径概率信息用于统计校正的提议分布，现有方法在路径重叠不足时存在权重退化、接受率低和自相关长等问题。

Method: NHMC从可处理的基分布出发，学习随机哈密顿式路径逼近目标分布。训练后固定提议参数，生成的完整路径和端点配置通过记录的非平衡功进行统计校正。该广义功由正向提议路径与反向参考路径的概率比定义。训练时最小化其均值可降低路径空间KL散度并控制端点不匹配的上界；评估时该量用于路径自归一化重要性采样（path-SNIS）、估计归一化常数/自由能差异，以及路径独立Metropolis-Hastings（path-IMH）的接受率。还提出共享桥往返Metropolis核直接作用于配置并保持玻尔兹曼目标。

Result: 在双势阱和有限体积格点φ⁴目标上，NHMC在路径重叠充分时给出校正估计；重叠不足时，权重退化、接受率低和长自相关暴露了提议失败。此外报告了使用MD先验和学习力路径提议的分子内坐标可行性研究。

Conclusion: NHMC提供了一种学习型非平衡路径采样框架，通过训练-校正范式在路径重叠充分时有效采样，同时路径统计量本身可作为提议质量诊断工具。

Original Abstract: Sampling from an unnormalized Boltzmann density requires proposals that move probability mass globally while retaining enough path-probability information for statistical correction. We introduce Neural Non-Equilibrium Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (NHMC), a train-then-correct learned Hamiltonian sampler. Starting from a tractable base distribution, NHMC learns stochastic Hamiltonian-style paths toward the target. Once training is complete, the learned proposal parameters are fixed; the proposal then generates complete paths and endpoint configurations, which are statistically corrected using the recorded non-equilibrium work. This dimensionless generalized work is determined by the probability ratio between the forward proposal path and a reverse reference path. During training, minimizing its mean reduces a path-space KL divergence and controls an upper bound on endpoint mismatch. During evaluation, the same quantity defines weights for self-normalized importance sampling on paths (path-SNIS), estimates normalizing constants or free-energy differences, and gives the acceptance ratio for path-space independent Metropolis-Hastings (path-IMH). The same forward-reverse laws also define a shared-bridge round-trip Metropolis kernel that acts directly on configurations and preserves the Boltzmann target. On double-well and finite-volume lattice $φ^4$ targets, the NHMC construction gives corrected estimates when path overlap is sufficient; when overlap is poor, weight degeneracy, low acceptance, and long autocorrelation expose proposal failure. We additionally report a molecular internal-coordinate feasibility study using an MD prior and learned-force path proposal.

中文摘要翻译: 从非归一化玻尔兹曼密度中采样需要能全局移动概率质量并保留足够路径概率信息用于统计校正的提议分布。我们提出了神经非平衡哈密顿蒙特卡洛（NHMC），一种先训练后校正的学习型哈密顿采样器。从可处理的基分布出发，NHMC学习随机哈密顿式路径向目标分布逼近。训练完成后，学习得到的提议参数被固定；提议分布随后生成完整路径和端点构型，并通过记录的非平衡功进行统计校正。这一无量纲的广义功由正向提议路径与反向参考路径之间的概率比决定。在训练阶段，最小化其均值可降低路径空间的KL散度，并控制端点不匹配的上界。在评估阶段，该量定义了路径自归一化重要性采样（path-SNIS）的权重、估计归一化常数或自由能差异，并为路径空间独立Metropolis-Hastings（path-IMH）提供接受率。相同的正向-反向法则还定义了一个共享桥往返Metropolis核，该核直接作用于构型并保持玻尔兹曼目标。在双势阱和有限体积格点φ⁴目标上，NHMC框架在路径重叠充分时给出校正估计；当重叠较差时，权重退化、低接受率和长自相关暴露了提议分布的失效。此外，我们还报告了一项使用分子动力学先验和学习力路径提议的分子内坐标可行性研究。

</details>


### [110] [Toward Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Models: A Topology-Aware Multimodal Alignment Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15687)
*Xunkai Li,Guohao Fu,Yuming Ai,Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedGAMMA是一种联邦多模态图基础模型学习方法，通过两阶段语义-结构对齐（预训练+提示微调）解决隐私受限的多模态属性图跨平台学习问题，在12个数据集上超越基线，最高提升12.96%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实中的多模态属性图（MAGs）分散在不同的隐私受限平台上，现有方法要么只处理多模态图学习，要么只处理联邦学习，缺乏同时解决两个挑战的统一框架。

Method: 提出FedGAMMA，包含两阶段：1) 联邦预训练阶段：共享-私有语义增强器通过最优传输解耦跨模态共性与模态特异性信息；拓扑感知图融合模块通过语义残差图和双位置编码解耦语义与结构视图；双通道亲和感知聚合机制从特征和图质心估计客户端相似性而不暴露原始数据。2) 提示微调阶段：轻量级图感知提示、共享提示池与可控探索、通道级提示同步。

Result: 在12个多模态图数据集上，FedGAMMA在下游任务中持续超越基线，最高提升12.96%；在少样本学习场景下，跨多领域数据集上最多提升5.71%。

Conclusion: FedGAMMA有效解决了联邦设置下多模态图基础学习面临的隐私与异构性挑战，验证了语义-结构对齐两阶段范式的有效性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), whose nodes carry modalities such as images and text alongside topological structure, now pervade applications including social platforms, e-commerce, and biomedical networks, offering richer semantic signals than single-modality graphs. In practice, such graphs are fragmented across privacy-restricted silos owned by different platforms and institutions, so learning a broadly transferable model over them demands collaborative training that never exposes raw data. This places the task at the intersection of multimodal graph learning and federated learning, yet existing methods cover only one side of it. To address the challenges from these two perspectives, we propose FedGAMMA, casting federated multimodal graph foundation learning as a two-stage semantic-structural alignment problem of federated pre-training and prompt-based fine-tuning. During pre-training, a shared-private semantic enhancer disentangles cross-modal commonality from modality-specific information, aligning it through optimal transport, a topology-aware graph fusion module decouples semantic and structural views via semantic residual graphs and dual positional encodings, and a dual-channel affinity-aware aggregation mechanism estimates client similarity from feature and graph centroids without exposing raw data. During fine-tuning, FedGAMMA adapts the pretrained encoder through lightweight graph-aware prompts, a shared prompt pool with controlled exploration, and channel-wise prompt synchronization. Experiments on twelve multimodal graph datasets show FedGAMMA consistently surpassing a broad range of baselines across downstream tasks, with gains of up to 12.96%. FedGAMMA further outperforms competitive baselines accross multi-domain datasets on multiple tasks with up to 5.71% under few-shot learning scenario.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态属性图（MAGs）的节点除拓扑结构外还携带图像和文本等模态信息，现已广泛应用于社交平台、电子商务和生物医学网络，比单模态图提供更丰富的语义信号。在实际应用中，此类图分散在不同平台和机构拥有的隐私受限数据孤岛中，因此学习一个广泛可迁移的模型需要在不暴露原始数据的情况下进行协作训练。这使得该任务处于多模态图学习与联邦学习的交叉点，然而现有方法仅覆盖了其中一方面。为了应对这两个视角带来的挑战，我们提出了FedGAMMA，将联邦多模态图基础学习建模为联邦预训练和基于提示的微调的两阶段语义-结构对齐问题。在预训练阶段，共享-私有语义增强器通过最优传输解耦跨模态共性与模态特异性信息；拓扑感知图融合模块通过语义残差图和双位置编码解耦语义与结构视图；双通道亲和感知聚合机制在不暴露原始数据的情况下，从特征和图质心估计客户端相似性。在微调阶段，FedGAMMA通过轻量级图感知提示、带有可控探索的共享提示池以及通道级提示同步来适配预训练编码器。在十二个多模态图数据集上的实验表明，FedGAMMA在下游任务中持续超越广泛的基线方法，最高提升12.96%。在少样本学习场景下，FedGAMMA在跨多领域数据集的多个任务上进一步优于竞争基线，最高提升5.71%。

</details>


### [111] [A Benchmark for Electrical Load Forecasting Across Grid Levels: Time-Series Transformers Outperform Established Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15705)
*Matthias Hertel,Sebastian Pütz,Jonathan Kolar,Benjamin Schäfer,Ralf Mikut,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多个电网层级（从输电系统运营商到终端用户）的短期负荷预测方法进行了全面基准测试，发现Transformer方法优于传统方法（误差降低6.6-10.7%），但标准Transformer优于改进架构YAformer。此外，时间序列基础模型Chronos-2表现出零样本竞争力但难以处理特殊事件，消融研究强调了长输入上下文、协变量和持续重训练的重要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 智能电网中不同层级（控制区域、低压馈线、终端用户）的精确负荷预测至关重要，但现有研究缺乏跨层级的系统性基准比较，且对Transformer架构设计选择的影响、基础模型在负荷预测中的适用性等关键问题缺乏深入分析。

Method: 构建了包含三个数据集（TSO控制区域、低压电网馈线、终端用户）的跨层级负荷预测基准；评估了十种短期负荷预测方法；引入了YAformer——一种灵活集成了先前工作中改进方案的Transformer架构并通过超参数优化；评估了基于Transformer的时间序列基础模型Chronos-2；进行了详细的消融研究。

Result: Transformer方法一致优于传统方法，误差降低6.6-10.7%；标准Transformer性能优于YAformer，表明架构改进对准确负荷预测并非必需；Chronos-2在两个数据集上表现竞争性零样本性能，但在TSO数据上无法准确捕捉特殊事件；消融研究显示长输入上下文、协变量和持续重训练的重要性。

Conclusion: Transformer是短期负荷预测的最佳选择，但复杂的架构修改可能不必要；基础模型有潜力但需针对性改进以处理特殊事件；长输入上下文、协变量和持续重训练是提升预测性能的关键因素。

Original Abstract: Accurate load forecasting at multiple grid levels is essential for future smart grids, ranging from aggregated control area forecasts for balancing supply and demand to forecasts of individual end-consumer loads for demand-side management and energy management systems. We present a comprehensive benchmark for load forecasting across grid levels, comprising three datasets that represent a transmission system operator control area, low-voltage grid feeders, and individual end consumers. We evaluate ten methods for short-term load forecasting and find that Transformer-based approaches consistently outperform established methods, reducing forecast error by 6.6-10.7 %. To analyze the impact of architectural design, we introduce YAformer, a flexible Transformer architecture that integrates modifications from prior work and is optimized via hyperparameter optimization. However, the standard Transformer achieves superior performance, suggesting that these architectural modifications are not required for accurate load forecasting. We further evaluate the Transformer-based time-series foundation model Chronos-2, which demonstrates competitive zero-shot performance on two datasets but fails to accurately capture special events in the TSO data. Detailed analyses reveal model-specific strengths and weaknesses, and ablation studies highlight the importance of long input contexts, covariates and continuous retraining - aspects that are often overlooked in the time-series forecasting literature.

中文摘要翻译: 精确的负荷预测对于未来智能电网至关重要，涵盖从用于平衡供需的聚合控制区域预测到用于需求侧管理和能源管理系统的单个终端用户负荷预测。本文提出了一个跨电网层级的负荷预测综合基准，包括三个分别代表输电系统运营商控制区域、低压电网馈线和单个终端用户的数据集。我们评估了十种短期负荷预测方法，发现基于Transformer的方法一致优于已有方法，将预测误差降低了6.6-10.7%。为分析架构设计的影响，我们引入了YAformer——一种灵活的Transformer架构，它整合了先前工作中的改进并通过超参数优化进行了优化。然而，标准Transformer实现了更优的性能，表明这些架构修改对于准确的负荷预测并非必需。我们进一步评估了基于Transformer的时间序列基础模型Chronos-2，该模型在两个数据集上表现出竞争性的零样本性能，但未能准确捕捉TSO数据中的特殊事件。详细分析揭示了模型特定的优劣势，消融研究强调了长输入上下文、协变量和持续重训练的重要性——这些方面在时间序列预测文献中常被忽视。

</details>


### [112] [CardioMeta: Calibrated Multi-Task Prediction of Diabetes, Hypertension, and Cardiovascular Disease Across Population and EHR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15721)
*S M Asif Hossain,Ruksat Khan Shayoni,M. F. Mridha,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CardioMeta，一个用于联合预测糖尿病、高血压和心血管疾病的校准多任务框架。该框架在NHANES和MIMIC-IV两个数据集上进行了验证，重点解决了标签泄露、校准、时间鲁棒性、外部可迁移性和子组可靠性等问题。结果表明，多任务心肺代谢建模的主要价值不在于提升准确率，而在于可复现的泄露控制、校准概率以及跨异构医疗数据源的透明可靠性报告。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 心肺代谢疾病（糖尿病、高血压、心血管疾病）经常共病且共享代谢、血管、人口学和行为决定因素，但现有机器学习研究通常仅关注单一数据集上的区分性能，忽视了标签泄露、校准、时间鲁棒性、外部可迁移性和子组可靠性等关键问题。

Method: CardioMeta框架包含三个核心组件：1）共享的心肺代谢编码器（shared cardiometabolic encoder）；2）疾病特定的门控头部（disease-specific gated heads）；3）事后概率校准（post-hoc probability calibration）。为减少循环标签重构，主要分析排除了对应预测头中的疾病定义变量，仅在全临床特征设置中作为敏感性分析保留。

Result: 在减少泄露的时间验证设置中，模型实现了macro-AUROC为0.839、macro-AUPRC为0.536、macro-F1为0.614、期望校准误差（ECE）为0.024，相较于强梯度提升和神经表格基线模型有适度但一致的改进。在MIMIC-IV上的外部评估显示，域偏移下性能明显下降，有限微调可部分恢复性能。

Conclusion: 多任务心肺代谢建模的主要价值不在于提升准确率，而在于可复现的泄露控制、校准概率以及跨异构医疗数据源的透明可靠性报告。

Original Abstract: Cardiometabolic diseases remain among the most persistent drivers of preventable morbidity because diabetes, hypertension, and cardiovascular disease frequently co-occur and share metabolic, vascular, demographic, and behavioral determinants. Existing machine learning studies for chronic disease prediction often emphasize discrimination on a single dataset, while underreporting label leakage, calibration, temporal robustness, external transportability, and subgroup reliability. This paper presents CardioMeta, a calibrated multi-task framework for joint prediction of diabetes, hypertension, and cardiovascular disease across population survey and electronic health record (EHR) data. The study uses NHANES for population-level model development and temporal validation, and MIMIC-IV for EHR-domain evaluation under substantial distribution shift. To reduce circular label reconstruction, the primary analysis excludes disease-defining variables from the corresponding prediction heads, while a full-clinical feature setting is retained only as sensitivity analysis. CardioMeta combines a shared cardiometabolic encoder with disease-specific gated heads and post-hoc probability calibration. In the leakage-reduced temporal validation setting, the model achieved a macro-AUROC of 0.839, macro-AUPRC of 0.536, macro-F1 of 0.614, and expected calibration error of 0.024, with modest but consistent improvements over strong gradient-boosting and neural tabular baselines. External evaluation on MIMIC-IV showed clear degradation under domain shift, while limited fine-tuning partially recovered performance. The findings indicate that the principal value of multi-task cardiometabolic modeling lies not in inflated accuracy, but in reproducible leakage control, calibrated probabilities, and transparent reliability reporting across heterogeneous healthcare data sources.

中文摘要翻译: 心肺代谢疾病仍然是可预防发病的主要驱动因素之一，因为糖尿病、高血压和心血管疾病经常共病，并且共享代谢、血管、人口学和行为决定因素。现有的慢性病预测机器学习研究通常强调在单一数据集上的区分性能，而较少报告标签泄露、校准、时间鲁棒性、外部可迁移性和子组可靠性。本文提出了CardioMeta，一个用于在人口调查和电子健康记录数据上联合预测糖尿病、高血压和心血管疾病的校准多任务框架。该研究使用NHANES进行人群级模型开发和时序验证，并在显著的分布偏移下使用MIMIC-IV进行电子健康记录域评估。为减少循环标签重构，主要分析排除了对应预测头中的疾病定义变量，而全临床特征设置仅作为敏感性分析保留。CardioMeta结合了共享的心肺代谢编码器、疾病特定的门控头部和事后概率校准。在减少泄露的时间验证设置中，模型实现了宏观AUROC为0.839、宏观AUPRC为0.536、宏观F1为0.614和期望校准误差为0.024，相较于强梯度提升和神经表格基线模型有适度但一致的改进。在MIMIC-IV上的外部评估显示，域偏移下性能明显下降，而有限微调可部分恢复性能。研究结果表明，多任务心肺代谢建模的主要价值不在于虚高的准确率，而在于可复现的泄露控制、校准概率以及跨异构医疗数据源的透明可靠性报告。

</details>


### [113] [Learning Faster without Deeper Networks: A*-Inspired Batch Selection for Efficient CNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15745)
*Anxhelo Shehu,Enes Stastoli,Arben Cela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出A*-BS（A*启发式批选择策略），将小批量调度建模为启发式搜索问题，通过损失难度与复用惩罚的联合评分选择信息量更大的批次，在不修改网络架构或优化算法的情况下提升训练效率与模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统随机打乱小批量的训练方式存在收敛速度慢和学习信号衰减两个问题（大量样本在训练早期被快速归类为简单样本），需要更高效的批次选择策略来改善梯度更新质量。

Method: 将每个批次视为搜索空间中的节点，采用类似A*的评分函数（结合基于损失的难度度量与复用惩罚）对批次进行排序，优先选择信息量高且多样性好的批次，实现轻量级、模型无关的批调度策略。

Result: 在MedMNIST-v2基准的12个2D分类任务上，使用约2.25×10^5参数的轻量模型搭配A*-BS，在6个任务上准确率和AUC均超过ResNet-18和ResNet-50，相对增益最高达15%；在相同架构和超参数下，A*-BS在所有12个任务上优于随机批次打乱；轻量CNN+A*-BS在相同硬件上比ResNet-18/50训练速度显著更快。

Conclusion: 智能批次排序可以在一定程度上弥补架构复杂度的降低，为深层模型提供一种计算高效的替代方案，即使在面对更深、更复杂的架构时仍表现出可靠的性能。

Original Abstract: Common practice when training Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) is to use randomly shuffled mini-batches. This creates two limitations: slower convergence, and a diminishing learning signal, since many samples are quickly classified as easy during training. We address these inefficiencies with A*-Inspired Batch Selection (A*-BS), a lightweight, model-agnostic strategy that formulates mini-batch scheduling as a heuristic search problem. Each batch is treated as a node in a search space and ranked using an A*-like score combining a loss-based difficulty measure with a reuse penalty. This encourages informative gradient updates and batch diversity throughout training, without modifying network architectures or optimization algorithms, so it integrates seamlessly into existing pipelines. We evaluate A*-BS on the twelve 2D classification tasks of the MedMNIST-v2 benchmark, using a deliberately simple architecture of approximately 2.25x10^5 parameters, compared against the ResNet-18 and ResNet-50 baselines reported by the benchmark. On half of these tasks, the lightweight model with A*-BS reaches higher accuracy and AUC than both ResNet baselines, with relative gains of up to 15%. An ablation under identical architecture and hyperparameters shows A*-BS outperforms random batch shuffling on all twelve tasks. Wall-clock measurements further show the lightweight CNN with A*-BS trains substantially faster than ResNet-18 and ResNet-50 on identical hardware. These results indicate that intelligent batch ordering can partially compensate for reduced architectural complexity, offering a computationally efficient alternative to deeper models, with reliability reinforced by strong performance even against deeper, more sophisticated architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 训练卷积神经网络（CNN）时的常见做法是使用随机打乱的小批量数据。这带来了两个限制：收敛速度较慢，以及学习信号不断减弱——因为许多样本在训练早期就被快速归类为简单样本。我们通过A*启发式批选择（A*-BS）来解决这些低效问题，这是一种轻量级、与模型无关的策略，将小批量调度建模为一个启发式搜索问题。每个批次被视为搜索空间中的一个节点，并使用类似A*的评分进行排序，该评分结合了基于损失的难度度量与复用惩罚。这在整个训练过程中促进了信息丰富的梯度更新和批次多样性，而无需修改网络架构或优化算法，因此可以无缝集成到现有流程中。我们在MedMNIST-v2基准的十二个2D分类任务上评估了A*-BS，使用了约2.25×10^5参数的故意简化的架构，并与该基准报告中的ResNet-18和ResNet-50基线进行比较。在其中一半的任务上，采用A*-BS的轻量模型在准确率和AUC上均超过了两个ResNet基线，相对增益高达15%。在相同架构和超参数下的消融实验表明，A*-BS在所有十二个任务上的表现均优于随机批次打乱。时钟时间的测量进一步表明，采用A*-BS的轻量CNN在相同硬件上的训练速度显著快于ResNet-18和ResNet-50。这些结果表明，智能批次排序可以在一定程度上弥补架构复杂度的降低，为深层模型提供一种计算高效的替代方案，即使在面对更深、更复杂的架构时，其性能的稳健性也进一步强化了该方法的可靠性。

</details>


### [114] [Trainable Spline Representations for Physics-Informed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15751)
*Giovanni Canali,Nicola Demo,Gianluigi Rozza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-Splines，一种基于B样条的结构化架构替代神经网络用于物理信息学习，在保持残差训练范式的同时提供紧支撑、显式光滑控制、解析导数及几何可解释性，实验表明其作为神经网络架构的竞争性替代方案，尤其在结构化表示和参数效率方面表现突出。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）使用神经网络作为解的表达方式，存在参数不可解释、缺乏局部支撑、光滑性难以显式控制、边界条件施加复杂等问题。本文旨在设计一种替代架构，保留残差训练范式的同时克服上述不足。

Method: 提出PI-Splines架构，用张量积B样条展开替代神经网络来参数化微分方程的未知场，其中控制系数为可训练参数。该方法保留了PINN的残差训练范式，同时利用B样条的紧支撑性实现局部更新，通过样条阶数显式控制光滑性，提供解析导数，并可通过固定边界控制系数强施加边界条件。

Result: 在多个难度递增的基准问题上，PI-Splines与标准物理信息框架在相同控制方程、配点集、损失项和优化流程下进行公平对比，实验表明PI-Splines提供了有竞争力和稳定性的替代方案，尤其在结构化表示、局部性和参数效率方面表现优越。

Conclusion: PI-Splines作为神经网络架构的可行替代方案，在物理信息学习场景中具有竞争力和稳定性，特别适用于需要结构化表示、局部性和参数高效的应用场景。

Original Abstract: This work introduces Physics-Informed Splines (PI-Splines), a structured spline-based architecture for physics-informed learning. Instead of representing the solution of a differential equation with a neural network, PI-Splines directly parametrize the unknown field through a tensor-product B-spline expansion with trainable control coefficients.
