This formulation preserves the residual-based training paradigm of Physics-Informed Neural Networks while providing compact support, explicit smoothness control, analytical derivatives, and a direct geometric interpretation of the trainable parameters. When compatible with the spline representation, boundary conditions can be imposed strongly by fixing suitable boundary control coefficients. The proposed method is evaluated on several benchmark problems of increasing difficulty and compared with standard physics-informed frameworks under matched governing equations, collocation sets, loss terms, and optimization procedures, so as to isolate the effect of the approximation architecture. Numerical experiments show that PI-Splines provide a competitive and stable alternative to neural physics-informed architectures, particularly in settings where structured representations, locality, and parameter efficiency are desirable.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了物理信息样条（PI-Splines），一种用于物理信息学习的结构化样条架构。该方法不使用神经网络表示微分方程的解，而是通过具有可训练控制系数的张量积B样条展开直接参数化未知场。这一公式保留了物理信息神经网络的残差训练范式，同时提供了紧支撑、显式光滑性控制、解析导数以及可训练参数的直接几何解释。当与样条表示兼容时，可以通过固定合适的边界控制系数来强施加边界条件。该方法在多个难度递增的基准问题上进行了评估，并在匹配的控制方程、配点集、损失项和优化流程下与标准物理信息框架进行了比较，以隔离近似架构的影响。数值实验表明，PI-Splines为神经物理信息架构提供了有竞争力和稳定的替代方案，特别是在需要结构化表示、局部性和参数效率的场景中。

</details>


### [115] [CoG-Guided Weight Correction for Fault-Tolerant Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15753)
*Bahram Parchekani,Samira Nazari,Ali Azarpeyvand,Mohammad Hasan Ahmadilivani,Tara Ghasempouri,Jaan Raik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于重心（CoG）引导的权重校正方法，利用层内权重的空间特征来检测和纠正硬件故障导致的权重错误，无需重训练或修改网络架构，在LSTM和CNN模型上均显著提升了容错能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 安全关键应用中的深度神经网络易受硬件和内存故障影响，导致权重损坏和可靠性下降，需要一种无需重训练或架构修改的轻量级容错方法。

Method: 提出重心（Center of Gravity, CoG）引导的权重校正方法，基于每层内权重的空间特征，利用距离感知校正规则检测和纠正故障权重，无需重训练或架构修改。

Result: 在LSTM网络（StageNet和MTFNet）上，BER为10^{-3}时容错能力分别提升230倍和6.41倍；在CNN网络（ResNet-18和VGG-16）上，容错能力分别提升49.55倍和20.79倍，且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 首次将重心（CoG）概念应用于神经网络权重张量以增强模型可靠性，实验证明该方法在多种网络架构上均能显著提升容错能力。

Original Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) used in safety-critical applications are vulnerable to hardware and memory faults that corrupt network weights and degrade reliability. In this paper, we propose a Center of Gravity (CoG) guided weight correction method that restores faulty weights based on their spatial characteristics within each layer. The proposed approach detects and corrects weight faults using distance-aware correction rules, eliminating the need for retraining or architectural modification. The effectiveness of the proposed method in terms of the capability of tolerating hardware faults has been evaluated through performing fault injection at different Bit Error Rates (BERs).
  Experiments on safety-critical LSTM-based Networks, including StageNet for disease progression tracking and MTFNet for cardiac anomaly detection, demonstrate fault tolerance improvements of up to 230x and 6.41x, respectively, at a BER of 10^{-3}, with negligible accuracy loss. When extended to Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), the method achieves up to 49.55x and 20.79x improvements under comparable fault conditions on ResNet-18 and VGG-16, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply the CoG concept to neural network weight tensors for enhancing model reliability.

中文摘要翻译: 用于安全关键应用的深度神经网络（DNN）易受硬件和内存故障的影响，这些故障会破坏网络权重并降低可靠性。本文提出了一种重心（Center of Gravity, CoG）引导的权重校正方法，该方法基于每层内权重的空间特征来恢复故障权重。所提出的方法利用距离感知校正规则检测并纠正权重故障，无需重训练或架构修改。通过在不同误码率（BER）下进行故障注入实验，评估了所提方法在容忍硬件故障方面的有效性。在安全关键的LSTM网络（包括用于疾病进展追踪的StageNet和用于心脏异常检测的MTFNet）上的实验表明，在BER为10^{-3}时，容错能力分别提升高达230倍和6.41倍，且精度损失可忽略。当扩展到卷积神经网络（CNN）时，在ResNet-18和VGG-16上，该方法在可比故障条件下分别实现了49.55倍和20.79倍的提升。据我们所知，这是首次将重心概念应用于神经网络权重张量以增强模型可靠性的工作。

</details>


### [116] [From Diffusion to Reaction-Diffusion: A Dynamical-Systems View of Oversmoothing in Hypergraph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15773)
*Zhiheng Zhou,Mengyao Zhou,Yancheng Chen,Dengyi Zhao,Xingqin Qi,Guiying Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从动力系统角度研究超图神经网络的过平滑问题，提出超图神经反应-扩散（HNRD）框架，通过引入反应机制抵消扩散导致的表示坍缩，从而支持深层传播并保持高阶表达力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高阶耦合增强了超图神经网络的表达力，但也因强多路特征混合加剧了深层传播中的表示坍缩（过平滑问题）。现有研究缺乏从动力系统视角对超图过平滑问题的系统分析，亟需一种 principled 的深度抗性超图学习框架。

Method: 1) 从动力系统视角研究超图过平滑，定义超图梯度和散度算子，将消息传递解释为关联级扩散过程；2) 证明纯扩散的连续半流指数收缩节点表示的零模无关分量并使Dirichlet能量归零，揭示过平滑是横向能量耗散现象；3) 提出超图神经反应-扩散（HNRD），引入作用于横向分量的反应机制补偿扩散导致的耗散；4) 建立动力学的全局适定性，证明零模无关Dirichlet能量有界远离零；5) 采用前向欧拉离散化得到实用的HNRD层及深度传播稳定性条件。

Result: 在基准和合成异配超图上的实验表明，HNRD一致优于代表性超图基线方法。深度、鲁棒性和效率分析进一步显示，HNRD在深层传播和扰动下能保持稳定性能和非零Dirichlet能量。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计保持高阶表达力且不发生表示坍缩的深层超图架构提供了一种 principled 的动力系统框架。

Original Abstract: Higher-order couplings enhance the expressive power of hypergraph neural networks (HGNNs), but they also intensify representation collapse in deep propagation due to strong multi-way feature mixing. This work investigates hypergraph oversmoothing from a dynamical-systems perspective and develops a reaction--diffusion framework for depth-resistant hypergraph learning. By defining hypergraph gradient and divergence operators, we interpret message passing as an incidence-level diffusion process. The analysis of pure diffusion shows that its continuous semiflow exponentially contracts the null-mode-free component of node representations and drives the Dirichlet energy to zero, revealing hypergraph oversmoothing as an intrinsic transverse-energy dissipation phenomenon. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Hypergraph Neural Reaction--Diffusion (HNRD), which introduces a reaction mechanism acting on the transverse component to compensate diffusion-induced dissipation and stabilize discriminative variations. We establish global well-posedness of the proposed dynamics and prove that the null-mode-free Dirichlet energy remains bounded away from zero. A forward-Euler discretization provides a practical HNRD layer with a stability condition for deep propagation. Experiments on benchmark and synthetic heterophilic hypergraphs demonstrate that HNRD consistently improves over representative hypergraph baselines. Depth, robustness, and efficiency analyses further show that HNRD preserves stable performance and nonzero Dirichlet energy under deep propagation and perturbations. These results provide a principled dynamical framework for designing deep hypergraph architectures that maintain higher-order expressiveness without representation collapse.

中文摘要翻译: 高阶耦合增强了超图神经网络（HGNN）的表达能力，但也因强多路特征混合加剧了深层传播中的表示坍缩。本文从动力系统视角研究超图过平滑问题，并开发了一个深度抗性的超图学习反应-扩散框架。通过定义超图梯度算子和散度算子，我们将消息传递解释为关联级扩散过程。纯扩散分析表明，其连续半流指数收缩节点表示的零模无关分量，并将Dirichlet能量驱动至零，揭示了超图过平滑是一种内在的横向能量耗散现象。受此分析启发，我们提出超图神经反应-扩散（HNRD），引入一个作用于横向分量的反应机制，以补偿扩散引起的耗散并稳定判别性变异。我们建立了所提动力学的全局适定性，并证明零模无关Dirichlet能量有界远离零。前向欧拉离散化提供了一个实用的HNRD层，并附带深度传播的稳定性条件。在基准和合成异配超图上的实验表明，HNRD一致优于代表性超图基线方法。深度、鲁棒性和效率分析进一步表明，HNRD在深层传播和扰动下能保持稳定性能和非零Dirichlet能量。这些结果为设计保持高阶表达力而不发生表示坍缩的深度超图架构提供了一个 principled 的动力系统框架。

</details>


### [117] [Scaling Time Series Classification via XAI-Driven Data Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15774)
*Davide Italo Serramazza,Thach Le Nguyen,Georgiana Ifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: drXAI是一种利用XAI归因方法对时间序列分类进行数据降维的新方法，通过GPU加速分类器生成特征重要性评分，实现80%-90%的数据压缩同时保持分类精度，使资源密集型模型能扩展到原本无法处理的大规模数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前时间序列分类面临可扩展性挑战，最先进的模型（如Transformer）在序列长度上呈现二次复杂度、在通道数上呈线性复杂度，对大规模数据集计算成本过高。尽管可解释AI（XAI）在时间序列领域有显著算法进展，但其在下游任务中提供可测量的性能增益这一潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出drXAI方法：首先使用快速、GPU加速的分类器（Hydra）生成局部归因；然后将局部归因聚合为全局特征重要性分数；最后采用自动肘部截断启发式方法选择最显著特征，无需手动设置阈值。

Result: 在合成数据上，drXAI成功恢复真实特征，而传统基线方法失败；在真实数据上，实现80%-90%的数据压缩同时保持与全量数据训练相当的分类精度；使得ConvTran等资源密集型模型能够扩展到之前因内存限制而无法处理的数据集。

Conclusion: XAI不仅可用于可解释性，还可作为时间序列分析中特征选择和可扩展性的鲁棒工具，有效缓解大规模时间序列分类的计算瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) for time series has seen significant algorithmic growth, but its utility in providing measurable performance gains for downstream tasks remains under-explored. This paper bridges this gap by introducing drXAI, a novel methodology that repurposes XAI attribution methods for effective data reduction in Time Series Classification (TSC). The core challenge in modern TSC is scalability; state-of-the-art models, such as Transformers, exhibit quadratic complexity relative to sequence length and linear complexity relative to the number of channels. This renders them computationally prohibitive for massive datasets. drXAI addresses this by using a fast, GPU-accelerated classifier (Hydra) to generate local attributions. We aggregate these into global feature importance scores and employ an automated elbow-cut heuristic to select the most salient features without requiring manual thresholds.
  We evaluate our approach on both synthetic and real-world univariate and multivariate datasets. On synthetic benchmarks, drXAI successfully recovers ground-truth features where traditional baselines fail. On real-world data, drXAI achieves between 80% and 90% data reduction while maintaining classification accuracy comparable to models trained on the full dataset. Most importantly, we show that drXAI allows resource-intensive models like ConvTran to scale to datasets that were previously inaccessible due to memory constraints. Our results show the benefits of using XAI not just for interpretability, but as a robust tool for feature selection and scalability in time series analysis. All our code and data are openly available.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列的可解释人工智能（XAI）在算法层面取得了显著进展，但其在下游任务中提供可测量性能增益的实用性仍未被充分探索。本文通过引入drXAI——一种将XAI归因方法重新用于时间序列分类（TSC）高效数据降维的新方法——弥合了这一差距。现代TSC的核心挑战是可扩展性；最先进的模型（如Transformer）在序列长度上呈现二次复杂度、在通道数上呈线性复杂度，这使得它们在处理大规模数据集时计算成本过高。drXAI通过使用快速、GPU加速的分类器（Hydra）生成局部归因来解决这一问题。我们将这些局部归因聚合为全局特征重要性分数，并采用自动肘部截断启发式方法选择最显著特征，无需手动设置阈值。我们在合成数据集和真实世界的单变量及多变量数据集上评估了该方法。在合成基准上，drXAI成功恢复了传统基线方法无法恢复的真实特征。在真实数据上，drXAI实现了80%至90%的数据压缩，同时保持了与全量数据训练模型相当的分类精度。最重要的是，我们证明了drXAI使得ConvTran等资源密集型模型能够扩展到之前因内存限制而无法处理的数据集。我们的结果展示了将XAI不仅作为可解释性工具，更作为时间序列分析中特征选择和可扩展性的鲁棒工具的价值。我们的所有代码和数据均已公开。

</details>


### [118] [AquaAugmentor: A Novel Feature Augmentation Algorithm for Water Potability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15775)
*Muntasir Tabasum,Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Tanpia Tasnim,Md. Ekramul Islam,Md Younus Ahamed,Md Asif Bin Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AquaAugmentor的特征增强算法，用于提升机器学习与深度学习模型在低维水质数据集上的饮用水可饮用性分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 准确分类水质对健康、经济发展和可持续性至关重要，但水质数据的复杂性和变异性使得分类面临巨大挑战。现有方法在低维数据集上的预测性能有限，亟需有效的特征增强技术来提升模型表现。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的特征增强算法AquaAugmentor，用于增强低维数据集的预测性能。该算法应用于包含pH值、硬度、固体物、氯胺、硫酸盐等化学属性的水质数据集，并通过测试准确率和AUC分数评估各模型在使用与不使用AquaAugmentor时的分类性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，AquaAugmentor在提升水质可饮用性分类模型的预测性能方面表现出优势与局限性，具体通过测试准确率和AUC分数进行量化对比。

Conclusion: 本研究为提升水质分类预测性能提供了有效技术框架，有助于研究人员、政策制定者和公共卫生官员基于可靠的机器学习预测做出明智决策，并为将机器学习应用于环境质量评估做出了贡献。

Original Abstract: Access to potable water is crucial for health, economic development, and sustainability. However, accurately classifying water quality remains a significant challenge due to the complexity and variability of water source data. This paper addresses the challenge of predicting water potability through machine learning and deep learning algorithms. It introduces a novel feature augmentation algorithm, AquaAugmentor, to enhance the predictive performance of these models for low-dimensional datasets. Utilizing a dataset that includes chemical attributes of water, such as pH, hardness, solids, chloramines, sulfate, and others. This study evaluates the performance of the models with and without AquaAugmentor. Each model applied to classify water as potable or non-potable and its performance is then evaluated and compared based on test accuracy and AUC score. The results highlight the strengths and limitations of our proposed algorithm, providing insights into the most effective techniques for improving the predictive performance of water quality classification. This study contributes to the broader efforts of ensuring safe water access and serves as a framework for employing machine learning in environmental quality assessments. The findings aim to assist researchers, policymakers, and public health officials in making informed decisions based on reliable machine learning predictions.

中文摘要翻译: 获得可饮用水对健康、经济发展和可持续性至关重要。然而，由于水源数据的复杂性和变异性，准确分类水质仍然是一个重大挑战。本文通过机器学习和深度学习算法来解决饮用水可饮用性预测的挑战。文章提出了一种新颖的特征增强算法AquaAugmentor，用于提升这些模型在低维数据集上的预测性能。本研究利用包含水的化学属性（如pH值、硬度、固体物、氯胺、硫酸盐等）的数据集，评估了模型在使用和不使用AquaAugmentor时的性能。每个模型被应用于将水分类为可饮用或不可饮用，并基于测试准确率和AUC分数对其性能进行评估和比较。结果突显了我们提出算法的优势与局限性，为改进水质分类预测性能的最有效技术提供了见解。本研究为确保持续安全用水的更广泛努力做出了贡献，并作为将机器学习应用于环境质量评估的框架。研究结果旨在帮助研究人员、政策制定者和公共卫生官员基于可靠的机器学习预测做出明智决策。

</details>


### [119] [Knowledge-Assisted Multi-Graph Dependency Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection in Multi-Stage Industrial Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15799)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Taeseong Yoon,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种知识辅助的多图框架，结合过程知识与数据驱动图，通过多图注意力网络增强多阶段工业过程中的多元时间序列异常检测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于GNN的多元时间序列异常检测方法常忽略关键过程知识，即使考虑了过程知识，也难以无缝融入现有模型，导致性能次优。

Method: 构建三个互补图：一个纯数据驱动图，两个融合过程知识结构约束的精炼图；采用多图注意力网络融合这些图，实现更准确鲁棒的依赖关系建模。

Result: 在两个真实多阶段工业数据集上的综合实验表明，融合过程知识显著提升了异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 将过程知识显式纳入图学习能够增强依赖关系建模，有效提升多阶段工业过程中多元时间序列异常检测的性能。

Original Abstract: Industrial processes often generate complex, interdependent time-series data from multiple sensors across multiple stages, forming complex dependencies among variables and process stages. Effective monitoring and timely anomaly detection of these time series through multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTAD) is crucial for preventing failures and ensuring the reliability of automated systems. Graph neural networks (GNNs) have advanced MTAD by leveraging data-driven graphs to model complex dependencies among variables, effectively capturing relational structures within multivariate time series to enhance anomaly detection performance. However, existing GNN-based approaches often overlook critical process knowledge, and even when this knowledge is considered, seamlessly incorporating it into existing models remains inherently challenging, leading to suboptimal performance. To address this limitation, we propose a knowledge-assisted multi-graph framework for modeling sensor dependencies in multi-stage industrial processes for MTAD, which explicitly incorporates process knowledge into graph learning to enhance dependency modeling and improve anomaly detection performance. Our method constructs three complementary graphs: one purely data-driven and two refined by integrating structural constraints derived from process knowledge. To effectively leverage these graphs for anomaly detection, we employ a multi-graph attention network, enabling a more accurate and robust representation of complex dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on two real-world, multi-stage industrial datasets demonstrate that incorporating process knowledge substantially enhances anomaly detection performance.

中文摘要翻译: 工业过程通常会产生来自多个阶段、多个传感器的复杂且相互依赖的时间序列数据，形成变量与工艺阶段之间复杂的依赖关系。通过多元时间序列异常检测（MTAD）对这些时间序列进行有效监控和及时异常检测，对于防止故障和确保自动化系统的可靠性至关重要。图神经网络（GNN）通过利用数据驱动图来建模变量间的复杂依赖关系，有效捕捉多元时间序列中的关系结构，从而提升了异常检测性能。然而，现有的基于GNN的方法常常忽略关键的过程知识，即使考虑了这些知识，将其无缝融入现有模型仍然具有内在挑战，导致性能次优。针对这一局限，我们提出了一种面向多阶段工业过程中传感器依赖关系建模的知识辅助多图框架，用于多元时间序列异常检测。该框架显式地将过程知识纳入图学习，以增强依赖关系建模并提升异常检测性能。我们的方法构建了三个互补图：一个纯数据驱动图，以及两个融入过程知识结构约束的精炼图。为了有效利用这些图进行异常检测，我们采用多图注意力网络，从而实现对复杂依赖关系更准确、更鲁棒的表示。在两个真实的多阶段工业数据集上的综合实验表明，融合过程知识显著提升了异常检测性能。

</details>


### [120] [QUADS: Stabilizing NVFP4 Reinforcement Learning for MoE via QUantization-error Alignment across Dual Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15810)
*Zhengyang Zhuge,Hao Yu,Xin Wang,Zheng Li,Yizhong Cao,Dayiheng Liu,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE强化学习中使用NVFP4低精度格式进行rollout加速时出现的训练崩溃问题，论文提出了QUADS方法，通过训练侧的非对称量化感知训练和推理侧的残差激活补偿，实现了BF16级别的精度和比FP8更高的吞吐量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MoE大语言模型强化学习中的rollout生成是主要瓶颈，低精度加速（如FP8）被用来缓解这一问题。新兴的NVFP4格式本应比FP8有更高吞吐量，但直接应用于MoE RL rollout会导致训练约150步后崩溃，且rollout与训练器之间的log-probability差距迅速增大。论文旨在找出不稳定根源并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出QUADS（Quantization-error Alignment across Dual Sides）：1) 训练侧：非对称量化感知训练（Asymmetric QAT），对权重进行假量化（fake-quantizing），同时保持激活值未量化以更好地对齐；2) 推理侧：残差激活补偿（Residual Activation Compensation），修正高误差的激活通道，同时保留原生W4A4 FP4 GEMM运算。

Result: 通过训练-推理误差分析和受控消融实验，发现激活误差（而非权重误差）是FP4 RL不稳定的主要来源。权重可通过共享量化-反量化路径同步对齐，而激活在线重计算且被粗粒度的E2M1网格放大误差。QUADS在多个基准上达到BF16级精度，相比朴素NVFP4 RL平均pass@1提升21.49点，rollout吞吐量比FP8高约16%。

Conclusion: 通过识别FP4 MoE RL不稳定的根源（激活误差），并设计训练-推理双侧对齐的量化策略，QUADS成功将NVFP4低精度格式应用于MoE强化学习中，在保持精度的同时实现了比FP8更高的吞吐量加速。

Original Abstract: Rollout generation is a major bottleneck in Reinforcement Learning (RL) for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) Large Language Models, motivating low-precision rollout acceleration such as FP8. As an emerging low-precision format, NVFP4 combines fine-grained scaling for accuracy preservation with native W4A4 FP4 GEMMs for higher throughput than FP8. However, we find that directly applying NVFP4 to MoE RL rollout is impractical. NVFP4 rollout with BF16 training collapses after roughly 150 steps, accompanied by rapidly growing rollout-trainer log-probability gaps. Through training-inference error analysis and controlled ablations, we identify activation error, rather than weight error, as the dominant source of FP4 RL instability: weights can be synchronized and aligned by a shared quantization-dequantization path, whereas activations are recomputed online and error is amplified by the coarse E2M1 grid. Therefore, to stabilize NVFP4 RL for MoE, we propose QUantization-error Alignment across Dual Sides (QUADS). On the trainer side, we introduce Asymmetric Quantization-Aware Training fake-quantizing weights while keeping activations unquantized for better alignment. On the rollout side, Residual Activation Compensation corrects high-error activation channels while preserving native W4A4 GEMMs. In our MoE RL experiments on several benchmarks, QUADS achieves BF16-level accuracy, improves average pass@1 by 21.49 points over naive NVFP4 RL, and delivers ~16% higher rollout throughput than FP8.

中文摘要翻译: Rollout生成是混合专家（MoE）大语言模型强化学习（RL）中的主要瓶颈，这促使了如FP8等低精度rollout加速方法的发展。作为一种新兴的低精度格式，NVFP4结合了细粒度缩放以保持精度，以及原生W4A4 FP4通用矩阵乘法（GEMM）以提供比FP8更高的吞吐量。然而，我们发现直接将NVFP4应用于MoE RL rollout是不切实际的。使用NVFP4进行rollout、BF16进行训练的配置在约150步后训练崩溃，同时rollout与训练器之间的log-probability差距迅速增大。通过训练-推理误差分析和受控消融实验，我们识别出激活误差（而非权重误差）是FP4 RL不稳定的主导来源：权重可以通过共享的量化-反量化路径进行同步和对齐，而激活值则在线重新计算，其误差被粗粒度的E2M1网格放大。因此，为了稳定MoE模型的NVFP4 RL，我们提出了双侧量化误差对齐方法（QUADS）。在训练侧，我们引入了非对称量化感知训练——对权重进行假量化，同时保持激活值未量化以实现更好的对齐。在推理侧，残差激活补偿在保留原生W4A4 FP4 GEMM的同时，修正了高误差的激活通道。在多个基准上的MoE RL实验中，QUADS达到了BF16级别的精度，相比朴素NVFP4 RL平均pass@1提升了21.49个点，并且rollout吞吐量比FP8高出约16%。

</details>


### [121] [Graph Coloring Approach to Solving Sudoku with Oscillatory Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15814)
*Filip Sabo,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于振荡神经网络（ONN）的数独求解器，通过将数独问题建模为图着色问题，并改进现有求解器以降低计算成本、增加约束项，在4×4和9×9数独上显著提升了求解精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于HNN和ONN的数独求解器精度不足，需要一种更准确的物理启发的组合优化求解方法。

Method: 将数独问题形式化为图着色问题，修改现有图着色ONN求解器以降低计算复杂度，并引入额外的约束项来确保数独约束（每行、每列、每宫数字不重复）得到满足。

Result: 在4×4数独上达到近乎完美的精度，在9×9数独上针对不同未知数字数量也取得了较高精度，显著优于已有的HNN和ONN求解器。

Conclusion: 所提出的ONN求解器在精度上大幅超越现有HNN和ONN求解器，验证了ONN作为物理启发式计算范式在约束组合优化问题上的潜力。

Original Abstract: Oscillatory Neural Networks (ONNs) present an attractive physics-based computing paradigm rooted in the dynamics of a network of typically fully coupled oscillators aiming to minimize an underlying energy function. In this paper, we propose an ONN-based solver for one well-known constrained combinatorial optimization problem, namely a Sudoku, by formulating the problem as a Graph Coloring problem. By modifying the already existing Graph Coloring solver to a computationally cheaper version and introducing an additional term ensuring the fulfillment of the Sudoku constraints, our solver is shown to significantly outperform the existing HNN- and ONN solvers in terms of accuracy. In particular, we are able to achieve nearly flawless accuracies on $4 \times 4$ as well as rather high accuracies on $9 \times 9$ Sudoku puzzles for different numbers of unknown digits.

中文摘要翻译: 振荡神经网络（ONN）是一种有吸引力的、基于物理学的计算范式，其根植于通常全耦合的振荡器网络的动力学，旨在最小化底层能量函数。在本文中，我们通过将数独问题形式化为图着色问题，提出了一种基于ONN的求解器来解决这一著名的约束组合优化问题。通过将已有的图着色求解器修改为计算成本更低的版本，并引入一个确保数独约束得到满足的额外项，我们的求解器在精度上显著优于现有的HNN和ONN求解器。特别地，对于4×4数独谜题，我们能够实现近乎完美的精度；对于不同未知数字数量的9×9数独谜题，我们也取得了相当高的精度。

</details>


### [122] [In-context learning of closed form solution to simple linear regression task using transformer with linear self-attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15819)
*Katsuyuki Hagiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer在上下文学习中的机制，发现通过线性自注意力和层归一化，Transformer可以近似实现最小二乘估计的闭式解，而非基于梯度下降的近似解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 已有的研究表明Transformer能够在线上下文中实现线性/非线性回归问题的求解器（大多实现梯度下降算法），但这些实现是否真正通过训练获得仍不清楚。本文旨在澄清这一机制。

Method: 构建了一个具有线性自注意力的Transformer，在简单回归任务中通过上下文学习最小二乘估计。关键在于利用层归一化（Layer Normalization）近似获得闭式（解析）解，而非基于梯度下降算法的近似解。

Result: 实验表明，当目标输出为最小二乘估计时，该实现主要应用于经过l1正则化训练的Transformer中。

Conclusion: Transformer可以通过线性自注意力和层归一化实现最小二乘估计的闭式近似解，这为理解Transformer的上下文学习机制提供了新的视角。

Original Abstract: In-context learning is a remarkable property of transformers and has recently received a lot of interest. In many studies of in-context learning, it has been shown that transformers are capable of implementing solver for linear and non-linear regression problems, in which the most of them implement gradient descent algorithm. However, it is still unclear whether those implementations have actually been acquired through training. In this paper, we construct a transformer with linear self-attention, which in-context learns the least squares estimate in a simple regression task. The point here is that the closed form (analytical) solution is approximately obtained by using layer normalization rather than an approximate solution based on gradient descent algorithm. Then, we show an experimental example, in which our implementation is mainly used in the transformer trained with l1 regularization when the target output is the least squares estimate.

中文摘要翻译: 上下文学习是Transformer的一个显著特性，近来受到了广泛关注。在许多关于上下文学习的研究中，已有研究表明Transformer能够实现线性和非线性回归问题的求解器，其中大多数实现了梯度下降算法。然而，这些实现是否真正通过训练获得仍不清楚。在本文中，我们构建了一个具有线性自注意力的Transformer，该Transformer在简单回归任务中通过上下文学习最小二乘估计。关键在于，通过使用层归一化近似获得闭式（解析）解，而非基于梯度下降算法的近似解。随后，我们展示了一个实验示例，其中我们的实现主要应用于经过l1正则化训练的Transformer中，此时目标输出为最小二乘估计。

</details>


### [123] [Data-Native Global Optimization for Big Data K-means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15835)
*Ravil Mussabayev,Rustam Mussabayev,Zukhra Yerdaliyeva,Kuldeyev Nursultan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Big-means++ 是一种面向大数据的 K-means 聚类算法，通过在多随机样本诱导的替代 MSSC 景观上执行局部 K-means 精化，实现可扩展的全局搜索，并引入几何级样本量变化、多智能体异步搜索和自动收敛检测等机制，在 22 个数据集上优于 11 种对比算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大数据聚类中，K-means 对应的最小平方和聚类（MSSC）问题是 NP 难的，现有方法要么陷入局部最小值，要么需要代价高昂的元启发式混合策略，缺乏可扩展且全局搜索质量高的算法。

Method: Big-means++ 提出数据原生的全局搜索框架：每个随机样本定义不同的经验 MSSC 近似，通过流动 incumbent 策略在样本间传递质心状态（无回滚），实现样本间变异到全局搜索的转化。引入几何级样本量变化（shaking）以跨越不同分辨率尺度并处理簇不平衡。采用竞争性多智能体系统异步探索独立样本景观，将多样化随机轨迹转化为集体搜索智能。自动收敛检测在达到高质量解后及时停止，避免搜索退化。

Result: 在 22 个数据集上，Big-means++ 与 11 种对比算法（包括 Big-means、K-means++、变体等）进行对比实验，结果表明该方法在有效性、效率和鲁棒性方面均具有优势。

Conclusion: Big-means++ 通过将样本到样本的变异性转化为全局搜索机制，结合多尺度 shaking 和多智能体协作，有效解决了大数据 MSSC 聚类的可扩展性和全局优化难题，实验验证了其优越性能。

Original Abstract: Big data clustering remains challenging: the Minimum Sum-of-Squares Clustering (MSSC) problem underlying K-means is NP-hard, and existing methods either reach poor local minima or require prohibitive metaheuristic hybrids. We target arbitrarily tall data: a fixed feature space may contain arbitrarily many, possibly infinitely many, observations, while the algorithm accesses only finite random samples. We propose Big-means++, an algorithm achieving scalability and global-search quality by curating inputs to MSSC optimization on big data. It orchestrates local K-means refinements into a data-native global search for big data clustering. Rather than optimizing the full-data MSSC objective, Big-means++ traverses sample-induced surrogate landscapes. Each sample defines a distinct empirical MSSC approximation with a perturbed local-optimum structure, turning sample-to-sample variation into a global-search mechanism. Unlike Big-means, a flowing-incumbent strategy propagates centroid state across empirical landscapes through K-means refinements on fresh samples without rollback to a best-so-far solution. This increases mobility and favors stable, high-quality configurations across approximations of the full-data structure. A new shaking mechanism varies sample size geometrically, broadening the surrogate landscapes explored across resolution scales, accounting for cluster imbalance, and improving solution quality. A competitive multi-agent system asynchronously explores independent sampled landscapes, transforming diverse stochastic trajectories into collective search intelligence. Automatic convergence detection stops each agent after attaining a high-quality solution but before further search risks degrading it, while providing a universal speed-quality control. Experiments on 22 datasets against 11 competing algorithms demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness of Big-means++.

中文摘要翻译: 大数据聚类仍然具有挑战性：K-means 所基于的最小平方和聚类（MSSC）问题是 NP 难的，现有方法要么陷入较差的局部最小值，要么需要代价高昂的元启发式混合策略。我们针对任意高规模数据：一个固定的特征空间可能包含任意多、甚至无限多的观测值，而算法仅能访问有限的随机样本。我们提出 Big-means++，一种通过为大数据上的 MSSC 优化精心设计输入来实现可扩展性和全局搜索质量的算法。它将局部 K-means 精化协调为面向大数据聚类的数据原生全局搜索。Big-means++ 并不优化全数据的 MSSC 目标，而是遍历样本诱导的替代景观。每个样本定义了一个不同的经验 MSSC 近似，具有扰动的局部最优结构，从而将样本到样本的变异性转化为全局搜索机制。与 Big-means 不同，一种流动 incumbent 策略通过在新样本上执行 K-means 精化来在经验景观间传递质心状态，而无需回滚到迄今为止的最佳解。这增加了流动性，并倾向于在全数据结构的各近似上保持稳定且高质量的配置。一种新的 shaking 机制以几何级数变化样本量，拓宽了在不同分辨率尺度上探索的替代景观，考虑了簇不平衡，并提高了解质量。一个竞争性多智能体系统异步地探索独立的采样景观，将多样化的随机轨迹转化为集体搜索智能。自动收敛检测在智能体达到高质量解之后、进一步搜索可能导致退化之前及时停止每个智能体，同时提供了通用的速度-质量控制。在 22 个数据集上与 11 种竞争算法的实验证明了 Big-means++ 的有效性、效率和鲁棒性。

</details>


### [124] [ContinuityBench: A Benchmark and Systems Study of Stateful Failover in Multi-Provider LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15899)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有状态的多提供商代理架构，通过历史转发策略解决大语言模型部署中故障切换时对话上下文丢失的问题，并引入两个新指标（CPR和CLO）以及开源评测工具 continuity-bench，实验证明该方法在750次故障切换事件中实现了99.20%的连续性保留率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在生产级大语言模型部署中，高API可用性保证并不等同于对话连续性。当主提供商发生故障或严格限流时，传统的无状态故障切换机制虽能维持正常运转，但会静默丢弃对话历史，严重破坏用户体验。现有方法缺乏对故障切换时上下文保留程度的量化评估指标和有效解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种有状态的多提供商代理架构，采用历史转发（History-Forwarding）策略，在故障切换事件中跨异构大语言模型端点无缝重建对话状态。同时发布了 continuity-bench 开源评测框架，用于在高并发提供商故障条件下对上下文保留进行压力测试。

Result: 在750次故障切换事件的实证评估中，有状态代理实现了99.20%的连续性保留率（CPR）[95%置信区间: 98.27%, 99.63%]，能够将深层对话上下文干净地转移到备用提供商，而标准无状态架构的保留率接近0%。此外，实验还刻画了故障切换延迟分布，论证了使用带抖动的异步指数退避策略对于防止严格限流的备用API出现级联重试风暴至关重要。

Conclusion: 研究结果为构建鲁棒的、保留状态的多模型推理系统提供了原则性基础，证明了有状态故障切换机制在保持对话连续性方面的显著优势，并强调了合理的退避策略在防止系统雪崩中的关键作用。

Original Abstract: In production large language model (LLM) deployments, high API availability guarantees do not equate to conversational continuity. When a primary provider experiences an outage or strict rate-limiting, naive stateless failover mechanisms successfully maintain uptime but silently discard conversation history, severely disrupting the user experience. To rigorously quantify and resolve this failure mode, we introduce two novel metrics: Continuity Preservation Rate (CPR) and Continuity Latency Overhead (CLO). We propose a stateful, multi-provider proxy architecture utilizing a History-Forwarding strategy to seamlessly reconstruct conversational state across heterogeneous LLM endpoints during failover events. Furthermore, we release continuity-bench, https://github.com/Vishal-sys-code/continuity-bench, an open evaluation harness designed to stress-test context preservation under high-concurrency provider failure conditions. Our empirical evaluation ($N=750$ failover events) demonstrates that our stateful proxy achieves a 99.20\% CPR [95\% CI: 98.27\%, 99.63\%], cleanly transferring deep conversational context to fallback providers, compared to a near-0\% preservation rate for standard stateless architectures. Finally, we characterize failover latency distributions, identifying the critical necessity of asynchronous exponential backoff with jitter to prevent cascading retry storms against strict-limit fallback APIs. Our results provide a principled foundation for building robust, state-preserving multi-model inference systems.

中文摘要翻译: 在生产级大语言模型（LLM）部署中，高API可用性保证并不等同于对话连续性。当主提供商发生故障或遭遇严格限流时，朴素的无状态故障切换机制虽能维持正常运行时间，但会静默丢弃对话历史，严重破坏用户体验。为严格量化并解决这一失效模式，我们引入了两个新颖的指标：连续性保留率（Continuity Preservation Rate, CPR）和连续性延迟开销（Continuity Latency Overhead, CLO）。我们提出了一种有状态的多提供商代理架构，利用历史转发（History-Forwarding）策略，在故障切换事件中跨异构LLM端点无缝重建对话状态。此外，我们发布了 continuity-bench（https://github.com/Vishal-sys-code/continuity-bench），这是一个开源评测框架，旨在对高并发提供商故障条件下的上下文保留进行压力测试。我们的实证评估（N=750次故障切换事件）表明，所提出的有状态代理实现了99.20%的CPR [95%置信区间: 98.27%, 99.63%]，能够将深层对话上下文干净地转移到备用提供商，而标准无状态架构的保留率接近0%。最后，我们刻画了故障切换延迟分布，识别出使用带抖动的异步指数退避策略对于防止严格限流备用API出现级联重试风暴的关键必要性。我们的研究结果为构建鲁棒的、保留状态的多模型推理系统提供了原则性基础。

</details>


### [125] [A Semiparametric Framework for Stochastic Fundamental Diagram Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15907)
*Pengnan Chi,Xiaoliang Ma,Magnus Jansson,Magnus Nordenvaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种半参数随机基本图（SFD）建模框架，通过设计内在满足物理约束的函数形式，结合神经网络结构捕捉复杂非线性模式，并推导矩匹配方程确保模型良定性，在真实交通数据集上优于基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有随机基本图模型难以同时满足严格的物理约束并保持足够的灵活性以捕捉复杂的非线性交通模式。

Method: 提出半参数SFD建模框架：设计特殊函数形式内在满足条件流量分布矩的物理约束，同时融入神经网络结构捕捉经验模式；推导矩匹配方程组将物理约束转化为条件分布的参数化，并证明位置-尺度分布族存在唯一解以保证模型良定性；还可扩展至非位置-尺度分布。

Result: 在真实数据集上，所提方法一致优于代表性基线模型，在概率预测精度和不确定性量化鲁棒性方面表现更佳，尤其在拥堵区域优势显著。

Conclusion: 该框架为随机交通流建模提供了理论扎实且灵活的基础，兼具物理约束的一致性与复杂模式的拟合能力。

Original Abstract: The stochastic fundamental diagram (SFD) provides a probabilistic description of the relationship between traffic density and flow or speed, enabling uncertainty-aware traffic modeling. However, existing stochastic models frequently struggle to accommodate rigorous physical constraints while retaining sufficient flexibility to capture complex nonlinear patterns. To address this, we propose a novel semiparametric SFD modeling framework by leveraging specially designed functional forms. These functions intrinsically satisfy physical constraints defined on the moments of the conditional flow distribution given traffic density while incorporating neural-network-based structures to capture complex empirical patterns. We derive a system of moment-matching equations to convert physical constraints into the parameterization of the conditional distribution, proving that a unique solution exists for the location-scale family of distributions, thereby guaranteeing model well-posedness. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the framework can be extended to non-location-scale distributions, including those requiring additional boundary constraints. Empirical evaluations on a real-world dataset reveal that our approach consistently outperforms representative baselines, delivering superior probabilistic accuracy and robust uncertainty quantification, particularly in congested regimes. Overall, the proposed framework provides a theoretically grounded and flexible foundation for stochastic traffic flow modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 随机基本图（SFD）提供了交通密度与流量或速度之间关系的概率描述，从而支持不确定性感知的交通建模。然而，现有的随机模型常常难以在保持足够灵活性以捕捉复杂非线性模式的同时，满足严格的物理约束。针对这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的半参数SFD建模框架，该框架利用特殊设计的函数形式。这些函数内在满足定义在给定交通密度下条件流量分布矩上的物理约束，同时融入基于神经网络的结构以捕捉复杂的经验模式。我们推导了一个矩匹配方程组，将物理约束转化为条件分布的参数化，并证明了位置-尺度分布族存在唯一解，从而保证了模型的良定性。此外，我们还展示了该框架可以扩展到非位置-尺度分布，包括需要额外边界约束的分布。在真实数据集上的实证评估表明，我们的方法一致优于代表性基线模型，在概率预测精度和鲁棒的不确定性量化方面表现更佳，尤其是在拥堵区域。总体而言，所提出的框架为随机交通流建模提供了理论扎实且灵活的基础。

</details>


### [126] [(MPO)$^2$: Multivariate Polynomial Optimization based on Matrix Product Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15916)
*Niccolò Ciolli,Anders Vestergaard Nørskov,Michael Kastoryano,Petr Taborsky,Morten Mørup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出(MPO)²框架，利用矩阵乘积算子(MPO)进行特征嵌入结合紧凑多项式权重张量，实现特征顺序无关的多项式函数逼近，在回归和分类任务上优于现有张量分解多项式模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有张量化多项式模型中，典型多项式分解(CANDECOMP/PARAFAC)的秩表达能力有限，而张量列(Tensor Train)公式具有特征顺序依赖性，限制了多项式函数逼近的灵活性和效率。

Method: 提出(MPO)²框架，结合了学习得到的MPO特征嵌入和紧凑多项式权重张量，支持投影、卷积和掩码等结构化算子，实现特征顺序无关的多项式表示。

Result: 在回归和分类基准测试中，(MPO)²优于现有基于张量分解的多项式模型。

Conclusion: (MPO)²提供了一种灵活且高效的多项式函数逼近替代方案。

Original Abstract: Central to machine learning and signal processing is the ability to perform universal function approximation and learn complex input-output relationships from limited numbers of observations. Multivariate polynomial models offer a natural way to express such relationships through multiplicative feature interactions, but their coefficient tensors grow exponentially in size with the polynomial degree. Existing tensorized polynomial models reduce this cost, yet canonical polyadic decompositions have rank-limited expressivity, and tensor train formulations are feature order dependent. We introduce Multivariate Polynomial Optimization based on Matrix Product Operators (MPO)$^2$, a framework that combines learned MPO feature embeddings with compact polynomial weight tensors. This yields feature order independent polynomial representations that can incorporate structured operators such as projections, convolutions, and masks for weight tensor symmetries. Across regression and classification benchmarks, (MPO)$^2$ improves over existing tensor decomposition based polynomial models and provides a flexible alternative for efficient polynomial function approximation.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习和信号处理的核心能力是执行通用函数逼近，并从有限的观测中学习复杂的输入-输出关系。多元多项式模型通过乘性特征交互自然表达了这种关系，但其系数张量的大小随多项式次数呈指数增长。现有的张量化多项式模型降低了这一成本，然而典型多项式分解(CP分解)的表达能力受限于秩，而张量列(Tensor Train)公式则依赖于特征顺序。我们提出了基于矩阵乘积算子(MPO)²的多元多项式优化框架，该框架将学习得到的MPO特征嵌入与紧凑的多项式权重张量相结合，实现了特征顺序无关的多项式表示，并能融入投影、卷积和掩码等结构化算子以处理权重张量的对称性。在回归和分类基准测试中，(MPO)²优于现有的基于张量分解的多项式模型，为高效多项式函数逼近提供了一种灵活的替代方案。

</details>


### [127] [On the Failure of Boundary-Seeking Distillation in Bottlenecked Generative Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15919)
*Mohamed Amine Kina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了无数据知识蒸馏中边界搜索原则是否适用于自编码器蒸馏，发现该原则在瓶颈式生成架构中存在根本性问题，并提出了一种流形感知的合成方法作为有效基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索先前用于分类器的边界搜索知识蒸馏方法（如CAKE）能否扩展到自编码器的无数据蒸馏中。

Method: 将连续重构任务重新表述为逐特征的密集分类任务，使解码器输出分类logits；对比分析了边界搜索目标与流形感知合成方法在瓶颈式生成架构中的表现。

Result: 边界搜索目标在瓶颈式生成架构中根本不可行，因为解码器是受共享低维瓶颈约束的紧密耦合特征级分类器阵列，独立采样对比目标会产生梯度冲突；而流形感知合成方法能完全避免这些冲突。

Conclusion: 边界搜索原则不适用于自编码器蒸馏，流形感知合成是更有效的无数据生成式蒸馏基线方法。

Original Abstract: Data-free knowledge distillation transfers the knowledge encoded in a teacher model to a student model without access to the original training data. Prior work such as Contrastive Abductive Knowledge Extraction (CAKE) achieves this for classifiers by synthesizing samples near the teacher's decision boundary. In this work, we investigate whether this boundary-seeking principle extends to autoencoder distillation through experiments on the MNIST dataset . To enable a direct comparison, we reformulate continuous reconstruction as a dense, per-feature classification task, allowing the decoder to output categorical logits. We show that boundary-seeking objectives are fundamentally ill-posed in bottlenecked generative architectures. CAKE operates on a single, instance-level objective, but a decoder acts as an array of tightly coupled, feature-level classifiers constrained by a shared low-dimensional bottleneck. Independently sampling contrastive targets for these coupled outputs violates the geometry of the learned latent manifold and produces severe gradient conflicts instead of informative boundary samples. Manifold-aware synthesis bypasses these conflicts entirely and establishes an effective baseline for data-free generative distillation.

中文摘要翻译: 无数据知识蒸馏可以在不访问原始训练数据的情况下，将教师模型中编码的知识迁移到学生模型中。先前的工作如对比溯因知识提取（CAKE）通过合成教师决策边界附近的样本来实现分类器的蒸馏。在本工作中，我们通过在MNIST数据集上的实验探究了该边界搜索原则是否适用于自编码器蒸馏。为了实现直接对比，我们将连续重构任务重新表述为逐特征的密集分类任务，使解码器能够输出分类logits。我们发现，边界搜索目标在瓶颈式生成架构中从根本上是不适定的。CAKE基于单一的实例级目标，而解码器则是由共享低维瓶颈约束的紧密耦合的特征级分类器阵列构成。对这些耦合输出独立采样对比目标会破坏所学潜在流形的几何结构，并产生严重的梯度冲突，而非信息性的边界样本。流形感知的合成方法能够完全避开这些冲突，并为无数据生成式蒸馏建立了有效的基线。

</details>


### [128] [Knowledge-Guided Cross-Modal Fusion for Adult-to-Pediatric ECG Transfer via Label-Conditioned Contrastive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15928)
*Xinran Liu,Yuwen Li,Hongxiang Gao,Heyang Xu,Jianqing Li,Zongmin Wang,Chengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PEACE框架，通过知识引导的多模态对齐（轴级描述+标签查询网络+课程式自适应融合），利用成人ECG数据预训练提升儿科ECG诊断性能，在标签稀缺场景下显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 儿科ECG标注稀缺，而现有主要在成人ECG上预训练的模型迁移到儿科效果差；已有的多模态ECG-文本方法在全局样本级对齐，会纠缠共存诊断间的证据，限制了跨年龄段的迁移能力。

Method: PEACE框架包含三条技术路线：(1) 将每个诊断按节律、形态、ST-T三个轴描述，每个记录只组合阳性标签的描述为三个轴令牌和一个融合嵌入；(2) 标签查询网络(LQN)以诊断标签为查询，交叉关注ECG令牌和轴令牌；(3) 标签集感知双向对比学习(LSBC)对齐池化ECG特征与融合嵌入；(4) 课程自适应融合(CAF)根据平滑分类损失和训练进度门控对齐强度。知识分支仅用于训练监督，推理仅需ECG信号。

Result: 在ZZU-pECG上，零样本、50样本和全微调下的宏平均AUC分别达59.39%、81.74%和91.56%，在有限监督下提升最明显；零样本显著优于域自适应基线，50样本AUC与DANN相当；在PTB-XL上微调后9个统一标签的宏平均AUC达96.90%；消融实验证实标签条件化的知识对齐而非全局文本融合是儿科迁移增益的关键。

Conclusion: 标签条件化的知识对齐（按诊断轴描述+标签查询+课程式融合）优于全局文本融合，能有效弥合成人-儿科ECG的跨域差距，在标注稀缺的儿科场景下实现显著性能提升。

Original Abstract: Adult and pediatric electrocardiogram (ECG) interpretation relies on age-sensitive criteria, and models pretrained mainly on adult ECGs often transfer poorly to pediatric populations when pediatric labels are scarce. Existing multimodal ECG--text methods typically align waveforms and text at the global sample level, entangling evidence from co-occurring diagnoses and limiting transfer under this gap. We propose Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment via Cross-modal Enhancement (PEACE), a knowledge-guided framework pretrained on the largely adult MIMIC-IV ECG corpus. PEACE describes each diagnosis along rhythm, morphology, and ST--T axes and, per recording, composes only positive-label descriptors into three axis tokens and a fused embedding. A label query network (LQN) uses diagnostic labels as queries to cross-attend over ECG tokens and axis tokens, while label set aware bidirectional contrastive learning (LSBC) aligns pooled ECG features with the fused embedding when recordings share diagnoses. Curriculum adaptive fusion (CAF) gates alignment strength according to smoothed classification loss and training progress, limiting disruption during early optimization. The knowledge branch is used only for training supervision; inference uses ECG signals alone. On ZZU-pECG, PEACE reaches macro average AUCs of 59.39%, 81.74%, and 91.56% under zero-shot, 50-shot, and full fine-tuning, with the clearest gains over foundation and knowledge-pretraining baselines under limited supervision; versus domain adaptation initializations, zero-shot improves substantially while 50-shot AUC is comparable to DANN. After fine-tuning on PTB-XL, PEACE reaches 96.90% macro average AUC over nine harmonized labels. Ablations confirm that label-conditioned knowledge alignment, rather than global text fusion, is the key driver of pediatric transfer gains.

中文摘要翻译: 成人和儿科心电图（ECG）解读依赖于年龄敏感的标准，而主要在成人ECG上预训练的模型在儿科标签稀缺时往往难以有效迁移。现有的多模态ECG-文本方法通常在全局样本级别对齐波形和文本，这会纠缠共存诊断间的证据，限制了跨域迁移的能力。我们提出儿科-成人ECG对齐的跨模态增强框架（PEACE），一种主要在成人MIMIC-IV ECG语料库上预训练的知识引导框架。PEACE沿节律、形态和ST-T轴描述每个诊断，并为每条记录仅组合阳性标签的描述，形成三个轴令牌和一个融合嵌入。标签查询网络（LQN）以诊断标签为查询，对ECG令牌和轴令牌进行交叉注意力计算；同时，标签集感知双向对比学习（LSBC）在记录共享诊断时对齐池化的ECG特征与融合嵌入。课程自适应融合（CAF）根据平滑分类损失和训练进度门控对齐强度，在早期优化阶段限制干扰。知识分支仅用于训练监督；推理仅使用ECG信号。在ZZU-pECG数据集上，PEACE在零样本、50样本和全微调设置下分别达到59.39%、81.74%和91.56%的宏平均AUC，在有限监督下相比基础模型和知识预训练基线提升最为明显；与域自适应初始化相比，零样本性能显著提升，而50样本AUC与DANN相当。在PTB-XL上微调后，PEACE在九个统一标签上达到96.90%的宏平均AUC。消融实验证实，标签条件化的知识对齐（而非全局文本融合）是儿科迁移增益的关键驱动因素。

</details>


### [129] [An Exploratory Study of Single Channel Surface Electromyography for Hand Gesture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15972)
*Daanish Hindustani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用单通道表面肌电信号(sEMG)结合轻量级机器学习模型进行十种手势分类的可行性。结果表明，结合时频域特征、皮尔逊相关性过滤和紧凑型前馈神经网络，在有限的时间和空间信息下可达90%的准确率，为低成本、低功耗手势识别应用提供了可行方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统高精度sEMG手势识别依赖多通道传感器阵列和计算密集型模型，难以部署在低功耗和嵌入式系统中。因此，本文探索使用单通道sEMG结合轻量级机器学习架构实现手势分类的可行性。

Method: 将原始sEMG信号转换为综合特征表示（包括时域、频域、高阶过零和相对强度特征），采用皮尔逊相关性过滤去除高相关特征进行降冗余，并选择性应用LDA和PCA降维。系统评估了三种分类器：前馈神经网络(NN)、K近邻(KNN)和支持向量机(SVM)，并在四项实验中进行对比。

Result: 结合时域和频域特征、皮尔逊过滤以及紧凑型神经网络，在有限时空信息条件下，手势分类准确率最高可达90%。

Conclusion: 单通道sEMG系统结合轻量级机器学习模型具有实现高精度手势识别的潜力，为成本效益高、低功耗的手势识别应用提供了可行途径。

Original Abstract: Accurate hand gesture recognition using surface electromyography (sEMG) typically relies on multichannel sensor arrays and computationally intensive models. This limits practical deployment in low-power and embedded systems. This study investigates the feasibility of classifying ten hand gestures using a single sEMG channel combined with lightweight machine learning architectures. Raw sEMG signals were transformed into a comprehensive feature-based representation, including time-domain, frequency-domain, higher-order-crossing, and relative-intensity features. Feature redundancy was reduced using Pearson correlation filtering and the removal of highly correlated features, while dimensionality-reduction techniques (LDA and PCA) were applied selectively. Three classifiers, a feed-forward neural network (NN), k-nearest neighbors (KNN), and a support vector machine (SVM), were systematically evaluated across four experiments. Results demonstrate that combining time and frequency features with Pearson filtering and a compact NN can achieve up to 90 percent accuracy, even with limited temporal and spatial information. These findings highlight the potential of single-channel sEMG systems for cost-effective, low-power gesture-recognition applications.

中文摘要翻译: 基于表面肌电信号(sEMG)的精确手势识别通常依赖于多通道传感器阵列和计算密集型模型，这限制了其在低功耗和嵌入式系统中的实际部署。本研究探究了使用单通道sEMG结合轻量级机器学习架构对十种手势进行分类的可行性。原始sEMG信号被转换为综合特征表示，包括时域特征、频域特征、高阶过零特征和相对强度特征。通过皮尔逊相关性过滤去除高相关特征来降低特征冗余，并选择性应用线性判别分析(LDA)和主成分分析(PCA)等降维技术。系统评估了三种分类器——前馈神经网络(NN)、K近邻(KNN)和支持向量机(SVM)在四项实验中的表现。结果表明，将时域和频域特征与皮尔逊过滤以及紧凑型神经网络相结合，即使在时空信息有限的情况下，也能达到高达90%的准确率。这些发现凸显了单通道sEMG系统在开发成本效益高、低功耗的手势识别应用中的潜力。

</details>


### [130] [Presentation, Not Mechanism: A Render Confound in Deprecation-Aware Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16019)
*Zhaoyang Jiang,Zhizhong Fu,Zicheng Li,Yunsoo Kim,Jiacong Mi,Xuanqi Peng,Fei Teng,Honghan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了证据状态检索中的一个关键混杂因素：呈现方式的差异。通过控制呈现格式的消融实验发现，精细粒度的版本管理方法（RevisionLedger）相比扁平检索的优势几乎全部来自更清晰的呈现方式，而非其细粒度的机制本身。在控制呈现后，粗粒度的失效标记反而是对当前状态查询最有效的机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AI系统越来越多地从会自我修订的记录（如问题线程、百科历史、政策日志、长对话）中检索信息，挑战不仅在于找到相关证据，还在于判断哪些主张仍然有效、哪些已被取代、何时应放弃回答。现有结构化记忆方法虽然承诺通过类型化边、时间更新和冲突状态来解决这一问题，但评估时常常同时改变了机制和提示呈现方式，存在混淆效应。

Method: 研究了证据状态修订（Evidence-State Revision）问题，对比了三种方法：扁平检索（flat retrieval）、粗粒度边失效标记（coarse edge invalidation）和细粒度版本账本（fine-grained RevisionLedger）。在来自GitHub、多仓库问题历史、Wikipedia和DyKnow风格时间流的2,907个高一致性问题上进行评估。关键设计是引入了一个呈现匹配的控制实验（render-matched control），通过相同布局但禁用弃用功能来隔离呈现方式的影响。

Result: 呈现匹配控制实验发现：当某个值被更改后又恢复时，RevisionLedger看似比扁平基线高出+0.182，但几乎所有增益都来自更容易的呈现方式；细粒度机制的残差与零无法区分（在两组评判模型中为+0.021至+0.025）。控制呈现后，粗粒度失效标记是唯一对当前状态查询有效的机制，比细粒度账本高出0.084。

Conclusion: 记忆评估应固定呈现方式（render fixed），弃用感知系统应部署覆盖其查询所需的最粗保留状态。查询充分性原则指出，溯源机制主要需要保留已失效的证据，而非更丰富的类型标注。

Original Abstract: AI systems increasingly retrieve from records that revise themselves: issue threads, encyclopedic histories, policy logs, and long conversations. The challenge is not only finding relevant evidence, but deciding which claims remain in force, which were superseded, and when to abstain. Structured memories promise to solve this with typed edges, temporal updates, and conflict status, yet evaluations often change mechanism and prompt presentation together. We study this as Evidence-State Revision, comparing flat retrieval, coarse edge invalidation, and fine-grained RevisionLedger on 2,907 high-agreement questions from GitHub, multi-repo issue histories, Wikipedia, and DyKnow-style temporal streams. A render-matched control (same layout, deprecation disabled) reveals the central confound: when a value is changed and later restored, RevisionLedger appears to beat a flat baseline by +0.182, but almost all the gain comes from easier presentation; the fine-grained mechanism residual is indistinguishable from zero (+0.021 to +0.025 across two judge families). After presentation is controlled, coarse invalidation is the only mechanism that pays for current-state queries, beating the fine ledger by 0.084; the same query-sufficiency principle says provenance mainly needs retained invalidated evidence, not richer typing. Memory evaluations should hold render fixed, and deprecation-aware systems should deploy the coarsest retained state that covers their queries.

中文摘要翻译: AI系统越来越多地从会自我修订的记录中进行检索：问题线程、百科历史、政策日志和长对话。挑战不仅在于找到相关证据，还在于判断哪些主张仍然有效、哪些已被取代、以及何时应当放弃回答。结构化记忆承诺通过类型化边、时间更新和冲突状态来解决这一问题，然而评估时常常同时改变了机制和提示呈现方式。我们将此作为证据状态修订问题进行研究，在来自GitHub、多仓库问题历史、Wikipedia和DyKnow风格时间流的2,907个高一致性问题上，对比了扁平检索、粗粒度边失效标记和细粒度的RevisionLedger。一个呈现匹配的控制实验（相同布局，禁用弃用功能）揭示了核心混淆因素：当某个值被更改后又恢复时，RevisionLedger看似比扁平基线高出+0.182，但几乎所有增益都来自更容易的呈现方式；细粒度机制的残差与零无法区分（在两组评判模型中为+0.021至+0.025）。在控制呈现方式后，粗粒度失效标记是唯一对当前状态查询有效的机制，比细粒度账本高出0.084；同样的查询充分性原则指出，溯源机制主要需要保留已失效的证据，而非更丰富的类型标注。记忆评估应固定呈现方式，弃用感知系统应部署覆盖其查询所需的最粗保留状态。

</details>


### [131] [Constrained Hebbian Learning Supports Efficient Representational Allocation under Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16027)
*Patrick Inoue,Florian Röhrbein,Andreas Knoblauch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在生物系统的代谢与连接性约束下，兴奋性竞争Hebbian规则能否支持突触资源分配，并与参考学习规则（DDTP、BP）在成本-性能权衡上进行比较。结果表明Hebbian学习能在降低任务信息成本的同时保持相当的分类性能，支持将其视为一种突触资源分配机制而非通用分类优化策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生物神经系统面临高昂的突触维护成本和有限的连接性，这些约束要求神经编码以低冗余的方式压缩行为相关信息。现有学习规则（如BP）在资源受限条件下的表现尚不明确，本文旨在检验Hebbian学习能否在此类约束下实现更优的资源分配。

Method: 使用基于互信息的变分信息瓶颈（Variational Information Bottleneck）量化表征成本；在三个视听基准数据集（AVE、Kinetics-Sounds、VGGSound100）上使用固定视听嵌入，隔离下游联想可塑性；在匹配稀疏性与架构约束条件下，将Hebbian学习与稠密差分目标传播（DDTP）和反向传播（BP）进行比较。

Result: 在主要压缩比较中，Hebbian学习的任务信息成本（CTI）低于稀疏BP和DDTP，与带非负权重的浅层BP相当。Hebbian学习并未统一提升分类性能，而是改变了任务相关信息与表征成本之间的权衡，在多个设置中以更低CTI保持了相当的功能性能。

Conclusion: 存在成本-性能权衡而非统一的准确率提升。对于给定的任务相关信息水平，Hebbian表征保留了更少的输入信息同时保持了功能性能（尽管在某些数据集上准确率略有下降）。这些发现支持将Hebbian学习解释为一种突触资源分配机制，而非最大化视听分类准确率的通用策略。

Original Abstract: Introduction: Biological systems face anatomical and metabolic constraints, including costly synaptic maintenance and limited connectivity. These constraints favor neural codes that compress behaviorally relevant information into low-redundancy patterns. We test whether an excitatory competitive Hebbian rule can support synaptic resource allocation under such constraints and whether the resulting representations occupy a more favorable cost-performance regime than reference learning rules.
  Methods: Representational cost is quantified using mutual-information-based measures derived from the Variational Information Bottleneck. Experiments use fixed audiovisual embeddings from three audiovisual benchmarks (AVE, Kinetics-Sounds, VGGSound100) to isolate downstream associative plasticity. Hebbian learning is compared with Dense Difference Target Propagation (DDTP) and backpropagation (BP) under matched sparsity and architectural constraints.
  Results: Hebbian learning achieves lower task-information cost (CTI) than sparse BP and DDTP in the main compressed comparisons, while reaching CTI values comparable to shallow BP with nonnegative weights. Rather than uniformly improving classification performance, Hebbian learning shifts the trade-off between task-relevant information and representational cost, yielding lower CTI at comparable functional performance in several settings.
  Discussion: The results indicate a cost-performance trade-off rather than uniform accuracy gains. For a given level of task-relevant information, Hebbian representations retain less input information while preserving functional performance, although accuracy is slightly reduced on some datasets. These findings support interpreting Hebbian learning as a mechanism for synaptic resource allocation rather than as a general strategy for maximizing audiovisual classification accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 引言：生物系统面临解剖学和代谢方面的约束，包括高昂的突触维护成本和有限的连接性。这些约束偏好那些将行为相关信息压缩为低冗余模式的神经编码。我们测试了兴奋性竞争Hebbian规则能否在此类约束下支持突触资源分配，以及由此产生的表征是否比参考学习规则占据更有利的成本-性能区间。方法：使用基于变分信息瓶颈的互信息度量来量化表征成本。实验采用来自三个视听基准数据集（AVE、Kinetics-Sounds、VGGSound100）的固定视听嵌入，以隔离下游联想可塑性。在匹配的稀疏性和架构约束条件下，将Hebbian学习与稠密差分目标传播（DDTP）及反向传播（BP）进行比较。结果：在主要的压缩比较中，Hebbian学习实现了比稀疏BP和DDTP更低的任务信息成本（CTI），同时达到与带非负权重的浅层BP相当的CTI值。Hebbian学习并未统一提升分类性能，而是改变了任务相关信息与表征成本之间的权衡，在多个设置中以更低CTI保持了相当的功能性能。讨论：结果表明存在成本-性能权衡而非统一的准确率提升。对于给定的任务相关信息水平，Hebbian表征保留了更少的输入信息同时保持了功能性能，尽管在某些数据集上准确率略有下降。这些发现支持将Hebbian学习解释为一种突触资源分配机制，而非最大化视听分类准确率的通用策略。

</details>


### [132] [CLaC@FinMMEval 2026 Task 3: Sentiment-Augmented Deep Reinforcement Learning for Active Trading -- An Alpha-Reward Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16028)
*Andrei Neagu,Eeham Khan,Leila Kosseim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab任务3，提出了一种基于深度强化学习的交易决策系统。该系统将每日交易决策建模为离散动作马尔可夫决策过程，比较了四种强化学习算法（PG、PPO、DQL、DDPG），利用技术指标、周期日历编码和LLaMA 3.2 1B生成的新闻情感分数作为输入，并引入超额市场回报的alpha奖励和随机化回合起始日期来减少过拟合。实验结果表明，DDPG在测试集上整体表现最强，DQL因验证集夏普比率最高而被选为线上部署模型。对于TSLA和BTC，强化学习策略均显著优于买入并持有策略，但也揭示了从牛市到熊市环境下验证集到测试集的泛化差距问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有金融交易方法通常依赖传统规则或简单信号，缺乏自适应决策能力；同时，基于新闻和市场数据的跨资产交易决策问题具有时序性和高噪声特点，需要更高效的强化学习框架来处理。本文旨在探索多种深度强化学习算法在每日做多、做平、做空交易决策中的表现，并寻找能够持续跑赢买入并持有策略的稳健方法。

Method: 将交易问题建模为离散动作马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）；使用技术指标、周期日历编码和LLaMA 3.2 1B生成的每日新闻情感分数作为状态特征；比较四种深度强化学习算法：Policy Gradient (PG)、Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)、Deep Q-Learning (DQL) 和 Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG)；引入基于超额市场回报的alpha奖励函数，并采用随机化回合起始日期来缓解过拟合；使用Ray Tune进行超参数优化（每个算法-资产对180次试验），基于验证集夏普比率进行早停和模型选择。

Result: 在CLEF任务3测试集上，DDPG整体表现最强。DQL因验证集夏普比率最高被预先选定为线上部署模型。对于TSLA，DDPG和DQL分别实现54.96%和52.62%的累积收益，远优于买入并持有的16.45%。对于BTC，DDPG实现1.58%的正收益，而买入并持有亏损34.27%。实验结果还揭示了显著的验证集到测试集泛化差距，表明在牛市条件下选择的策略难以迁移到熊市环境。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习方法在基于新闻和市场数据的交易日交易决策中能够显著优于买入并持有策略，尤其DDPG和DQL表现突出。然而，不同市场环境（牛市vs熊市）之间存在明显的策略泛化差距，提示未来需要引入跨市场周期的领域适应或鲁棒训练方法。同时，alpha奖励设计和训练数据随机化对提升模型泛化能力具有重要作用。

Original Abstract: This paper presents our system for Task 3 of the CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab, which requires daily long, flat, or short trading decisions for Bitcoin (BTC) and Tesla (TSLA) using news and historical market data. We formulate the problem as a discrete-action Markov Decision Process and compare four deep reinforcement learning algorithms: Policy Gradient (PG), Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Deep Q-Learning (DQL), and Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG). The agents use technical indicators, cyclical calendar encodings, and daily news sentiment scores produced by LLaMA 3.2 1B. To reduce overfitting and align training with the objective of outperforming buy-and-hold, we introduce an alpha reward based on excess market return and randomize episode start dates. Hyperparameters are optimized with Ray Tune over 180 trials per algorithm-asset pair, with early stopping and model selection based on validation Sharpe ratio. On the CLEF Task 3 test set, DDPG achieves the strongest overall performance. DQL was selected a priori for the live endpoint because it obtained the highest validation Sharpe ratio, with selection performed without access to the test period. For TSLA, DDPG and DQL achieve cumulative returns of 54.96% and 52.62%, respectively, compared with 16.45% for buy-and-hold. For BTC, DDPG achieves a positive return of 1.58% while buy-and-hold declines by -34.27%. The results also reveal a substantial validation-to-test generalization gap, highlighting the difficulty of transferring policies selected in bull-market conditions to a bear-market regime.

中文摘要翻译: 本文介绍了我们为CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab任务3开发的系统。该任务要求基于新闻和历史市场数据，每日对比特币（BTC）和特斯拉（TSLA）做出做多、做平或做空的交易决策。我们将该问题建模为离散动作马尔可夫决策过程，并比较了四种深度强化学习算法：策略梯度（PG）、近端策略优化（PPO）、深度Q学习（DQL）和深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）。智能体使用技术指标、周期日历编码以及由LLaMA 3.2 1B生成的每日新闻情感分数作为输入。为减少过拟合并使训练目标与跑赢买入并持有策略对齐，我们引入了基于超额市场回报的alpha奖励，并随机化回合起始日期。通过Ray Tune对每个算法-资产对进行180次试验的超参数优化，并基于验证集夏普比率实施早停和模型选择。在CLEF任务3测试集上，DDPG取得了最强的整体性能。DQL因在验证集上获得最高的夏普比率而被预先选定用于线上部署，且该选择过程未使用测试期数据。对于TSLA，DDPG和DQL分别实现了54.96%和52.62%的累积收益，而买入并持有策略的收益为16.45%。对于BTC，DDPG实现了1.58%的正收益，而买入并持有策略亏损34.27%。实验结果还揭示了显著的验证集到测试集泛化差距，突显了将在牛市场景下选出的策略迁移到熊市环境中的困难。

</details>


### [133] [Revisiting data-driven dynamic security assessment with a tabular foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16031)
*Olayiwola Arowolo,Maosheng Yang,Jochen Cremer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文学习实现数据驱动的预故障动态安全评估，无需为每个预想事故单独训练模型，仅需极少量标注样本即可达到高精度，并能泛化到未见过的预想事故。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据驱动的预故障动态安全评估方法存在两个局限：一是需要大量标注数据库，且需为每个预想事故单独训练、调优和维护一个模型；二是训练好的模型对未见过的预想事故泛化能力差。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 采用表格基础模型（TFM），通过上下文学习（in-context learning）评估系统稳定性，无需重新训练或超参数优化。使用电气距离坐标（EDC）作为连续特征，并研究其在不同情况下对泛化能力的影响，通过少量标注样本提升模型对未见预想事故的泛化性能。

Result: 在IEEE 68节点系统上，单个TFM仅需每个预想事故120个标注样本（比传统方法少两个数量级）即可达到约90%的平均Macro F1分数，无需任何模型重训练或超参数调优。对于未见过的预想事故，仅需10个新预想事故的标注样本配合EDC编码，即可匹敌最佳可迁移学习oracle模型。

Conclusion: 该初步研究为开发和部署用于电力系统运行的基础模型铺平了道路，表明TFM可有效应用于多个运行任务，大幅降低对标注数据和模型维护的需求。

Original Abstract: Data-driven pre-fault dynamic security assessment (DSA) rapidly evaluates the dynamic risk of credible contingencies on a power system using machine learning. Existing approaches face two limitations. First, they require a large labelled database for training, with a separate model trained, tuned, and maintained for each contingency in a potentially long list of credible contingencies. Second, the trained models generalize poorly to unseen contingencies. This work addresses the limitations by using a tabular foundation model (TFM) that assesses stability through in-context learning, requiring no retraining or hyperparameter optimization. A single TFM can assess many contingencies at once, removing the need for one model per classifier. We also characterize when the use of electrical distance coordinates (EDC) as continuous features enables generalization of TFM to unseen contingencies and when they do not, demonstrating how a few labelled samples can reliably improve generalization. Through comprehensive case studies on the IEEE 68-bus system, we show that a single TFM attains an average Macro F1 score of about 90% with only 120 labelled samples per contingency, roughly two orders of magnitude fewer than conventionally assumed, without any model retraining or hyperparameter tuning. For new/unseen contingencies, we show that using just 10 labelled samples of the new contingency with EDC encoding matches the best achievable transfer learning oracle model, which requires fully labelled data and is not deployable in practice. Overall, this initial study paves the way towards developing and deploying foundation models for power system operations, with possible applications across multiple operational tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 数据驱动的预故障动态安全评估（DSA）利用机器学习快速评估电力系统中可信预想事故的动态风险。现有方法面临两个局限：首先，它们需要大量标注数据库进行训练，并且需要为潜在的长列表中的每个可信预想事故单独训练、调优和维护一个模型；其次，训练好的模型对未见过的预想事故泛化能力差。本文通过使用表格基础模型（TFM）来解决这些局限，该模型通过上下文学习评估稳定性，无需重新训练或超参数优化。单个TFM可以同时评估多个预想事故，消除了每个分类器需要一个模型的需求。我们还刻画了何时使用电气距离坐标（EDC）作为连续特征能使TFM泛化到未见过的预想事故，以及何时不能，并展示了少量标注样本如何可靠地提升泛化能力。通过在IEEE 68节点系统上的综合案例研究，我们证明单个TFM仅需每个预想事故120个标注样本（比传统假设少约两个数量级）即可达到约90%的平均Macro F1分数，无需任何模型重训练或超参数调优。对于新的/未见过的预想事故，我们表明仅需使用新预想事故的10个标注样本配合EDC编码，即可匹敌最佳的可迁移学习oracle模型，而后者需要完全标注的数据且在实际中不可部署。总体而言，这项初步研究为开发和部署用于电力系统运行的基础模型铺平了道路，并可能应用于多个运行任务。

</details>


### [134] [DELUGE: Towards Continental-Scale Daily Pluvial Flood Damage Prediction via Interpretable Conditioning on Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16050)
*Yuya Kawakami,Daniel Cayan,Dongyu Liu,Kwan-Liu Ma,Tom Corringham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DELUGE是一个多模态深度学习框架，用于美国全国范围~1公里分辨率的每日暴雨洪涝灾害损失预测，通过可解释的参数化模块设计优于多种基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 暴雨洪涝占美国NFIP索赔的45%，但现有方法存在分辨率粗糙、局限于区域范围或计算成本高的问题，缺乏适用于日常大陆尺度的高精度预测模型。

Method: 提出DELUGE多模态深度学习框架，包含水文气象分支中的值调制器和时间调制器两个参数化模块，结合地形描述符和AlphaEarth基础模型嵌入，实现架构级别的可解释性设计。

Result: 在空间块留出验证下，DELUGE在美元加权的PR-AUC指标上比调优后的Random Forest、XGBoost和LightGBM基线高出9%至30%，重点提升了罕见高成本索赔的预测性能。

Conclusion: DELUGE在每日全国尺度暴雨洪涝损失预测上优于传统机器学习方法，其可解释的条件调制方案可作为将基础模型嵌入集成到其他地理空间预测任务中的可迁移模式。

Original Abstract: Pluvial (rainfall-driven) flooding accounts for 45% of National Flood Insurance Program (NFIP) claims in the United States and is harder to predict than its riverine and coastal counterparts, with existing approaches limited to coarse resolution, regional domains, or computationally intensive process-based models unsuitable for daily continental-scale use. We present DELUGE, a multimodal deep learning framework for daily pluvial flood damage prediction at ~1 km resolution and national scale, trained on spatially and temporally corrected NFIP claims (2017-2022) and structured around the hazard, exposure, and vulnerability components of disaster risk. Rather than blanket coverage of the Conterminous United States (CONUS), we model the top 100 highest-claim 75 km cells, distributed nationwide and accounting for ~81% of total pluvial flood claims. Our architectural novelty is a pair of parametric modules in the hydrometeorology branch, a Value Modulator and a Temporal Modulator, conditioned on terrain descriptors and AlphaEarth foundation-model embeddings, that expose directly inspectable hydrological response parameters and provide architecture-level interpretability-by-design. Under a spatial block holdout, DELUGE outperforms tuned Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM baselines by 9% to 30% on a dollar-weighted area under the precision-recall curve (PR-AUC), a metric that emphasizes the rare, high-cost claims of greatest operational interest. Beyond DELUGE, we argue this interpretable conditioning scheme is a transferable pattern for integrating foundation-model embeddings into other geospatial prediction tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 暴雨（降雨驱动）洪涝占美国国家洪水保险计划（NFIP）索赔的45%，且比河洪和沿海洪涝更难预测。现有方法局限于粗分辨率、区域范围或计算密集、不适用于日常大陆尺度使用的过程模型。我们提出DELUGE，一个多模态深度学习框架，用于在~1公里分辨率和全国尺度上进行每日暴雨洪涝损失预测，该框架在经时空校正的NFIP索赔数据（2017-2022）上训练，并围绕灾害风险的危险性、暴露度和脆弱性组成部分构建。我们不采用对美国本土（CONUS）的全覆盖方式，而是建模索赔额最高的前100个75公里单元，这些单元分布在全国各地，约占暴雨洪涝总索赔的81%。我们的架构创新在于水文气象分支中的一对参数化模块——值调制器和时间调制器，它们以地形描述符和AlphaEarth基础模型嵌入为条件，暴露了可直接检查的水文响应参数，并提供了架构级别的可解释性设计。在空间块留出验证下，DELUGE在美元加权的精确率-召回率曲线下面积（PR-AUC）指标上比调优后的Random Forest、XGBoost和LightGBM基线高出9%至30%，该指标强调具有最大操作价值的罕见高成本索赔。超越DELUGE本身，我们认为这种可解释的条件调制方案是将基础模型嵌入集成到其他地理空间预测任务中的一种可迁移模式。

</details>


### [135] [When Model Merging Rivals Joint Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning: A Task-Vector Geometry Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16062)
*S. Aaron McClendon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了强化学习中模型合并（TIES、RAM+）与联合多任务训练的效果，发现在AppWorld基准上合并性能与联合训练统计上无显著差异。通过分析任务向量几何特性（近正交、支持集重叠但方向解耦），解释了为何不同合并方法表现相似——它们都退化为接近均匀平均。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 模型合并被宣传为联合多任务训练的替代方案，但在强化学习场景中，这种替代关系从未被严格验证——由于联合模型不可用才使用合并。论文旨在填补这一缺失的比较。

Method: 在AppWorld基准上使用LOOP训练Qwen3-8B的困难-1和困难-2专家模型，采用TIES和RAM+方法进行模型合并，并与在相同数据上联合训练的模型进行对比。同时测量专家任务向量的几何特性（余弦相似度、支持集重叠等），并设置随机初始化基线和相同运行上限进行校准。

Result: 在任务目标完成上，模型合并的性能与联合强化学习相当，且所有合并变体之间统计上无显著差异。任务向量接近正交（余弦相似度0.06-0.10），尽管支持集重叠约65%，方向和支撑集是解耦的，导致基于支撑集和符号的合并（RAM、TIES）退化为接近均匀平均。

Conclusion: 在强化学习设置中，当任务向量方向与支持集解耦时，不同模型合并方法的效果趋同，均退化为近均匀平均。这解释了为何在此场景下合并方法的选择不重要。

Original Abstract: Model merging is promoted as a substitute for joint multi-task training, yet in the reinforcement-learning setting this substitution is essentially never tested against the baseline it claims to replace: methods merge independently released agents precisely because a joint model is unavailable. We build the missing comparison. Training difficulty-1 and difficulty-2 Qwen3-8B specialists on the AppWorld agent benchmark with LOOP, we merge them (TIES, RAM+) and pit the result against a jointly trained model on the same data. On task-goal completion, merging matches joint RL -- and every merge variant is statistically indistinguishable. To explain why merge method does not matter here, we measure the geometry of the specialists' task vectors, which carries no task-sampling noise: they are near-orthogonal (cosine 0.06 - 0.10) despite ~65% support overlap, a small, shared direction that grows over training and that we calibrate against a random-init floor and a same-run ceiling to confirm it reflects learning, not the low-rank parameterization. Because direction and support are decoupled, support and sign-based merging (RAM, TIES) collapse to near-uniform averaging. We release all code and statistics.

中文摘要翻译: 模型合并被推崇为联合多任务训练的替代方案，但在强化学习环境中，这种替代关系几乎从未对照它所声称要取代的基线进行过测试：方法合并独立发布的智能体，正是因为联合模型不可用。我们构建了这一缺失的比较。在AppWorld智能体基准上，使用LOOP训练难度-1和难度-2的Qwen3-8B专家，将其合并（TIES、RAM+），并将结果与在相同数据上联合训练的模型进行对比。在任务目标完成上，合并的性能与联合强化学习相当——且所有合并变体在统计上无显著差异。为了解释为何合并方法在这里无关紧要，我们测量了专家任务向量的几何特性，该向量不携带任务采样噪声：它们接近正交（余弦相似度0.06-0.10），尽管支持集重叠约65%，存在一个小的、共享的方向，该方向随训练增长；我们通过随机初始化基线和相同运行上限对其进行校准，以确认它反映了学习过程，而非低秩参数化。由于方向与支持集是解耦的，基于支持集和符号的合并（RAM、TIES）退化为接近均匀平均。我们发布了所有代码和统计数据。

</details>


### [136] [Neural spectroscopy of AlphaFold2 reveals encoded protein conformational landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16087)
*Kaustav Mehta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaFold2的权重不仅是结构预测的工具，本身也是可分析的科学对象。通过引入“神经光谱学”（Neural Spectroscopy）方法，特别是缩放高斯卷积（Scaled Gaussian Convolution），对Evoformer的权重张量进行平滑和缩放扰动，揭示出训练好的模型中编码了物理上有意义的结构构象景观，且这些构象信息并非显式训练目标，而是作为预测静态结构任务的副产品涌现的。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统上AlphaFold2的9300万参数仅被视为从序列到结构的转换引擎，本文认为这些权重本身蕴含了蛋白质构象组织的学习编码，可以直接作为科学对象进行分析与表征，从而揭示模型从训练数据中学到的超越静态结构预测的结构约束信息。

Method: 提出神经光谱学方法，具体采用缩放高斯卷积（Scaled Gaussian Convolution）对Evoformer的权重张量进行高斯平滑并缩放，以扰动方式探查模型编码的构象景观。通过对比匹配功率的随机噪声控制实验，验证扰动产生的构象结构是否具有物理意义。

Result: 1) 对泛素，扰动后天然接触按数十年折叠实验建立的顺序断裂；2) 对KaiB，五个独立训练模型均一致显示扰动下无法恢复替代折叠构象；3) 对α-突触核蛋白，五个模型产生五种不同但连贯的构象景观，映射了训练信号确定表示的区域和未确定的区域；4) 匹配功率的噪声控制显示随机破坏产生的是碎片而非构象。

Conclusion: AlphaFold2的权重编码了结构约束信息，这些信息由进化和结构训练数据塑造，且超出了未扰动推理所揭示的内容。构象组织信息作为预测静态结构目标的副产品涌现。提出的神经光谱学方法为解读深度学习模型学到的生物物理知识提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: AlphaFold2's 93 million parameters, shaped by the evolutionary record of protein structure encoded in the Protein Data Bank and in sequence alignments, are conventionally treated only as machinery for converting sequence to structure. We propose they are also a scientific object that can be analyzed directly: a learned encoding of protein conformational organization that can be probed and characterized. By smoothing the Evoformer's weight tensors with a Gaussian convolution and scaling the result, we show that the trained model produces physically structured conformational landscapes. Under perturbation, ubiquitin's native contacts break in the order established by decades of folding experiments. For KaiB, five independently trained models agree that the alternative fold is not recovered under perturbation. For alpha-synuclein, five models produce five different but coherent landscapes, mapping where the training signal has determined the representation and where it has not. Matched-power noise controls confirm that random corruption of equal magnitude produces debris, not conformations. The model learned to predict static structures; the conformational organization visible under perturbation was not an explicit training target, suggesting it emerged as a byproduct of that objective. AlphaFold2's weights appear to encode structural constraints, shaped by evolutionary and structural training data, that extend beyond what unperturbed inference reveals. We call the approach of reading them neural spectroscopy, and Scaled Gaussian Convolution one such protocol.

中文摘要翻译: AlphaFold2的9300万个参数由蛋白质数据库中蛋白质结构的进化记录以及序列比对共同塑造，传统上仅被视为将序列转换为结构的机器。我们提出，这些参数本身也是一个可以直接分析的科学对象：一个可被探查和表征的蛋白质构象组织的学习编码。通过对Evoformer的权重张量进行高斯卷积平滑并缩放结果，我们证明训练后的模型产生了具有物理结构的构象景观。在扰动下，泛素的天然接触按照数十年折叠实验建立的顺序依次断裂。对于KaiB，五个独立训练的模型一致认为在扰动下无法恢复替代折叠构象。对于α-突触核蛋白，五个模型产生了五种不同但连贯的景观，刻画了训练信号已确定表示的区域和尚未确定的区域。匹配功率的噪声控制实验证实，等幅度的随机破坏产生的是碎片而非构象。该模型学习的是预测静态结构；而扰动下可见的构象组织并非显式训练目标，表明它是作为该目标的一个副产品涌现出来的。AlphaFold2的权重似乎编码了由进化和结构训练数据塑造的结构约束，其范围超出了未扰动推理所揭示的内容。我们将这种解读权重的方法称为神经光谱学，而缩放高斯卷积是其中一种具体实现方案。

</details>


### [137] [DADiff: Diffusion-Driven Cross-Domain Policy Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16090)
*Hanyang Chen,Anirudh Satheesh,Longchao Da,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DADiff，一种基于扩散模型的在线动态适应框架，通过生成轨迹偏差来估计源域与目标域之间的动态不匹配，并设计了奖励修正和数据选择两种变体来适应目标域策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 强化学习中跨域策略迁移面临源域与目标域动态不匹配的挑战，现有方法依赖域分类器、价值引导数据过滤或表示学习，本文从生成建模视角出发，利用扩散模型捕捉生成轨迹偏差以更直接地估计动态差异。

Method: 提出DADiff框架，利用扩散模型在下一状态生成过程中对比源域和目标域的生成轨迹，通过轨迹偏差估计动态不匹配；并开发了奖励修正和数据选择两种变体来适应目标域策略。同时提供了理论分析，证明策略在两域间的性能差异受生成轨迹偏差上界约束。

Result: 在多种偏移环境下的实验表明，DADiff在解决动态不匹配问题上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的生成轨迹偏差能有效估计动态不匹配，奖励修正和数据选择两种变体均能成功适应目标域，理论分析为方法的有效性提供了保障。

Original Abstract: Transferring policies across domains poses a vital challenge in reinforcement learning, due to the dynamics mismatch between the source and target domains. In this paper, we consider the setting of online dynamics adaptation, where policies are trained in the source domain with sufficient data, while only limited interactions with the target domain are allowed. There are a few existing works that address the dynamics mismatch by employing domain classifiers, value-guided data filtering, or representation learning. Instead, we study the domain adaptation problem from a generative modeling perspective. Specifically, we introduce DADiff, a diffusion-based framework that leverages the discrepancy between source and target domain generative trajectories in the generation process of the next state to estimate the dynamics mismatch. Both reward modification and data selection variants are developed to adapt the policy to the target domain. We also provide a theoretical analysis to show that the performance difference of a given policy between the two domains is bounded by the generative trajectory deviation. More discussions on the applicability of the variants and the connection between our theoretical analysis and the prior work are further provided. We conduct extensive experiments in environments with various shifts to validate the effectiveness of our method. The results demonstrate that our method provides superior performance compared to existing approaches, effectively addressing the dynamics mismatch. We provide the code of our method at https://github.com/hanyang-chen/DADiff-release

中文摘要翻译: 在强化学习中，由于源域和目标域之间的动态不匹配，跨域策略迁移是一项至关重要的挑战。本文考虑在线动态适应的设定，即在源域中使用充足数据训练策略，同时仅允许与目标域进行有限的交互。现有一些工作通过域分类器、价值引导数据过滤或表示学习来解决动态不匹配问题。相反，我们从生成建模的角度研究域适应问题。具体来说，我们引入了DADiff，一种基于扩散的框架，该框架利用源域和目标域生成轨迹在下一状态生成过程中的差异来估计动态不匹配。我们开发了奖励修正和数据选择两种变体，以将策略适应到目标域。我们还提供了理论分析，表明给定策略在两域之间的性能差异受生成轨迹偏差的上界约束。进一步讨论了两类变体的适用性以及我们的理论分析与先前工作之间的联系。我们在具有各种偏移的环境中进行大量实验，以验证我们方法的有效性。结果表明，我们的方法相比现有方法提供了优越的性能，有效解决了动态不匹配问题。我们的方法代码可在 https://github.com/hanyang-chen/DADiff-release 获取。

</details>


### [138] [Understanding Reasoning from Pretraining to Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16097)
*Jingyan Shen,Ang Li,Salman Rahman,Yifan Sun,Micah Goldblum,Matus Telgarsky,Pavel Izmailov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以国际象棋为受控实验平台，系统研究了从预训练到强化学习后训练的全流程中，预训练选择（模型规模、数据量）如何影响RL收益，以及RL对模型的具体作用机制。发现RL后性能可由预训练损失良好预测，RL奖励曲线斜率随预训练数据量近似线性提升，且RL并非简单锐化SFT策略——在简单难题上增强已有正确动作，在困难难题上挖掘SFT中近乎不存在的正确动作。这些规律在数学领域也得到验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练研究与预训练阶段相互割裂，两个基本问题尚未明确：(1)预训练选择（模型大小、数据）如何影响RL计算投入的回报；(2)RL实际上对模型做了什么。由于标准LLM设置中预训练语料庞大且不可控，难以归因行为变化来自预训练还是RL，且跨两阶段的系统计算规模扫描成本过高，亟需一个可控实验平台来研究这些问题。

Method: 以国际象棋作为受控推理测试平台，遵循标准LLM训练流程：(1)在人类棋谱上预训练5M到1B参数的语言模型；(2)在合成推理轨迹上进行监督微调(SFT)；(3)在具有可验证奖励的国际象棋谜题上运行RL。此外，在数学领域文本上训练1B模型进行迁移验证。

Result: (1)给定RL计算量下的RL后性能可由预训练损失良好预测；(2)RL奖励曲线的斜率随预训练数据量近似线性改善；(3)RL并非简单锐化SFT策略：对简单谜题，RL增强SFT已偏好的正确动作；对困难谜题，RL挖掘出SFT中几乎不存在的正确动作；(4)上述规律迁移至数学领域：预训练更久的检查点达到更高的RL后性能，且RL下改善更快。

Conclusion: 提供了预训练到RL接口的定量刻画，以及一个从预训练到后训练全流程中研究推理科学的受控实验平台。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to improving large language models (LLMs) on complex reasoning tasks, yet RL post-training is largely studied in isolation from the pretraining that precedes it. As a result, two basic questions remain open: (1) how do pretraining choices (model size, data) shape the returns to RL compute, and (2) what does RL actually do to the model? These questions are difficult to study in the standard LLM setting: pretraining corpora are vast and uncontrolled, making it hard to attribute behaviors to pretraining versus RL, and systematic compute sweeps across both stages are prohibitively expensive. To address these challenges, we use chess as a controlled testbed for studying reasoning across the full pretraining-to-post-training pipeline. We follow the standard LLM training pipeline by pretraining language models from 5M to 1B parameters on human chess games, supervised fine-tuning on synthetic reasoning traces, and running RL on chess puzzles with verifiable rewards. Using this framework, we find that the post-RL performance at given RL compute level is well-predicted from the pretraining loss, and slope of the RL reward curves improves approximately linearly with the pretraining tokens. Beyond scaling, we find that RL does not simply sharpen the SFT policy: on easy puzzles it amplifies correct moves the SFT policy already preferred, while on hard puzzles it surfaces correct moves that were nearly absent under SFT. We further test whether our findings transfer beyond chess by training a 1B language model on math-domain text, where the same predictive pattern emerges: longer-pretrained checkpoints reach higher post-RL performance and improve faster under RL. In sum, we provide a quantitative account of the pretraining-to-RL interface and a controlled testbed for studying the science of reasoning across the full pretraining-to-post-training pipeline.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）已成为改进大型语言模型（LLM）在复杂推理任务上表现的核心手段，然而，RL后训练在很大程度上与其之前的预训练阶段相互割裂。因此，两个基本问题依然悬而未决：(1)预训练选择（模型规模、数据）如何塑造RL计算投入的回报；(2)RL实际上对模型做了什么？这些问题在标准LLM设置中难以研究：预训练语料庞大且无法控制，使得行为变化难以归因于预训练还是RL，且跨两阶段的系统性计算规模扫描代价过高。为应对这些挑战，我们使用国际象棋作为受控测试平台，研究从预训练到后训练的完整推理流程。我们遵循标准LLM训练流程，在人类棋谱上预训练5M到1B参数的语言模型，在合成推理轨迹上进行监督微调（SFT），并在具有可验证奖励的国际象棋谜题上运行RL。利用这一框架，我们发现：给定RL计算量下的RL后性能可由预训练损失良好预测，且RL奖励曲线的斜率随预训练数据量近似线性改善。超出规模效应之外，我们发现RL并非简单锐化SFT策略：在简单谜题上，它增强了SFT已偏好的正确动作；而在困难谜题上，它挖掘出了SFT中几乎不存在的正确动作。我们进一步测试了这些发现是否超越国际象棋领域，在数学领域文本上训练1B语言模型，观察到相同的预测模式：预训练更久的检查点达到更高的RL后性能，并在RL下改善更快。总之，我们提供了预训练到RL接口的定量刻画，以及一个从预训练到后训练全流程中研究推理科学的受控实验平台。

</details>


### [139] [The Honest Quorum Problem: Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance for Agentic Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16109)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对智能体基础设施和后确定性分布式系统的拜占庭容错新模型——认知拜占庭容错（EBFT），该模型扩展了传统拜占庭容错框架，通过引入两个独立的置信度索引参数（e_δ和u_ε）来量化非拜占庭节点因推理错误导致的语义无效背书和活性退化问题，揭示了协议合规但推理错误的智能体验证者可能形成"诚实话语"并对无效状态转换达成共识的"诚实话语问题"。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统SMR和BFT共识协议假设协议合规的参与者不会产生语义无效的决策，但智能体验证者因其推理能力受限（如共享模型权重、训练分布、提示或工具链），即使遵守协议也可能因推理错误而背书无效的状态转换。这种被称为"认知故障"的失败模式无法被现有拜占庭容错模型所覆盖，因此需要新的容错理论框架。

Method: 定义了EBFT容错模型，在传统拜占庭故障界基础上引入两个置信度索引参数：e_δ（拜占庭集合外的一致性无效背书的数量上界）和u_ε（降低活性的不可用验证者支持数量上界）。推导了语义有效性、共识一致性、活性和可行阈值选择的法定人数-阈值条件，并提出了估计这些预算的校准方法论。

Result: 证明了仅靠传统一致性协议无法保证语义有效性或执行安全性；名义上不同的智能体只有在能显著减少无效背书或不可用支持的上尾集中度时，才能提高容错能力；推导了EBFT模型下保证语义有效性和活性的法定人数条件。

Conclusion: 协议合规（"诚实"）不等于语义正确，智能体验证者的认知故障会导致语义无效的共识形成；EBFT模型提供了一种独立量化语义安全风险和活性退化程度的理论框架；增加名义上不同的智能体并非总能提升容错能力，必须满足可测量的尾部集中度降低条件。

Original Abstract: State machine replication (SMR) and Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus guarantee agreement despite a bounded number of arbitrary, colluding faulty participants. However, these guarantees rely on participants outside this set correctly executing the protocol's transition semantics. Agentic validators expose a weaker boundary: an authenticated, responsive, non-equivocating, and protocol-compliant reasoning participant may still endorse a semantically invalid transition due to reasoning errors.
  We call this failure mode an epistemic fault, and the collective phenomenon the Honest Quorum Problem (where "honest" means protocol-compliant, not semantically correct). Such a quorum can satisfy ordinary checks while forming a certificate for an invalid transition. Thus, agreement alone does not guarantee semantic validity or execution safety. Furthermore, because agentic validators often share model weights, training distributions, prompts, or toolchains, they are highly susceptible to correlated epistemic faults.
  We define Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance (EBFT), a fault-tolerance model for agentic infrastructure and post-deterministic distributed systems. EBFT augments the conventional Byzantine fault bound with two separate, confidence-indexed quantities: $e_δ$ bounds coherent invalid endorsements outside the Byzantine set, and $u_ε$ bounds unusable validator support that degrades liveness. These quantities characterize semantic safety risk and liveness degradation independently. We derive quorum-threshold conditions for semantic validity, consensus agreement, liveness, and feasible threshold selection, and outline a calibration methodology for estimating these budgets. We show that adding nominally distinct agents improves fault tolerance only when it measurably reduces the upper-tail concentration of invalid endorsements or unusable support.

中文摘要翻译: 状态机复制（SMR）和拜占庭容错（BFT）共识协议保证了在有界数量的任意、共谋故障参与者存在的情况下达成一致性。然而，这些保证依赖于该集合之外的参与者正确执行协议的转换语义。智能体验证者暴露了一个更弱的边界：一个经过认证的、响应式的、非共谋的、且遵守协议的推理参与者，仍可能因推理错误而支持一个语义无效的转换。我们将这种失败模式称为认知故障，并将这种集体现象称为"诚实话语问题"（其中"诚实"指遵守协议，而非语义正确）。这样一个话语可以满足常规检查的同时，为无效转换形成认证证书。因此，仅靠一致性并不能保证语义有效性或执行安全性。此外，由于智能体验证者通常共享模型权重、训练分布、提示或工具链，它们极易受到关联性认知故障的影响。我们定义了认知拜占庭容错（EBFT），一种针对智能体基础设施和后确定性分布式系统的容错模型。EBFT用两个独立的、基于置信度索引的量化参数增强了传统的拜占庭故障界：e_δ约束拜占庭集合外的一致性无效背书，u_ε约束降低活性的不可用验证者支持。这些参数独立地刻画了语义安全风险和活性退化程度。我们推导了语义有效性、共识一致性、活性和可行阈值选择的法定人数-阈值条件，并概述了估算这些预算的校准方法论。我们证明，只有当添加名义上不同的智能体能够可测量地减少无效背书或不可用支持的上尾集中度时，才能提高容错能力。

</details>


### [140] [When Do Multi-Agent Systems Help? An Information Bottleneck Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16133)
*Wendi Yu,Lianhao Zhou,Xiangjue Dong,Sai Sudarshan Barath,Declan Staunton,Byung-Jun Yoon,Xiaoning Qian,James Caverlee,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息瓶颈视角揭示了多智能体系统（MAS）相对于单智能体系统（SAS）的优势条件：当中继带宽有限时，MAS通过上下文压缩在效率与信息损失之间取得平衡，这种权衡由参数β控制，实验验证了模型能力越强，MAS优势越小甚至逆转。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究表明多智能体系统（MAS）相比单智能体系统（SAS）的优势在不同设置下表现不一致，缺乏统一的理论解释来阐明二者差异的根本原因。

Method: 提出信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）视角分析MAS与SAS的差异。核心观察：SAS将所有推理轨迹累积在共享上下文中，而MAS使用隔离的局部上下文通过有限带宽的中继消息连接。通过形式化参数β控制压缩与信息损失的权衡，并在5个基准、3种模型规模上开展18项对照实验验证理论。

Result: 1) 无限中继带宽下MAS可模拟SAS；2) MAS的优势源于有限带宽下的上下文压缩，减少冗余可提升效率但可能损失任务相关信息；3) 当中继接近足够时MAS一致有效，尤其对弱模型；4) 当信息损失严重时MAS优势缩小甚至逆转，强模型从冗余中获益更多，压缩带来的收益有限。

Conclusion: 多智能体设计本质上是一个信息瓶颈优化问题，该视角解释了有界的中继通信何时有助于或有害于系统性能。

Original Abstract: LLM powered multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for complex tasks. However, their advantages over single-agent systems (SAS) remain unclear, with performance varying inconsistently across settings. Here, we provide an information bottleneck perspective on elucidating the differences between MAS and SAS. Specifically, our key observation is that a SAS accumulates its full reasoning trace in one shared context, while a MAS uses isolated local contexts connected by bounded relay messages. We show that, under infinite relay bandwidth, any SAS can be simulated by a MAS that transmits the full upstream context. Thus, the nontrivial advantage of MAS arises under bounded relays, where compression introduces a fundamental trade-off: reducing redundant context can improve efficiency, but may also incur loss of task-relevant information. We formalize this trade-off as an information bottleneck controlled by an effective parameter $β$, which captures how the balance shifts with model capability, and shows that MAS gains arise when context reduction outweighs relay information loss. We conduct 18 controlled experiments across five benchmarks and three model scales to validate our theoretical studies. We observe that MAS consistently helps when relays are near-sufficient, especially for weaker models. In contrast, MAS gains shrink or reverse when relays incur information loss, especially for stronger models that can already extract useful information from redundant context and thus gain little from compression. Our study shows that multi-agent design is fundamentally an information-bottleneck optimization problem. This perspective explains when bounded inter-agent communication helps or hurts.

中文摘要翻译: 以大型语言模型为动力的多智能体系统（MAS）已成为处理复杂任务的一种有前景的范式。然而，相比单智能体系统（SAS），它们的优势仍不明确，其性能在不同设置下表现不一致。本文从信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）视角出发，阐明MAS与SAS之间的差异。具体而言，我们的关键观察是：单智能体系统将其完整的推理轨迹累积在一个共享上下文中，而多智能体系统则使用隔离的局部上下文，通过有界的中继消息进行连接。我们证明，在无限中继带宽下，任何SAS都可以通过传输完整上游上下文的MAS来模拟。因此，MAS的非平凡优势出现在有界中继条件下，此时压缩引入了一个根本性的权衡：减少冗余上下文可以提升效率，但也可能导致任务相关信息的损失。我们将此权衡形式化为一个由有效参数β控制的信息瓶颈问题，该参数捕捉了平衡随模型能力变化的转移，并表明当上下文缩减带来的收益超过中继信息损失时，MAS的优势便得以显现。我们在五个基准测试和三种模型规模下开展了18项对照实验，以验证我们的理论研究。我们观察到，当中继接近足够时，MAS始终有帮助，尤其对较弱的模型而言。相反，当中继导致信息损失时，MAS的优势缩小甚至逆转，尤其是对于已经能够从冗余上下文中提取有用信息的强模型，它们从压缩中获益甚微。我们的研究表明，多智能体设计本质上是一个信息瓶颈优化问题。该视角解释了有界的智能体间通信何时有助于或有害于系统性能。

</details>


### [141] [Improving Improved Kernel PLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16138)
*Ole-Christian Galbo Engstrøm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对改进核偏最小二乘(IKPLS)算法中的两个共享步骤——X旋转矩阵R和Y载荷矩阵Q的计算进行加速，通过直接评估策略替代逐项累加以提升并行性，并首次发现Y载荷可从先前已算量中直接导出（将复杂度从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)），在CPU和GPU上分别实现约2倍和6倍的整次拟合加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: IKPLS算法1和2是目前最快的PLS校准算法之一，但其X旋转矩阵R和Y载荷矩阵Q的计算仍有加速空间。现有逐项累加方法并行性较差，而Y载荷计算成本较高（Θ(KM)），限制了在大规模数据和高性能计算场景下的效率。

Method: 1) 对R矩阵：将逐项累加替换为直接评估策略，所需乘法次数不变但并行性更好。2) 对Q矩阵：首次发现每个Y载荷可由同次迭代中已计算出的量（加上显式导出的常数）直接获得，从而将每个载荷的计算复杂度从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)（适用于M=1或2≤M<K的情况）。两种改进在数学上与原算法等价，保证输出相同的W、P、Q、R、T矩阵。

Result: 在NumPy（CPU）和JAX（GPU）上的基准测试表明：孤立步骤的加速可达两个数量级；整次拟合的加速在CPU上约为2倍，在GPU上约为6倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两种改进方案在保证与原算法输出完全一致的前提下，显著提升了IKPLS算法的计算效率，尤其在GPU上表现优异。相关实现已集成到免费开源的Python包ikpls中。

Original Abstract: Improved Kernel Partial Least Squares (IKPLS) algorithms 1 and 2 are among the fastest PLS calibration algorithms. This article focuses on two shared steps, the computation of the $\mathbf{X}$ rotations, $\mathbf{R}$, and the $\mathbf{Y}$ loadings, $\mathbf{Q}$, and accelerates both. For $\mathbf{R}$, term-by-term accumulation is replaced by a direct evaluation strategy that requires the same number of multiplications but parallelizes better on modern hardware. For $\mathbf{Q}$, I identify - to the best of my knowledge, for the first time - equivalences showing that each $\mathbf{Y}$ loading is obtainable, up to explicitly derived constants, from quantities already computed earlier in the same iteration, and I exploit them in IKPLS to reduce the cost of each loading from $Θ\left(KM\right)$ to $Θ\left(M\right)$ operations whenever $M = 1$ or $2 \leq M < K$, with $K$ predictor variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{X}$) and $M$ response variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{Y}$). Both improvements provably yield exactly the same $\mathbf{W}$, $\mathbf{P}$, $\mathbf{Q}$, $\mathbf{R}$, and $\mathbf{T}$ as the original algorithms. Benchmarks with NumPy (CPU) and JAX (GPU) show speedups of up to two orders of magnitude for the isolated steps and of approximately $2\times$ (CPU) and $6\times$ (GPU) for entire fits. Both improvements are implemented in the free, open-source Python package \texttt{ikpls}.

中文摘要翻译: 改进核偏最小二乘（IKPLS）算法1和2是目前最快的PLS校准算法之一。本文聚焦于两个共享步骤——X旋转矩阵R的计算和Y载荷矩阵Q的计算，并对两者进行了加速。对于R，将逐项累加替换为直接评估策略，该策略所需乘法次数相同但在现代硬件上具有更好的并行性。对于Q，本文首次发现——据我所知——每个Y载荷均可由同一迭代中先前已计算出的量（加上显式导出的常数）直接得到，并在IKPLS中利用这一发现将每个载荷的计算成本从Θ(KM)降低至Θ(M)，适用于M=1或2≤M<K的情况，其中K为预测变量数（X的列数），M为响应变量数（Y的列数）。两种改进均可证明产生与原算法完全相同的W、P、Q、R和T矩阵。在NumPy（CPU）和JAX（GPU）上的基准测试显示，孤立步骤的加速可达两个数量级，整次拟合在CPU上加速约2倍，在GPU上加速约6倍。两种改进均已实现并集成到免费开源的Python包ikpls中。

</details>


### [142] [PRISA: Proactive Infrastructure LiDAR Framework for Intersection Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16156)
*Tam Bang,Hussam Abubakr,Emiliano de la Garza Villarreal,Truc Phuong Nguyen,Austin Harris,Toru Hirano,Mina Sartipi,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG
