TL;DR: PRISA 是一个模块化路侧激光雷达框架，通过隐私保护、低光鲁棒的传感器实现长期交通监测和边缘端实时风险检测。它包含感知层和即插即用风险评估模块，后者能自动从感知输出中整理训练数据训练轨迹预测模型，并利用 TTC 和 PPET 进行纵向与交叉冲突评估。在公共数据集和真实路口部署验证，PPET 评估端到端延迟仅 194 毫秒，满足实时性要求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 城市交叉口是道路网络中最危险区域之一，特别是对行人、自行车等弱势道路使用者。多智能体交互的复杂性需要连续、实时的监控系统来预测冲突并防止事故发生。现有方法缺乏自动化的训练数据整理机制和轻量级、低延迟的边缘部署方案。

Method: PRISA 由两层组成：1) 传感与感知层，使用隐私保护、低光鲁棒的路侧 LiDAR；2) 即插即用风险评估模块，该模块自动从累积感知输出中整理站点特定的训练数据，无需人工标注即可训练轨迹预测模型，然后部署该模型进行连续运动预测和双重代理安全评估：TTC 用于纵向冲突，PPET 用于交叉和涉及弱势道路使用者的交互。

Result: 在 R-LiViT 公共数据集和田纳西州查塔努加实际信号交叉口的 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor 上部署评估。PPET 评估在 2.4 秒预测范围内端到端延迟为 194 毫秒，TTC 检测和感知均保持在实时约束内，证明了多智能体交叉口主动安全监控的实用性。

Conclusion: PRISA 框架在实际路口部署中展现出良好的实时性能和实用性，能够有效支持多智能体交叉口的主动安全监控。其模块化设计和自动化数据整理机制降低了部署门槛，为智慧交通基础设施提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Urban intersections are among the most hazardous locations in road networks, posing significant risks to vehicles and vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as pedestrians and cyclists. The complexity of multi-agent interactions demands continuous, real-time monitoring systems capable of anticipating conflicts before they escalate into crashes. We present PRISA, a modular infrastructure LiDAR framework leveraging privacy-preserving, low-light-robust roadside sensors for long-term traffic observation and real-time risk detection at the edge. The framework comprises two core components: a sensing and perception layer and a plug-and-play risk assessment module. The latter automatically curates site-specific training data from accumulated perception outputs to train a trajectory prediction model without manual annotation. It then deploys the trained model for continuous motion forecasting and dual surrogate safety evaluation, using Time-to-Collision (TTC) for longitudinal conflicts and Predicted Post-Encroachment Time (PPET) for crossing and VRU-involved interactions. PRISA is evaluated on the public R-LiViT dataset and deployed on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor at a live signalized intersection in Chattanooga, Tennessee. PPET-based assessment operates at 194~ms end-to-end latency over a 2.4-second predictive horizon, with TTC-based detection and perception remaining within real-time constraints, demonstrating practical feasibility for proactive multi-agent intersection safety monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 城市交叉路口是道路网络中最危险的地点之一，对车辆和行人、骑行者等弱势道路使用者构成重大风险。多智能体交互的复杂性要求具备连续、实时的监控系统，该系统能够在冲突升级为碰撞之前预测冲突。我们提出 PRISA——一种模块化的基础设施激光雷达框架，利用隐私保护、低光鲁棒的路侧传感器进行长期交通观测和边缘端实时风险检测。该框架包含两个核心组件：传感与感知层，以及一个即插即用的风险评估模块。后者从累积的感知输出中自动整理站点特定的训练数据，无需人工标注即可训练轨迹预测模型。随后，该模块部署训练好的模型进行连续运动预测和双重代理安全评估，使用碰撞时间（TTC）评估纵向冲突，使用预测后侵占时间（PPET）评估交叉和涉及弱势道路使用者的交互。PRISA 在公开的 R-LiViT 数据集上进行了评估，并在田纳西州查塔努加的一个实际信号交叉口部署于 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor 平台。在 2.4 秒的预测范围内，基于 PPET 的评估端到端延迟为 194 毫秒，基于 TTC 的检测和感知均保持在实时约束内，证明了 PRISA 在多智能体交叉口主动安全监控中的实际可行性。

</details>


### [143] [Behaviour-Conditioned Neural Processes for Adaptive Residential Short-Term Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16168)
*Ramin Soleimani,Andrea Visentin,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种行为条件化注意力神经过程框架，将推断出的行为结构嵌入概率预测模型中，用于居民短期负荷预测，在SGSC数据集上相比基线方法实现了MAE降低7.9%、CRPS降低6.9%的效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 居民短期负荷预测因家庭用电异质性、时序变化性和多样化行为模式而具有挑战性。现有方法仅将行为结构作为外部分组信号，未嵌入预测机制内部，本文旨在探索能否将推断的行为结构直接嵌入神经过程概率模型的预测机制中。

Method: 提出行为条件化注意力神经过程框架：将每个负荷曲线视为一个预测任务，用离散潜变量表示行为结构（从可用上下文中推断），用于条件化解码器；连续潜变量捕捉异质曲线间的共享功能不确定性。训练时利用聚类信息提供弱监督，测试时仅依赖上下文推断的类别分布进行条件化。

Result: 在SGSC数据集上，使用用户不相交划分、可变上下文长度和多步预测。所提变体在所有预测范围和上下文设置下均优于ANP基线，在有限上下文中增益最大。最佳变体相比ANP平均MAE降低7.9%、CRPS降低6.9%；与固定窗口基线相比，所有评估范围下RMSE更低，MAE相当。

Conclusion: 所提方法支持跨异质家庭、上下文和预测范围的单模型、不确定性感知预测，验证了将行为结构嵌入神经过程预测机制的有效性。

Original Abstract: Residential short-term load forecasting (STLF) is challenging because household demand is heterogeneous, temporally variable, and shaped by diverse behavioural routines. This work investigates whether inferred behavioural structure can be embedded within the forecasting mechanism of a Neural Process-based probabilistic model, rather than used only as an external grouping signal, for context-conditioned residential STLF. We propose a behaviour-conditioned Attentive Neural Process framework that treats each load profile as a forecasting task. Behavioural structure is represented by a discrete latent variable inferred from the available context and used for behaviour-conditioned decoder conditioning, while a continuous latent variable captures shared functional uncertainty across heterogeneous profiles. To enable conditioning without ground-truth behavioural labels, clustering-derived information provides weak supervision during training, whereas test-time conditioning relies only on context-inferred class distributions. Experiments on the Smart Grid, Smart City (SGSC) dataset use user-disjoint train/validation/test splits, variable context lengths, and multi-step forecast horizons, with comparisons against a label-agnostic ANP baseline and fixed-window deterministic STLF baselines. The proposed variants improve MAE and CRPS over ANP across horizons and context settings, with the largest gains under limited context. The best-performing variant achieves average reductions of 7.9% in MAE and 6.9% in CRPS relative to ANP. Compared with fixed-window baselines, this variant achieves lower RMSE across all evaluated horizons while maintaining competitive MAE, suggesting fewer large prediction deviations under heterogeneous consumption patterns. These results support single-model, uncertainty-aware forecasting across heterogeneous households, contexts, and horizons.

中文摘要翻译: 居民短期负荷预测具有挑战性，因为家庭用电需求是异质的、时序变化的，并受多样化行为模式的影响。本文研究推断出的行为结构能否嵌入基于神经过程的概率模型的预测机制中（而非仅作为外部分组信号），用于上下文条件化的居民短期负荷预测。我们提出了一个行为条件化注意力神经过程框架，将每个负荷曲线视为一个预测任务。行为结构由从可用上下文中推断出的离散潜变量表示，用于行为条件化解码器条件化，而连续潜变量则捕捉异质曲线间的共享功能不确定性。为实现在无真实行为标签的情况下进行条件化，聚类派生信息在训练期间提供弱监督，而测试时的条件化仅依赖于上下文推断的类别分布。在智能电网、智慧城市数据集上的实验采用用户不相交的训练/验证/测试划分、可变上下文长度和多步预测范围，并与标签无关的ANP基线及固定窗口确定性短期负荷预测基线进行对比。所提变体在各项预测范围和上下文设置下均改善了ANP的MAE和CRPS，在有限上下文下增益最大。最佳变体相比ANP平均MAE降低7.9%、CRPS降低6.9%。与固定窗口基线相比，该变体在所有评估范围下均实现了更低的RMSE，同时保持具有竞争力的MAE，表明在异质用电模式下减少了较大的预测偏差。这些结果支持跨异质家庭、上下文和预测范围进行单模型、不确定性感知预测。

</details>


### [144] [When Does Muon Help Agentic Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16169)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Zihe Huang,Ziqi Zhou,Qianshan Wei,Xuan Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统探索了Muon优化器在稀疏奖励智能体强化学习后训练中的应用，发现在ALFWorld任务上，Muon相比AdamW能带来显著性能提升（最高+88%），但其效果高度依赖于优势估计器和学习率的选择。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Muon在大规模预训练中已被证明可与AdamW竞争，但其在强化学习后训练，特别是稀疏奖励的智能体强化学习中的价值尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在ALFWorld任务上，使用Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct模型，在Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO)框架下，对隐藏权重矩阵应用Muon优化器（而非AdamW），并与AdamW进行单种子匹配对比实验。研究了不同优势估计器（GRPO、GraphGPO）和学习率（3e-5、1e-5）下的表现。

Result: 1) GiGPO下，Muon仅作用于隐藏权重矩阵使最终窗口验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%），高学习率的AdamW控制组无法保持更新后成功率；2) 在3e-5学习率下，Muon将GRPO从0.161提升至0.268，而GraphGPO的后期窗口差距在饱和时缩小；3) 在1e-5学习率下，GraphGPO Muon达到0.901，归一化验证AUC从0.399提升至0.556，达到0.5和0.75成功率分别提前30和60次更新。

Conclusion: Muon能够使智能体强化学习受益，这启发我们需要联合研究策略优化器、优势估计器和学习率的选择。多种子实验和跨任务验证仍是未来有待开展的工作。

Original Abstract: Muon is competitive with AdamW in large-scale pre-training, but its value for reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training remains unclear. We study vanilla Muon in sparse-reward agentic RL through matched single-seed comparisons with AdamW on ALFWorld using Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. Under Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO), applying Muon only to hidden weight matrices raises final-window validation success from 0.290 to 0.546 (+88%); high-rate AdamW controls retain no post-update success. The effect depends on the advantage estimator and learning rate. At 3e-5, Muon improves GRPO from 0.161 to 0.268, whereas GraphGPO's late-window gap narrows near saturation. At 1e-5, GraphGPO Muon reaches 0.901, raises normalized validation AUC from 0.399 to 0.556, and reaches 0.5 and 0.75 success 30 and 60 updates earlier, respectively. These exploratory results show that Muon can benefit agentic RL and motivate studying the policy optimizer, advantage estimator, and learning rate jointly. Multi-seed and cross-task validation remain open.

中文摘要翻译: Muon在大规模预训练中可与AdamW竞争，但其在强化学习后训练中的价值仍不明确。本文通过使用Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct模型在ALFWorld上进行的单种子匹配对比实验，研究了稀疏奖励智能体强化学习中vanilla Muon的表现。在组内组策略优化（GiGPO）下，仅对隐藏权重矩阵应用Muon可将最终窗口验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（提升88%）；高学习率的AdamW控制组无法保持更新后的成功率。该效果依赖于优势估计器和学习率。在3e-5学习率下，Muon将GRPO从0.161提升至0.268，而GraphGPO的后期窗口差距在接近饱和时缩小。在1e-5学习率下，GraphGPO Muon达到0.901，归一化验证AUC从0.399提升至0.556，达到0.5和0.75成功率分别提前30和60次更新。这些探索性结果表明Muon能够使智能体强化学习受益，并启发我们将策略优化器、优势估计器和学习率联合进行研究。多种子实验和跨任务验证仍有待开展。

</details>


### [145] [Physics-enhanced reinforcement learning for real-time optimal control of dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16177)
*Matteo Tomasetto,Nicolò Botteghi,Gabriele Bruni,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理增强强化学习（PEARL）范式，通过演员-伴随算法结合自动微分与伴随灵敏度，显著提升高维参数动力系统控制中的样本效率与泛化能力，在非稳态流场导航任务中超越现有RL算法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统RL算法样本效率低，在高维状态和动作空间中面临维度灾难，难以应用于传感器和执行器稀疏的复杂动力系统控制问题。

Method: 提出PEARL范式，采用演员-伴随（actor-adjoint）算法：短时域内利用自动微分计算策略梯度，长时域未来回报通过神经网络近似并利用伴随灵敏度计算梯度，从而减少环境交互次数并缓解长期梯度不稳定问题。

Result: 在非稳态流场中的两个参数化导航问题上，PEARL：(i) 超越最先进RL算法；(ii) 样本效率高；(iii) 在多种场景下具备泛化能力；(iv) 无需低维状态表示或多智能体策略即可扩展到高维状态和动作空间。

Conclusion: PEARL通过融合物理信息与RL，有效解决了高维参数动力系统控制中的样本效率与可扩展性问题，为RL在复杂物理系统中的应用提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has recently emerged as a promising feedback control strategy for nonlinear and complex dynamical systems. However, RL algorithms are sample inefficient and require a large number of interaction with the environment to synthesize optimal control strategies. Consequently, applications of RL are typically limited to sparse sensors and actuators due to the curse of dimensionality entailed by the exploration-exploitation dilemma in high-dimensional spaces. In this work, we bridge RL and traditional optimal control for dynamical system with a novel Physics-EnhAnced Reinforcement Learning (PEARL) paradigm tailored to the control of high-dimensional and parametric dynamical systems, exploiting the differentibility of their dynamics. Specifically, PEARL employs an actor-adjoint algorithm that leverages automatic differentiation to compute policy gradients over short horizons and adjoint-based sensitivities of future returns approximated via neural networks, significantly reducing the number of environment interactions, while mitigating long-term gradient instabilities. Through two challenging parametric navigation problems in unsteady flows, we show that PEARL (i) effectively exploits differentiable environments to outperform state-of-the-art RL algorithms, (ii) is sample efficient, thanks to the physics-guided policy learning, (iii) generalizes across multiple scenarios, which is crucial when dealing with parametric systems, and (iv) enables scaling RL to high-dimensional state and action spaces, without requiring low-dimensional state representations or multi-agent strategies.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）最近作为一种有前景的非线性复杂动力系统反馈控制策略而兴起。然而，RL算法样本效率低下，需要与环境进行大量交互才能合成最优控制策略。因此，由于高维空间中探索-利用困境所带来的维度灾难，RL的应用通常局限于稀疏的传感器和执行器场景。在本工作中，我们通过一种新颖的物理增强强化学习（PEARL）范式，将RL与传统最优控制相结合，专门针对高维和参数化动力系统的控制问题，利用其动力学的可微性。具体来说，PEARL采用一种演员-伴随算法，该算法利用自动微分在短时域上计算策略梯度，并通过神经网络近似未来回报的伴随灵敏度，显著减少了环境交互次数，同时缓解了长期梯度不稳定性。通过在非稳态流场中的两个具有挑战性的参数化导航问题，我们展示了PEARL：(i) 有效利用可微环境，超越了最先进的RL算法；(ii) 得益于物理引导的策略学习，具有样本高效性；(iii) 在多种场景下具备泛化能力，这对处理参数化系统至关重要；(iv) 能够将RL扩展到高维状态和动作空间，无需低维状态表示或多智能体策略。

</details>


### [146] [A Blueprint for Equilibrium-Based Differentiable Continuous-Variable Thermodynamic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16183)
*Owen Lockwood,Jérémy Béjanin,Joost Bus,Christopher Chamberland,Patrick Huembeli,Frank Schäfer,Guillaume Verdon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于热力学计算范式的节能硬件蓝图，利用可调能量势的朗之万动力学实现随机模拟，构建并训练基于能量的机器学习模型，并通过超导随机模拟电路实验验证了其可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机器学习工作负载对能耗和延迟的需求日益增长，传统计算架构难以满足，亟需探索新的物理计算范式以实现节能高效的概率计算。

Method: 利用物理硬件中可调能量势实现的朗之万动力学随机过程，构建硬件原生的基于能量的模型；结合概率图模型框架，在该热力学范式下构建和训练主流机器学习模型。

Result: 基于理论分析和数值研究，评估了不同模型在热力学范式下的运行时间和能耗；初步实验实现了热噪声驱动的超导随机模拟电路。

Conclusion: 研究结果勾勒了一条通往节能热力学硬件用于概率机器学习的可行路径。

Original Abstract: To address the escalating energy and latency demands of machine-learning workloads, we introduce a blueprint for an energy-efficient and fast thermodynamic computing stack that leverages stochastic analog processes in physical hardware. In this work, we focus on energy-based thermodynamic computing where the stochastic process is well described by Langevin dynamics with tunable energy potentials. The implementation of such potentials in physical hardware enables us to generate and sample from basic parameterized energy-based models. We demonstrate how to construct and train popular classes of machine learning models based on these hardware-native energy-based models, using the framework of probabilistic graphical models. We analyze the runtime and energy consumption of different models in this thermodynamic paradigm based on theoretical considerations and numerical studies. As a preliminary experimental realization of such hardware, we present our stochastic analog superconducting circuits driven by thermal noise. Together, these results outline a path toward energy-efficient thermodynamic hardware for probabilistic machine learning.

中文摘要翻译: 为应对机器学习工作负载日益增长的能耗和延迟需求，我们提出了一种节能快速热力学计算堆栈的蓝图，该方案利用物理硬件中的随机模拟过程。本文聚焦于基于能量的热力学计算，其中随机过程由具有可调能量势的朗之万动力学很好地描述。在物理硬件中实现此类能量势使我们能够生成和采样基本的参数化基于能量的模型。我们展示了如何基于这些硬件原生的基于能量的模型，利用概率图模型框架构建和训练主流的机器学习模型。基于理论分析和数值研究，我们评估了不同模型在该热力学范式下的运行时间和能耗。作为此类硬件的初步实验实现，我们展示了热噪声驱动的随机模拟超导电路。这些结果共同勾勒出一条通往用于概率机器学习的节能热力学硬件的路径。

</details>


### [147] [PagedWeight: Efficient MoE LLM Serving with Dynamic Quality-Aware Weight Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16184)
*Yuchen Yang,Yifan Zhao,Anisha Dasgupta,Sasa Misailovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PagedWeight提出了一种运行时动态量化MoE模型权重的方法，在KV缓存密集型场景下平衡专家权重精度与KV缓存大小，显著提升内存效率和吞吐量，同时保持模型准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在KV缓存密集型服务场景中，MoE大语言模型的权重与不断增长的KV缓存之间存在GPU内存竞争，导致内存压力大、推理性能受限。

Method: 提出PagedWeight方法，在运行时动态量化MoE模型的权重，根据内存压力自适应调整专家权重的精度，从而在任务准确率、内存消耗和吞吐量/延迟之间进行有效的权衡。

Result: 相比现有量化基线，PagedWeight在FP16等效精度下实现高达72.0%的GPU内存节省和1.94倍的吞吐量提升；在相似内存预算下，量化质量提升高达39.3%，吞吐量损失最多仅4.1%。

Conclusion: PagedWeight能有效解决MoE在KV缓存密集型服务中的内存竞争问题，在不显著牺牲精度和吞吐量的前提下大幅提升内存效率和服务性能。

Original Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a popular class of large language models (LLMs), offering high efficiency and accuracy. However, in KV-cache-intensive serving scenarios, MoEs often exhibit a tension between the GPU memory requirements of the model weights and the growing KV cache. We propose PagedWeight, a novel management method for MoE LLM serving that dynamically quantizes MoE model's weights at runtime and balances expert-weight precision with the KV cache sizes. PagedWeight exposes and effectively navigates the complex tradeoff between the model's task accuracy, memory consumption, and throughput/latency. Across several memory-sensitive MoE serving scenarios, PagedWeight improves the quality-memory tradeoff over several existing quantization baselines. PagedWeight achieves FP16-equivalent accuracy with up to 72.0% GPU memory savings and 1.94$\times$ throughput improvement, and improves quality over quantization methods by up to 39.3% at a similar memory budget with at most 4.1% throughput loss.

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家模型（MoE）是一类流行的大语言模型（LLM），具有高效率和准确性的特点。然而，在KV缓存密集型服务场景中，MoE模型通常面临模型权重的GPU内存需求与不断增长的KV缓存之间的张力。我们提出PagedWeight，一种用于MoE LLM服务的新型管理方法，该方法在运行时动态量化MoE模型的权重，并平衡专家权重精度与KV缓存大小。PagedWeight揭示并有效导航了模型任务准确率、内存消耗和吞吐量/延迟之间的复杂权衡。在多个内存敏感的MoE服务场景中，PagedWeight相比于几种现有量化基线改善了质量-内存权衡。PagedWeight在FP16等效精度下实现了高达72.0%的GPU内存节省和1.94倍的吞吐量提升，并且在相似内存预算下，量化质量提升高达39.3%，吞吐量损失最多为4.1%。

</details>

### [148] [STSBench: A Large-Scale Dataset for Modeling Neuronal Activity in the Dorsal Stream of Primate Visual Cortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15631)
*Ethan B. Trepka,Ruobing Xia,Shude Zhu,Sharif Saleki,Danielle Abreu Lopes,Stephen J. Niño Cital,Konstantin F. Willeke,Mindy Kim,Tirin Moore*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了STSBench——一个大规模灵长类动物上颞沟（STS）单神经元记录数据集，用于建立背侧流神经响应编码模型，填补了当前 dorsal stream 建模研究因缺乏大规模数据而进展缓慢的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 目前卷积神经网络（CNN）在预测腹侧流神经响应上非常成功，但背侧流（dorsal stream）由于缺乏大规模数据集，类似建模研究严重不足。

Method: 构建了STSBench数据集：从猕猴观看数千个自然视频时采集超过2,000个上颞沟（STS）神经元的单神经元电生理记录，数据规模较现有背侧流数据集增大近50倍。

Result: 该数据集可用于背侧流神经元响应的编码模型基准测试，以及从神经活动重建视觉输入。

Conclusion: STSBench数据集有效填补了背侧流大规模神经数据缺失的空白，为 dorsal stream 编码模型研究提供了重要基准资源。

Original Abstract: The primate visual system is typically divided into two streams - the ventral stream, responsible for object recognition, and the dorsal stream, responsible for encoding spatial relations and motion. Recent studies have shown that convolutional neural networks (CNNs) pretrained on object recognition tasks are remarkably effective at predicting neuronal responses in the ventral stream, shedding light on the neural mechanisms underlying object recognition. However, similar models of the dorsal stream remain underdeveloped due to the lack of large scale datasets encompassing dorsal stream areas. To address this gap, we present STSBench, a dataset of large-scale, single neuron recordings from over 2,000 neurons in the superior temporal sulcus (STS), a nearly 50-fold increase over existing dorsal stream datasets, collected while Rhesus macaques viewed thousands of unique, natural videos. We show that our dataset can be used for benchmarking encoding models of dorsal stream neuronal responses and reconstructing visual input from neural activity.

中文摘要翻译: 灵长类视觉系统通常被划分为两条通路——负责物体识别的腹侧流和负责编码空间关系与运动的背侧流。近期研究表明，在物体识别任务上预训练的卷积神经网络能够极为有效地预测腹侧流的神经元响应，揭示了物体识别背后的神经机制。然而，由于缺乏覆盖背侧流脑区的大规模数据集，类似的背侧流模型仍发展不足。为填补这一空白，我们提出了STSBench——一个大规模、单神经元记录数据集，包含恒河猴在观看数千个独特自然视频时采集的上颞沟（STS）中超过2,000个神经元的活动记录，相比现有背侧流数据集增加了近50倍。我们证明该数据集可用于背侧流神经元响应编码模型的基准测试，以及从神经活动重建视觉输入。

</details>


### [149] [Toward a mechanistic understanding of inference in visual cortex and diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15693)
*Zeyu Yun,Alexander Belsten,Dasheng Bi,Zahra Kadkhodaie,Yubei Chen,Bruno A. Olshausen*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个等价于最小扩散模型的主视觉皮层(V1)感知推理模型，基于稀疏编码和非因子化先验，通过去噪得分匹配和隐式微分训练。该模型不仅展现出与标准扩散架构相当的去噪性能，还揭示了水平连接结构、层次化表征等神经科学机制，弥合了机器学习与神经科学之间的鸿沟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 既有扩散模型性能优异但机制不透明（黑箱），而神经科学中V1的感知推理机制尚不明确。作者希望构建一个兼具可解释性与高性能的模型，一方面揭示V1中水平连接的功能意义，另一方面阐明扩散模型内部工作机制。

Method: 基于稀疏编码框架，引入非因子化先验（无约束成对交互矩阵）扩展为标准稀疏编码推理，形成一般循环动力系统；使用去噪得分匹配目标和隐式微分高效训练循环动力学；在自然图像上训练后，将学习到的交互矩阵与V1浅层水平连接结构进行对比分析。

Result: (1) 学习到的交互矩阵镜像了V1浅层水平连接的结构——连接相似朝向偏好的神经元；(2) 在极端视觉模糊条件下恢复长轮廓等图像特征，去噪性能接近标准黑箱扩散架构；(3) Jacobian可直接分解为潜变量间交互矩阵，揭示了循环动力学如何对自然结构形变赋予高概率；(4) 大量潜变量与视觉输入断开连接，形成强制图像特征全局一致性的层次化表征。

Conclusion: 该模型桥接了神经科学与机器学习两个领域：为神经科学提供了关于循环神经电路在感知推理任务中功能连接的具体可检验假设；为机器学习阐明了扩散模型内部所学机制——如何从有限训练集生成无限新图像。

Original Abstract: We describe a model of perceptual inference in primary visual cortex (V1) equivalent to a minimal diffusion model whose function can be readily understood from its parameters. The model is based on sparse coding with a non-factorial prior over latent variables in the form of an unconstrained, pairwise interaction matrix, extending standard sparse coding inference to a general recurrent dynamical system. We efficiently train these recurrent dynamics using a denoising score-matching objective and implicit differentiation. After training on natural images, the learned interaction matrix mirrors the structure of horizontal connections in superficial layers of V1 that link neurons of similar orientation tuning. This model exhibits exceptionally good denoising performance, restoring image features such as extended contours amid extreme visual ambiguity, nearly matching the behavior of standard, black-box diffusion architectures in generalization regime. Owing to the model's simplicity, the network's Jacobian can be decomposed directly in terms of the interaction matrix between latent variables, revealing mechanistically how the recurrent dynamics assign high probability over a continuous family of natural structural deformations. Intriguingly, within this circuit, a large fraction of latent variables learn to disconnect from visual input altogether, essentially forming a hierarchical representation that appears to enforce global consistency among image features. Together, the model and results bridge two distinct domains: for neuroscience, it generates concrete, testable hypotheses regarding functional connectivity in recurrent neural circuits during perceptual inference tasks; for machine learning, it elucidates the internal mechanisms learned by diffusion models that allow them to generate infinitely many novel images from a finite training set.

中文摘要翻译: 我们描述了一个主视觉皮层(V1)中的感知推理模型，该模型等价于一个最小扩散模型，其功能可以通过参数直接理解。该模型基于稀疏编码，对潜变量采用非因子化先验——形式为无约束的成对交互矩阵——将标准稀疏编码推理扩展为通用的循环动力系统。我们使用去噪得分匹配目标和隐式微分高效训练这些循环动力学。在自然图像上训练后，学习到的交互矩阵镜像了V1浅层水平连接的结构——这些连接将相似朝向调谐的神经元联系在一起。该模型展现出异常出色的去噪性能，能在极端视觉模糊中恢复诸如延长轮廓等图像特征，在泛化状态下几乎媲美标准黑箱扩散架构的行为。得益于模型的简洁性，网络的雅可比矩阵可以直接分解为潜变量间交互矩阵，从而从机制上揭示了循环动力学如何对连续自然结构形变家族赋予高概率。耐人寻味的是，在该电路中，大量潜变量学会了完全与视觉输入断开连接，本质上形成了看似强制图像特征全局一致性的层次化表征。综合来看，该模型及研究结果桥接了两种不同领域：对于神经科学，它生成了关于循环神经电路在感知推理任务中功能连接的具体可检验假设；对于机器学习，它阐明了扩散模型所学的内部机制——使其能够从有限训练集中生成无限多新颖图像。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Large Language Model-Enhanced Multi-hop Parallel Image Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15297)
*Bingyan Xie,Jihong Park,Rui Mao,Longyu Zhou,Tianhao Liang,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种大语言模型增强的多跳并行图像语义通信框架（LLM-MHPSC），通过残差补偿链路、粗到细压缩方案、LLM驱动的残差传输优化器及自适应跳数选择策略，有效缓解多跳无线图像传输中的失真累积问题，在带宽开销轻微增加的情况下实现鲁棒图像传输，优于现有语义通信与传统方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统单跳语义通信方案在多跳无线传输场景下存在失真累积问题，而直接扩展至多跳场景会导致性能严重下降。现有方法缺乏对多跳失真积累的有效补偿机制。

Method: (1) 在每个中继跳部署额外的残差补偿链路以抵消累积失真；(2) 提出粗到细残差压缩方案，结合深度学习压缩器与自适应算术编码（AAC）以减少额外带宽开销；(3) 开发基于大语言模型的残差传输优化器（LLM-RTO），准确估计残差分布并实现信道状态与跳数感知的码率调整；(4) 提出自适应跳数选择策略，按需激活残差链路以平衡传输性能与计算成本。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM-MHPSC在带宽开销仅轻微增加的情况下，优于现有最先进的语义通信方案及传统传输方案，实现了鲁棒的多跳图像传输。

Conclusion: 该框架为将语义通信扩展到实际多跳应用场景提供了一种灵活且有效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes a large language model-enhanced multi-hop parallel image semantic communication (LLM-MHPSC) framework to mitigate distortion accumulation in multi-hop wireless image transmission. Unlike conventional single-hop semantic communication schemes, LLM-MHPSC deploys an extra residual compensation link at each hop to counteract accumulated distortions. To minimize additional bandwidth overhead, a coarse-to-fine residual compression scheme is designed by integrating a deep learning-based compressor with adaptive arithmetic coding (AAC). Furthermore, a large language model-based residual transmission optimizer (LLM-RTO) is developed to accurately estimate residual distributions and enable channel state and hop-aware rate adjustment, thereby improving residual compression efficiency under varying channel and hop conditions. An adaptive hop selection strategy is also proposed to activate the residual link on demand, striking a balance between transmission performance and computational cost. Experimental results show that LLM-MHPSC outperforms state-of-the-art semantic communication and traditional schemes, realizing robust image transmission with a marginal increase in bandwidth. This framework provides a flexible and effective solution for extending semantic communication to practical multi-hop application scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出一种大语言模型增强的多跳并行图像语义通信（LLM-MHPSC）框架，以缓解多跳无线图像传输中的失真累积问题。与传统的单跳语义通信方案不同，LLM-MHPSC在每一跳额外部署残差补偿链路以抵消累积失真。为最小化额外带宽开销，设计了粗到细残差压缩方案，该方案将基于深度学习的压缩器与自适应算术编码（AAC）相结合。此外，开发了基于大语言模型的残差传输优化器（LLM-RTO），用于准确估计残差分布并实现信道状态与跳数感知的码率调整，从而在不同信道与跳数条件下提高残差压缩效率。还提出一种自适应跳数选择策略，可按需激活残差链路，在传输性能与计算成本之间取得平衡。实验结果表明，LLM-MHPSC优于现有最先进的语义通信方案及传统方案，在带宽开销仅轻微增加的情况下实现了鲁棒的图像传输。该框架为将语义通信扩展到实际多跳应用场景提供了一种灵活且有效的解决方案。

</details>


### [151] [Data-driven Video Codec with Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15298)
*Nishan Khanal,Saugat Neupane,Abhinav Chalise,Nimesh Gopal Pradhan,Dinesh Baniya Kshatri*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种将视频和音频联合编码为单个正弦表示网络（SIREN）权重的方法，通过知识蒸馏、量化和无损压缩实现整体2.61倍压缩比。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统编解码器以压缩像素数据存储视频，本文探索将视频和音频存储为神经网络权重的全新范式，验证其可行性与压缩效果。

Method: 使用SIREN网络将时空坐标映射到RGB值和音频振幅；通过响应式知识蒸馏将过拟合教师网络压缩为更小的学生网络；随后进行16位对称权重量化和LZMA2无损编码。

Result: 在6.08 MiB测试视频上，量化学生网络达到视频PSNR 28.72 dB、SSIM 0.75，音频PSNR 24.18 dB、对数谱距离10.69 dB，表示从9.05 MiB压缩至2.33 MiB，压缩比2.61。位宽扫描显示16位量化时重建质量饱和。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了视频音频联合的神经表示压缩，但与H.264、HEVC和MP3相比仍有差距；展示了基于浏览器的WebRTC原型系统。

Original Abstract: A conventional codec stores a video as compressed pixel data. We instead store the video, together with its audio track, as the weights of a single sinusoidal representation network (SIREN) that maps space-time coordinates to RGB values and audio amplitudes. The network uses separate audio and video initialization layers, a stack of shared fully connected hidden layers, and three output branches: one for video and two Siamese audio branches whose disagreement is used to estimate and subtract residual noise. The overfitted teacher network is then compressed by response-based knowledge distillation into a smaller student, followed by 16-bit symmetric weight quantization and lossless LZMA2 (xz) encoding. On a 6.08 MiB test video, the quantized student reaches a video PSNR of 28.72 dB with SSIM of 0.75, and an audio PSNR of 24.18 dB with a log spectral distance of 10.69 dB, while the pipeline shrinks the representation from 9.05 MiB to 2.33 MiB, an overall compression ratio of 2.61. A bit-width sweep from 1-bit to 32-bit quantization shows that reconstruction quality saturates at 16 bits. We compare against H.264, HEVC, and MP3, report where the approach falls short of them, and describe a browser-based prototype that trains, transfers, and decodes these models over WebRTC.

中文摘要翻译: 传统编解码器将视频存储为压缩后的像素数据。我们则提出将视频及其音频轨道一起存储为单个正弦表示网络（SIREN）的权重，该网络将时空坐标映射到RGB值和音频振幅。网络使用独立的音频和视频初始化层、一组共享的全连接隐藏层，以及三个输出分支：一个用于视频，两个用于音频的孪生分支，利用它们之间的差异来估计并减去残差噪声。过拟合的教师网络随后通过基于响应的知识蒸馏压缩为更小的学生网络，再进行16位对称权重量化和无损LZMA2（xz）编码。在一个6.08 MiB的测试视频上，量化后的学生网络达到视频PSNR 28.72 dB、SSIM 0.75，音频PSNR 24.18 dB、对数谱距离10.69 dB，同时该流水线将表示从9.05 MiB压缩至2.33 MiB，整体压缩比为2.61。从1位到32位量化的位宽扫描表明，重建质量在16位时趋于饱和。我们与H.264、HEVC和MP3进行了比较，报告了该方法在某些方面的不足，并描述了一个基于浏览器的原型系统，该系统能够通过WebRTC对这些模型进行训练、传输和解码。

</details>


### [152] [MSTF-Net: A UAV-Oriented Multi-Spectral Video Segmentation Method via Modality-Robust, Scale-Adaptive, and Consistent Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15628)
*Chenwei Wang,Zhida Wang,Zelin Li,Elif Ozden-Yenigun,Houde Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出MSTF-Net，一种用于无人机多光谱视频分割的模态鲁棒尺度自适应融合框架，通过模态空间互补抑制增强模块和多尺度时间跨模态语义一致性模块，分别解决模态融合困境和时间变化问题，在RGB-T数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多光谱视频分割面临两个核心挑战：(1) RGB与热成像特征存在显著差异，模态融合时互补线索被掩盖；(2) 无人机平台快速运动和视角变化导致时序外观不一致和帧间错位。现有方法未能同时有效解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出MSTF-Net框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 模态空间互补抑制增强模块(MSCSE)，通过跨模态注意力生成统一实例查询，并利用残差引导的差异过滤和一致性约束抑制模态特定噪声；(2) 多尺度时间跨模态语义一致性模块(MTCSC)，基于帧距离自适应调整时间感受野，同时捕获粗糙全局上下文和精细局部结构。

Result: 在MVSeg数据集上达到56.42% mIoU，在CART数据集上达到51.80% mIoU，尤其在目标小、遮挡和模态退化等困难条件下表现优异，达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: MSTF-Net通过有效建模跨模态融合和时间一致性，显著提升了多光谱视频分割性能，验证了在无人机复杂环境下的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Multi-spectral video segmentation is essential for robust scene understanding in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) applications such as city planning, land use monitoring, traffic monitoring, and crowd estimation. While the fusion of RGB and thermal modalities offers complementary information for perception under varying lighting and visibility conditions, two fundamental challenges remain: (1) the modal fusion dilemma, arising from significant discrepancies between RGB and thermal features that obscure complementary cues, and (2) temporal variation, induced by rapid motion and viewpoint changes on UAV platforms, which leads to appearance inconsistency and misalignment across frames. To address these issues, this study proposed MSTF-Net, a modality-robust scale-adaptive fusion framework for multi-spectral video segmentation that effectively models cross-modal fusion and temporal consistency. The Modality Spatial Complementary Suppression and Enhancement (MSCSE) module generates unified instance queries via cross-modal attention and suppresses modality-specific noise using residual-guided discrepancy filtering and consistency constraints. To model temporal dynamics, the Multi-scale Temporal Cross-modality Semantic Consistency (MTCSC) module adaptively adjusts the temporal receptive field based on frame distance, capturing both coarse global context and fine local structure across time. Extensive ablation experiments on public RGB-T datasets demonstrate that MSTF-Net achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance, especially under challenging conditions such as small targets, occlusion, and modality degradation. Specifically, reached 56.42\% mIoU on the MVSeg dataset and 51.80\% mIoU on the CART dataset, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 多光谱视频分割对于无人机在城市规划、土地利用监测、交通监控和人群估计等应用中的鲁棒场景理解至关重要。尽管RGB和热成像模态的融合在不同光照和能见度条件下提供了互补的感知信息，但仍存在两个基本挑战：(1) 模态融合困境，即RGB与热成像特征之间的显著差异会掩盖互补线索；(2) 时间变化，即无人机平台上的快速运动和视角变化会导致帧间外观不一致和错位。为解决这些问题，本研究提出MSTF-Net，一种用于多光谱视频分割的模态鲁棒尺度自适应融合框架，能够有效建模跨模态融合和时间一致性。模态空间互补抑制增强模块通过跨模态注意力生成统一的实例查询，并利用残差引导的差异过滤和一致性约束抑制模态特定噪声。为建模时间动态，多尺度时间跨模态语义一致性模块基于帧距离自适应调整时间感受野，在时间维度上同时捕获粗糙的全局上下文和精细的局部结构。在公开RGB-T数据集上的大量消融实验表明，MSTF-Net达到了最先进的分割性能，尤其是在小目标、遮挡和模态退化等具有挑战性的条件下。具体而言，在MVSeg数据集上达到56.42%的mIoU，在CART数据集上达到51.80%的mIoU。

</details>


### [153] [Robust Monitoring of Arc Welding Processes: A Generalizable Framework with DVAE and Particle Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16013)
*Yue Cao,Hai Lin,YuMing Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合无监督深度潜表示学习（DVAE）与贝叶斯滤波（粒子滤波）的通用弧焊监控框架，无需特定工艺调参即可在GTAW和GMAW上实现鲁棒且可泛化的实时状态监测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 弧焊过程易受干扰影响焊缝质量，实时监控至关重要但面临复杂视觉模式和非线性时变动力学挑战；现有深度学习方法依赖大规模标注数据和特定应用调参，可扩展性受限。本文探索能否通过统一的监控方法跨应用表征主要弧焊工艺并提升可扩展性。

Method: 提出一种鲁棒且可泛化的弧焊监控框架，结合无监督深度潜表示学习与贝叶斯滤波。具体地，采用动态变分自编码器（DVAE），由基于CNN的编码器-解码器和基于LSTM的转换模型组成，联合学习潜表示及其在控制输入下的演化；为进行鲁棒的实时推理，采用专用粒子滤波器（PF）传播潜变量和LSTM隐藏状态，保留过程历史并抑制传感器噪声。

Result: 在GTAW（钨极惰性气体保护焊）和GMAW（熔化极气体保护焊）两种工艺上进行验证，无需工艺特定调参，实验结果表明该框架具有良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够统一表征多种弧焊工艺，通过无监督学习和贝叶斯滤波的结合，在无需大规模标注数据和特定调参的情况下实现鲁棒的实时监控，适用于焊接的慢动态惯性动力学特性。

Original Abstract: Arc welding processes are essential for continuous fabrication but prone to disturbances that impair weld quality, making real-time monitoring critical yet difficult due to complex visual patterns and nonlinear, time-varying dynamics. Deep learning shows promise but faces scalability limits because of its dependence on large labeled datasets and application-specific tuning. We explore whether a unified approach can characterize major arc welding processes across applications and improve scalability through consistent state monitoring. This paper introduces a robust and generalizable monitoring framework for arc welding. It combines unsupervised deep latent representation learning, which extracts compact features from weld pool images, with Bayesian filtering to handle persistent and fluctuating disturbances such as arc radiation and specular reflections. Specifically, a Dynamic Variational Autoencoder (DVAE), consisting of a CNN-based encoder-decoder and an LSTM-based transition model, jointly learns latent representations and their evolution under control inputs. For robust real-time inference, a specialized Particle Filter (PF) propagates the latent and LSTM hidden states, preserving process history while suppressing sensor noise. This design is well suited to welding's slow and inertial dynamics. Validation on GTAW and GMAW without process-specific tuning demonstrates the framework's generalizability and robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 弧焊工艺对于连续制造至关重要，但易受干扰而损害焊缝质量，这使得实时监控既关键又困难，原因在于复杂的视觉模式以及非线性、时变的动力学特性。深度学习虽有前景，但因依赖大规模标注数据集和特定应用调参而面临可扩展性限制。我们探索是否能够通过统一的方法跨应用表征主要弧焊工艺，并通过一致的状态监控提升可扩展性。本文介绍了一种鲁棒且可泛化的弧焊监控框架。该框架将无监督深度潜表示学习（从熔池图像中提取紧凑特征）与贝叶斯滤波相结合，以处理诸如弧光辐射和镜面反射等持续性和波动性干扰。具体而言，一个动态变分自编码器（DVAE），由基于CNN的编码器-解码器和一个基于LSTM的转换模型组成，联合学习潜表示及其在控制输入下的演化。为实现鲁棒的实时推理，一个专门的粒子滤波器（PF）传播潜变量和LSTM隐藏状态，在保留过程历史的同时抑制传感器噪声。该设计非常适合焊接的慢动态和惯性动力学特性。在无需工艺特定调参的情况下，在GTAW和GMAW上的验证表明了该框架的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Low Complexity Neural Network Digital Predistortion of Wideband Power Amplifiers through Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15441)
*Cel Thys,Rodney Martinez Alonso,Ali H Alsarraf,Dominique Schreurs,Sofie Pollin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LASSO和MRMR特征选择的低复杂度神经网络数字预失真架构，在保持线性化性能的同时降低30%计算复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于神经网络（尤其是相位归一化NN架构）的DPD方法计算成本远高于多项式方法（如MP和GMP），为弥合研究性能与实际部署之间的差距，需设计低复杂度的NN-DPD方案。

Method: 提出一种低复杂度的特征选择NN-DPD架构，采用基于LASSO（最小绝对收缩与选择算子）和MRMR（最小冗余最大相关性）算法的离线特征工程流水线，构建紧凑且信息丰富的输入表示。

Result: 基于公开的宽带FR3功率放大器数据集，实现了高达30%的计算复杂度降低，同时保持相当（comparable）的线性化性能。

Conclusion: 所提特征选择NN-DPD架构能够有效降低神经网络DPD的计算复杂度，使其更贴近实际部署，且不会显著牺牲线性化性能。

Original Abstract: Due to the continuous increase in communication bandwidth and the use of highly efficient yet nonlinear power amplifiers, Digital Predistortion (DPD) algorithms are becoming increasingly complex. In particular, neural network (NN) based DPD approaches using Phase-Normalized NN architectures often incur substantially higher computational costs than widely deployed polynomial-based methods, such as the Memory Polynomial (MP) and Generalized Memory Polynomial (GMP) models. To bridge this gap between research performance and practical implementation, we propose a low-complexity Feature Selection NN DPD architecture. The proposed method employs an offline feature-engineering pipeline based on the Least Absolute Shrinkage and Selection Operator (LASSO) and the Minimum Redundancy Maximum Relevance (MRMR) algorithm to construct a compact and informative input representation. Using measured wideband FR3 power amplifier datasets that are publicly released with this work, we demonstrate up to 30% reduction in computational complexity while maintaining comparable linearization performance.

中文摘要翻译: 由于通信带宽的持续增长以及高效但非线性功率放大器的广泛使用，数字预失真（DPD）算法正变得越来越复杂。特别是，采用相位归一化神经网络架构的DPD方法往往比广泛部署的多项式方法（如记忆多项式MP和广义记忆多项式GMP）产生更高的计算成本。为弥合研究性能与实际实现之间的差距，我们提出了一种低复杂度的特征选择NN-DPD架构。该方法采用基于最小绝对收缩与选择算子（LASSO）和最小冗余最大相关性（MRMR）算法的离线特征工程流水线，构建紧凑且信息丰富的输入表示。通过使用随本文公开发布的实测宽带FR3功率放大器数据集，我们证明了该方案在保持相当线性化性能的同时，能够实现高达30%的计算复杂度降低。

</details>


### [155] [Pinching Antenna-Assisted ISAC with Waveguide Mode Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15547)
*Ruotong Zhao,Yijia Zhang,Shaokang Hu,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模式可选的钉墙天线辅助通感一体化框架，通过联合优化波导模式选择、发射波束成形及收发天线位置，在满足多用户服务质量约束下最大化感知信噪比，显著优于传统方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统钉墙天线辅助的通感一体化架构通常假设接收机位置静态或预定义接收波导，未能充分利用固有的空间自由度，限制了感知与通信性能的协同提升。

Method: 提出模式可选的PA辅助ISAC框架，联合优化波导模式选择、发射波束成形及收发PA位置；针对混合整数非凸优化问题，提出一种基于惩罚的majorization-minimization方法的低复杂度块坐标下降算法。

Result: 数值结果显示所提设计在性能上显著优于传统PA和固定天线基准，模式可选设计实现了收发操作与感知通信资源分配的协同优化，在严格通信约束下维持感知鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过协同利用空间适应性和模式可重构性，模式可选PA辅助ISAC框架能够有效提升感知与通信的联合性能，为通感一体化系统的天线设计提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Conventional pinching antenna (PA)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) architectures typically assume static receiver locations or predetermined receive waveguides, thereby underutilizing the inherent spatial degrees of freedom. This paper proposes a novel mode-selectable PA-assisted ISAC framework to maximize the post-combining sensing signal-to-noise ratio while satisfying multi-user quality-of-service constraints by jointly optimizing the waveguide mode selection, transmit beamforming, and transmit/receive PA positions. To tackle the resulting mixed-integer nonconvex optimization problem, we develop a low-complexity block-coordinate descent algorithm that leverages a penalty-based majorization-minimization method to achieve high-quality suboptimal solutions. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed design significantly outperforms both traditional PA and fixed-antenna benchmarks by synergistically harnessing spatial adaptability and modal reconfigurability. In particular, the mode-selectable design enables the coordinated optimization of transmit/receive operations and sensing-communication resource allocation, thereby maintaining sensing robustness under stringent communication requirements.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的钉墙天线辅助通感一体化架构通常假设接收机位置静态或接收波导预定义，从而未能充分利用固有的空间自由度。本文提出一种新颖的模式可选钉墙天线辅助通感一体化框架，通过联合优化波导模式选择、发射波束成形以及发射/接收钉墙天线位置，在满足多用户服务质量约束的同时最大化合并后的感知信噪比。为求解由此产生的混合整数非凸优化问题，我们开发了一种低复杂度的块坐标下降算法，该算法利用基于惩罚的majorization-minimization方法获得高质量次优解。数值结果表明，所提出的设计通过协同利用空间适应性和模式可重构性，显著优于传统钉墙天线和固定天线基准。特别地，模式可选设计实现了收发操作与感知通信资源分配的协同优化，从而在严格通信要求下维持感知鲁棒性。

</details>


### [156] [DFT-p-FDMA Based Chirp Transmission in CP-OFDM for Unified ISAC Waveform Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15575)
*Fabrizio Carpi,Joonyoung Cho,Kyeong Jin Kim,Charlie Jianzhong Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CP-OFDM的ISAC框架，利用DFT-p-FDMA波形实现无干扰的通信与雷达感知共存，支持灵活的距离-多普勒分辨率调整。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ISAC系统在通信与感知共存时存在干扰问题，缺乏灵活高效的波形设计来同时支持chirp信号传输和CP-OFDM数据通信。

Method: 采用DFT-p-FDMA波形，通过离散傅里叶变换相位旋转和排列实现频分多址，在部分频段传输chirp信号，其余频段进行CP-OFDM数据通信，并分析有效信道响应及多符号处理提升多普勒分辨率。

Result: 仿真验证了该框架在时延和多普勒估计方面的有效性，能通过优化的时频资源分配灵活调整距离-多普勒分辨率。

Conclusion: 该ISAC框架为下一代无线网络中通信与感知的融合提供了一种多功能、无干扰的解决方案。

Original Abstract: We propose an integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) framework that supports chirp signal transmission in CP-OFDM-based multiple access communication systems, enabling efficient coexistence of communication and sensing capabilities. Our framework employs the discrete Fourier transform phase rotated and permuted frequency division multiple access (DFT-p-FDMA) waveform to transmit chirp signals using a portion of the frequency resources, while ensuring interference-free concurrent CP-OFDM data transmissions on other bands. We analyze the effective channel behavior under the DFT-p-FDMA waveform, characterizing how delays and Doppler shifts impact radar target echoes. We also show how processing multiple received symbols improves Doppler resolution in practical scenarios. Our framework allows flexible adjustment of range-Doppler resolution through optimized time-frequency resource allocation, offering a versatile solution for ISAC applications. Simulation results validate the framework's performance in delay and Doppler estimation, highlighting its potential to support ISAC in next-generation wireless networks.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种集成感知与通信（ISAC）框架，支持在基于CP-OFDM的多址通信系统中传输chirp信号，实现通信与感知能力的高效共存。该框架采用离散傅里叶变换相位旋转与排列频分多址（DFT-p-FDMA）波形，使用部分频率资源传输chirp信号，同时确保其他频段上无干扰的并发CP-OFDM数据传输。我们分析了DFT-p-FDMA波形下的有效信道行为，刻画了时延和多普勒频移对雷达目标回波的影响。我们还展示了在实际场景中处理多个接收符号如何提升多普勒分辨率。通过优化的时频资源分配，该框架允许灵活调整距离-多普勒分辨率，为ISAC应用提供了一种多功能解决方案。仿真结果验证了该框架在时延和多普勒估计方面的性能，凸显了其在下一代无线网络中支持ISAC的潜力。

</details>


### [157] [An Open-Source, Autonomous Platform for High-Resolution Energy Monitoring in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15594)
*Vignesh Selvaraj,Aditya Nagaraj,Shengyuan Zhang,Sina Sadeghian,Sangkee Min*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出AEMS，一个开源、低成本、模块化的自主能量监测平台，结合研究级精度与工业部署能力，在三轴CNC加工中心上验证了其可解析多种能量状态并检测50 mm/min的进给速率变化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高分辨率工业能量数据对工业4.0至关重要，但现有商业功率分析仪专有封闭、限制高频数据访问且不可定制，而通用开源硬件缺乏工业测量所需的精度、隔离和鲁棒性，因此亟需一个兼具研究级保真度和工业可部署性的开放平台。

Method: AEMS采用隔离前端+24位同步采样ADC获取三相电压电流，双核架构分离确定性采集记录与主机通信控制，通过以太网、RS-485/Modbus和BLE工业接口实现多机时间对齐采集，配合主机-边缘网关-可选云端的完整软件栈。

Result: 在三轴CNC加工中心上的验证表明，该系统能够解析主轴、进给驱动、快速移动和材料去除等能量状态，并可检测50 mm/min的进给速率微小变化。

Conclusion: 通过开源全部硬件和固件，AEMS旨在为研究人员和中小制造商提供高保真能量监测的民主化访问。

Original Abstract: High-resolution energy data is increasingly central to Industry 4.0, where electrical signals such as three-phase voltage and current carry rich information about machine condition, tool wear, and process dynamics. Capturing this information in practice remains difficult: commercial power analysis are largely proprietary, offer limited or no access to high-sampling rate data for transient analysis, restrict access to raw waveform data, and offer no customization, while general-purpose open hardware lacks the front-end accuracy, isolation, and robustness required for industrial measurement. This paper presents Autonomous Energy Monitoring System (AEMS), an open-source, low-cost, and modular platform supported by a host, edge-gateway, and optional cloud software stack that enables autonomous, long-duration acquisition independent of a continuously connected host and thereby closes this gap by combining research-grade fidelity with industrial deployability. The system acquires three-phase voltage and current through an isolated front-end and a 24-bit, simultaneously sampling analog-to-digital converter, managed by a dual-core architecture that separates deterministic acquisition and on-board logging from host communication and control. Industrial interfaces (Ethernet, RS-485/Modbus, and BLE) together with hardware-level synchronization enable scalable, time-aligned acquisition across multiple machines, supported by a complete host, edge-gateway, and optional cloud software stack. We validate the platform on a three-axis CNC machining center, where it resolves spindle, feed-drive, rapid-traverse, and material-removal energy states and detects feed-rate changes as small as 50 mm/min. By releasing the full hardware and firmware openly, this work aims to democratize access to high-fidelity energy monitoring for both researchers and small and medium-sized manufacturers.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率能源数据在工业4.0中日益重要，其中三相电压和电流等电气信号承载着关于机器状态、刀具磨损和过程动态的丰富信息。然而，在实践中捕捉这些信息仍然困难：商用功率分析仪大多是专有的，对用于瞬态分析的高采样率数据提供有限或无法访问，限制对原始波形数据的访问，且无法定制；而通用开源硬件则缺乏工业测量所需的前端精度、隔离和鲁棒性。本文提出自主能量监测系统（AEMS），一个开源、低成本、模块化平台，由主机、边缘网关和可选云软件栈支持，能够在不依赖持续连接主机的条件下实现自主、长期的数据采集，从而弥合了研究级保真度与工业可部署性之间的差距。该系统通过隔离前端和24位同步采样模数转换器获取三相电压和电流，由双核架构管理，将确定性采集和板载记录与主机通信和控制分离。工业接口（以太网、RS-485/Modbus和BLE）结合硬件级同步，可实现跨多台机器的可扩展、时间对齐采集，并有完整的主机、边缘网关和可选云软件栈支持。我们在三轴CNC加工中心上验证了该平台，它能解析主轴、进给驱动、快速移动和材料去除等能量状态，并检测到小至50 mm/min的进给速率变化。通过完全开源硬件和固件，这项工作旨在为研究人员和中小型制造商提供高保真能量监测的民主化访问。

</details>


### [158] [Optimal Sampling and Reconstruction of Graph Signals in the Fractional Fourier Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15602)
*Xiaopeng Cheng,Zhichao Zhang,Yangfan He*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图分数阶傅里叶变换（GFRFT）域的图信号采样与重建框架，通过引入可调节的分数阶参数为图信号提供更优的表示域，从而提升重建性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统图傅里叶变换（GFT）域中，实际图信号频谱集中性不足，导致重建性能受限。为克服这一问题，引入图分数阶傅里叶变换域，通过优化分数阶参数获得更合适的信号表示域。

Method: 提出基于GFRFT域的图信号采样与重建统一框架。将分数阶作为可调谱域参数，通过优化选择为给定图信号和采样模型提供更合适的表示域。在统一采样重建公式下，融合子空间、平滑性和随机先验，考虑无约束和预定义两种重建机制，衍生出多种分数域采样与重建方法。

Result: 理论分析表明，最优GFRFT域通过改善能量集中性、降低投影残差，提供更合适的低维谱表示；进一步考虑残差泄漏和噪声放大效应，解释了表示优势如何转化为重建误差降低。实验结果显示GFRFT域方法整体优于GFT域方法。

Conclusion: 基于图分数阶傅里叶变换域的采样与重建框架相比传统GFT域方法能取得更好的恢复性能，分数阶参数为图信号处理提供了额外的灵活性和优化空间。

Original Abstract: Graph signal sampling and reconstruction are commonly formulated in the graph Fourier transform (GFT) domain. However, the reconstruction performance may be limited when practical graph signals are not sufficiently concentrated in the GFT spectrum. To address this issue, this paper proposes a graph signal sampling and reconstruction framework based on the graph fractional Fourier transform (GFRFT) domain. The fractional order is introduced as an adjustable spectral domain parameter, and the optimal order is selected to provide a more suitable representation domain for a given graph signal and sampling model. Under a unified sampling reconstruction formulation, subspace, smoothness, and stochastic priors are incorporated, and both unconstrained and predefined reconstruction mechanisms are considered, leading to several fractional domain sampling and reconstruction methods. Furthermore, the theoretical analysis shows that the optimal GFRFT domain can provide a more suitable low-dimensional spectral representation by improving energy concentration and reducing projection residual. The effects of residual leakage and noise amplification are further considered to explain how this representation advantage is translated into reconstruction error reduction. Experimental results show that, GFRFT domain sampling and reconstruction generally achieve better recovery performance than GFT domain methods.

中文摘要翻译: 图信号采样与重建通常在图傅里叶变换（GFT）域中表述。然而，当实际图信号在GFT频谱中集中性不足时，重建性能可能受到限制。为解决这一问题，本文提出一种基于图分数阶傅里叶变换（GFRFT）域的图信号采样与重建框架。该框架引入分数阶作为可调节的谱域参数，通过选择最优阶数为给定的图信号和采样模型提供更合适的表示域。在统一的采样重建公式下，本文融合了子空间、平滑性和随机先验，同时考虑了无约束和预定义两种重建机制，从而衍生出多种分数域采样与重建方法。此外，理论分析表明，最优GFRFT域通过改善能量集中性和降低投影残差，可以提供更合适的低维谱表示。进一步考虑残差泄漏和噪声放大效应，解释了这种表示优势如何转化为重建误差的降低。实验结果表明，GFRFT域采样与重建方法整体上优于GFT域方法。

</details>


### [159] [FSIM: Fluid Element Stacked Intelligent Metasurface for Multiuser Downlink Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15651)
*Tsung-Yu Wei,Li-Hsiang Shen,Kai-Ten Feng,Lie-Liang Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对多输入单输出（MISO）通信系统，提出了一种流体元件辅助的堆叠智能超表面（FSIM）架构，通过允许每层元原子在预设流体区域内移动来增加空间分集，并结合交替优化算法联合优化元原子位置、基站发射波束赋形和FSIM相移，以最大化系统和速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统堆叠智能超表面（SIM）的元原子位置固定，空间自由度有限，难以灵活重构级联信道以增强期望信号并抑制多用户干扰。本文旨在通过引入流体可移动元原子架构，进一步提升空间分集增益和系统性能。

Method: 提出交替优化（AO）算法，将原非凸非线性问题分解为三个子问题：波束赋形和FSIM相移子问题通过半定规划、逐次凸近似、一阶泰勒近似和基于惩罚的秩一松弛求解；元原子位置子问题通过投影梯度更新法处理。

Result: 仿真结果表明：紧凑型FSIM（层间厚度小）性能更优，增大层厚会削弱层间耦合、降低和速率；所提FSIM显著优于固定位置SIM、贴片结构、部分可动、受限流体区域及现有柔性智能超表面；所提AO算法优于子方案、元启发式方法和传统波束赋形基准。

Conclusion: FSIM通过赋予元原子流体移动能力，有效提升了空间分集和信道重构灵活性，在MISO通信系统中相比传统SIM具有显著性能优势，且所提AO算法能够高效求解该联合优化问题。

Original Abstract: A fluid element (FE)-aided stacked intelligent metasurface (FSIM) for multiple-input single-output (MISO) communication system is investigated, where a multi-antenna base station (BS) serves multiple single-antenna users through FSIM. Unlike conventional SIM with fixed meta-atom deployment, the architecture allows the meta-atoms in each layer to move within a predefined fluidic region to further increase the spatial diversity. By jointly optimizing the two-dimensional meta-atom positions, BS transmit beamforming, and FSIM phase-shifts, the cascaded BS-FSIM-user channels can be flexibly reconfigured to enhance the desired signals and suppress multiuser interference. The proposed sum-rate maximization problem is highly non-convex and nonlinear due to the coupled solutions of element position, beamforming, and phase-shift. To address this challenge, an alternating optimization (AO) algorithm is developed to iteratively update these variables. The beamforming and FSIM phase-shift subproblems are transformed into semi-definite programming problems and solved by using successive convex approximation (SCA), first-order Taylor approximation, and penalty-based rank-one relaxation, whilst the FE position subproblem is handled through a projected gradient-based update. Simulation results reveal that a compact FSIM with a small inter-layer thickness is preferable, as increasing the thickness weakens inter-layer coupling and degrades the achievable sum rate. Results also demonstrate that the proposed FSIM significantly outperforms conventional SIMs with fixed positions, patch-based structures, partial fluidity, restricted fluid regions, and existing flexible intelligent metasurfaces. Furthermore, the proposed AO-based algorithm achieves superior rate performance compared to sub-schemes, metaheuristic methods, and conventional beamforming benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种面向多输入单输出（MISO）通信系统的流体元件辅助堆叠智能超表面（FSIM），其中多天线基站（BS）通过FSIM服务多个单天线用户。与元原子部署位置固定的传统SIM不同，该架构允许每层中的元原子在预设流体区域内移动，以进一步增加空间分集。通过联合优化二维元原子位置、基站发射波束赋形和FSIM相移，可灵活重构级联的BS-FSIM-用户信道，以增强期望信号并抑制多用户干扰。所提出的和速率最大化问题因元件位置、波束赋形和相移的解耦合而呈现高度非凸和非线性。为应对这一挑战，本文开发了一种交替优化（AO）算法，迭代更新这些变量。波束赋形和FSIM相移子问题被转化为半定规划问题，并利用逐次凸近似（SCA）、一阶泰勒近似和基于惩罚的秩一松弛求解，而FE位置子问题则通过投影梯度更新法处理。仿真结果表明，层间厚度较小的紧凑型FSIM更优，因为增加厚度会削弱层间耦合并降低可达和速率。结果还表明，所提出的FSIM显著优于固定位置的传统SIM、贴片结构、部分可动性、受限流体区域以及现有的柔性智能超表面。此外，与子方案、元启发式方法和传统波束赋形基准相比，所提出的基于AO的算法实现了更优的速率性能。

</details>


### [160] [Energy-Efficient Resource Allocation for Six-Dimensional Movable Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15653)
*Ziyun Zhang,Ruotong Zhao,Shaokang Hu,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究配备六维可移动天线（6DMA）的多用户无线网络能效最大化问题，提出结合Dinkelbach变换和MM方法的BCD优化框架，在考虑机械能耗的情况下联合优化天线3D位置、旋转角度、发射波束赋形和时间分配。仿真结果表明所提设计显著优于传统基准，并揭示了吞吐量提升与机械开销之间的基本权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究大多忽略6DMA实际运行中不可忽略的机械能耗，以及天线位置、旋转角度、发射波束赋形和时间分配之间的强耦合关系，导致能效优化问题高度非凸且难以解析求解。

Method: 提出块坐标下降（BCD）优化框架，集成Dinkelbach变换与MM（majorization-minimization）方法，交替优化天线3D位置、旋转角度、发射波束赋形和时间分配，以获得具有收敛保证的高质量次优解。

Result: 所提设计在能效上显著优于传统基准；存在吞吐量提升与机械开销之间的基本权衡：更大的天线重构可改善信道条件，但其能效增益会因机械能耗增加而逐渐减小。

Conclusion: 在6DMA系统中考虑实际机械能耗至关重要；天线重构的能效增益存在边际递减效应，需在信道改善与机械开销之间取得平衡。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates the energy-efficiency (EE) maximization problem for a multiuser wireless network equipped with six-dimensional movable antennas (6DMAs), where the three-dimensional (3D) positions and orientations of the antennas are jointly optimized to fully exploit the additional spatial degrees of freedom offered by dynamic channel reconfiguration. However, the practical operation of 6DMAs incurs non-negligible mechanical energy consumption. Moreover, orientation-dependent phase variations, together with the strong coupling among antenna positions, rotation angles, transmit beamforming, and time allocation, render the resulting problem highly non-convex and analytically challenging. To address this issue, we develop a block coordinate descent (BCD) optimization framework that integrates Dinkelbach's transformation with the majorization-minimization (MM) approach to efficiently obtain a high quality suboptimal solution with guaranteed convergence. Simulation results unveil that the proposed design achieves significant EE improvements over conventional benchmarks, thereby highlighting the critical importance of accounting for practical mechanical energy costs in 6DMA enabled systems. Furthermore, our results reveal a fundamental trade-off between throughput enhancement and mechanical overhead: although larger antenna reconfigurations can improve channel conditions, their EE gains gradually diminish due to the increased mechanical energy consumption.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了配备六维可移动天线（6DMA）的多用户无线网络中的能效最大化问题，其中联合优化天线的三维位置和方向，以充分利用动态信道重构所提供的额外空间自由度。然而，6DMA的实际运行会产生不可忽略的机械能耗。此外，依赖于方向的相位变化，以及天线位置、旋转角度、发射波束赋形和时间分配之间的强耦合，使得所研究的问题高度非凸且解析求解极具挑战。为此，我们提出了一个块坐标下降（BCD）优化框架，该框架将Dinkelbach变换与majorization-minimization（MM）方法相结合，以高效地获得具有收敛保证的高质量次优解。仿真结果表明，所提出的设计方案在能效上显著优于传统基准，从而凸显了在6DMA系统中考虑实际机械能耗的关键重要性。此外，我们的结果揭示了吞吐量提升与机械开销之间的基本权衡：尽管更大的天线重构可以改善信道条件，但其能效增益会因机械能耗的增加而逐渐减小。

</details>


### [161] [Energy Efficient Active Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15654)
*Li-Hsiang Shen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种能量高效的主动堆叠智能超表面（ASIM）辅助下行传输框架，通过联合优化基站波束成形和ASIM配置来最大化能量效率（EE），并提出基于交替优化、SCA和贝叶斯优化的求解方法，数值结果显示该方案显著优于传统无源SIM和启发式基准方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统无源智能超表面在电磁波调控能力上存在局限，且能量效率有待提升。本文旨在利用主动放大和多层透射架构增强波束调控能力，并系统性地优化系统能效，为未来主动超表面辅助无线网络提供设计依据。

Method: 首先通过epigraph、拉格朗日对偶和二次变换将原非凸问题转化为可解形式；然后提出交替优化框架：基站波束成形子问题采用连续凸近似（SCA）求解，ASIM配置子问题基于高斯过程代理模型使用贝叶斯优化求解。

Result: 所提方案相比传统无源SIM和启发式基准方法显著提升了可实现能量效率；同时分析了放大能力、超表面层数和层间距对系统性能的影响，为未来主动超表面辅助无线网络提供了设计洞见。

Conclusion: 主动堆叠智能超表面（ASIM）结合所提优化框架能有效提升系统能量效率，放大能力、层数和层间距是影响性能的关键参数，为未来无线通信系统设计提供了重要参考。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates an energy-efficient active stacked intelligent metasurfaces (ASIM)-assisted downlink transmission framework, where a multi-antenna base station (BS) serves multiple users through a multi-layer metasurface architecture. Unlike conventional passive intelligent surfaces, the considered ASIM employs active amplification and multiple transmissive layers to enhance electromagnetic wave manipulation. We aim to maximize the system energy efficiency (EE) by jointly optimizing the BS beamforming and ASIM configurations under user quality-of-service and amplification constraints. The resulting problem is highly coupled and non-convex due to the cascaded near-field channel and multi-layer metasurface structure. To address this challenge, we first transform the original problem through epigraph, Lagrangian dual, and quadratic transformations. An alternative optimization framework is then developed, where the BS beamforming subproblem is solved via successive convex approximation (SCA), while the ASIM configuration is optimized using Bayesian optimization based on a Gaussian-process surrogate model. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly improves the achievable EE compared to conventional passive SIM and heuristic benchmark methods. Furthermore, the impacts of amplification capability, number of metasurface layers, and inter-layer spacing on system performance are investigated, providing useful design insights for future active metasurface-assisted wireless networks.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一种能量高效的主动堆叠智能超表面（ASIM）辅助下行传输框架，其中多天线基站（BS）通过多层超表面架构服务多个用户。与传统无源智能表面不同，所考虑的ASIM利用主动放大和多个透射层来增强电磁波操控能力。我们旨在通过联合优化基站波束成形和ASIM配置，在用户服务质量与放大约束下最大化系统能量效率（EE）。由于级联近场信道和多层超表面结构，该优化问题具有高度耦合和非凸的特性。为应对这一挑战，我们首先通过epigraph、拉格朗日对偶和二次变换对原始问题进行转化。随后开发了一种交替优化框架，其中基站波束成形子问题通过连续凸近似（SCA）求解，而ASIM配置则基于高斯过程代理模型采用贝叶斯优化进行优化。数值结果表明，与传统无源SIM和启发式基准方法相比，所提方案显著提升了可实现能量效率。此外，本文还研究了放大能力、超表面层数和层间距对系统性能的影响，为未来主动超表面辅助无线网络提供了有益的设计洞见。

</details>


### [162] [Dual-Security for Indoor OFDM-ISAC Systems via Temporal Artificial Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15710)
*Yinchao Yang,Yathreb Bouazizi,Prabhat Raj Gautam,Michael Breza,Julie A. McCann*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对OFDM-ISAC系统中的双重安全性问题（通信安全与感知安全），提出利用时域人工噪声同时抵御恶意通信用户的非法感知和感知用户的通信窃听，在保障合法用户性能的前提下实现双重安全保护。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ISAC安全研究仅单独关注通信安全或感知安全，缺乏对两者同时保护的方案。本文填补这一空白，研究室内OFDM-ISAC系统中通信与感知的双重安全性。

Method: 提出时域人工噪声（AN）方案：一方面通过增大恶意CU的时域感知误差来阻止其非法感知，另一方面利用标准OFDM接收机处理降低SU在频域的信干噪比（SINR），从而削弱其通信窃听能力。同时保证合法SU的感知性能和合法CU的通信性能。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提人工噪声方案能够有效为OFDM-ISAC系统提供感知与通信的双重保护，同时保证合法用户的性能。

Conclusion: 时域人工噪声可同时实现通信与感知的双重安全防护，为解决ISAC系统中授权用户可能成为更有效窃听者的病理情形提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: With the rapid development of integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) as a key enabler for future wireless networks, ensuring the security of both communication and sensing functions has become increasingly important. Current secure ISAC studies focus restrictively either on the communication or the sensing security, but not both. To bridge this gap, this paper investigates security for both, i.e., dual-security, in indoor orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) based ISAC systems. Specifically, we consider a scenario in which a sensing user (SU) is authorised for sensing but may eavesdrop on communication data, while a communication user (CU) is authorised for communication but may perform unauthorised sensing. We chose this scenario as the pathological case where an authorised eavesdropper has more information and is more effective than an unauthorised one. To address this case, we propose the use of temporal artificial noise (AN) to prevent malicious CU sensing by enlarging its time-domain sensing error, and simultaneously degrade SU data eavesdropping by reducing its frequency-domain signal-to-noise-plus-interference ratio (SINR) with standard OFDM receiver processing. Meanwhile, our proposed scheme guarantees the sensing performance of the SU and the communication performance of the CU. We present numerical results that demonstrate AN can effectively provide dual protection for sensing and communication in OFDM-ISAC systems while guaranteeing the performance of legitimate users.

中文摘要翻译: 随着集成感知与通信（ISAC）作为未来无线网络关键使能技术的快速发展，确保通信与感知功能的安全性变得日益重要。当前安全的ISAC研究要么单独聚焦于通信安全，要么单独关注感知安全，而非两者兼顾。为填补这一空白，本文研究了基于室内正交频分复用（OFDM）的ISAC系统中的双重安全性，即通信与感知两者的安全。具体而言，我们考虑这样一个场景：感知用户（SU）被授权进行感知，但可能窃听通信数据；而通信用户（CU）被授权进行通信，但可能进行未授权的感知。我们选择该场景作为病理案例，因为授权窃听者相比未授权窃听者拥有更多信息且更具威胁。为解决这一问题，我们提出使用时域人工噪声（AN），一方面通过增大恶意CU的时域感知误差来阻止其非法感知，另一方面通过标准OFDM接收机处理降低SU在频域的信干噪比（SINR）以削弱其数据窃听能力。同时，我们提出的方案保证了SU的感知性能和CU的通信性能。我们提供的数值结果表明，人工噪声能够有效为OFDM-ISAC系统提供感知与通信的双重保护，同时保证合法用户的性能。

</details>


### [163] [Map as a Prompt: Learning Multi-Modal Spatial-Signal Foundation Models for Cross-scenario Wireless Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15713)
*Yong Chu,Xun Zhou,Zenglin Xu,Hui Wang,Yue Yu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出SigMap，一种多模态基础模型，通过循环自适应掩码策略和“地图即提示”框架，实现无线定位的高精度、强泛化与零样本跨场景适应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据驱动的无线定位方法泛化能力有限，需要大量标注数据且难以适应新场景，无法满足5G/6G应用对精确鲁棒定位的需求。

Method: (1) 循环自适应掩码策略：根据信道周期性特征动态调整掩码模式，学习鲁棒的无线表示；(2) “地图即提示”框架：通过轻量软提示将3D地理信息集成到模型中，实现有效的跨场景适应。

Result: 在多个定位任务上达到最优性能，在未见环境中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，显著优于监督和自监督基线方法。

Conclusion: SigMap通过结合周期自适应掩码和地图提示机制，有效解决了无线定位中的泛化难题，为6G时代高精度定位提供了基础模型方案。

Original Abstract: Accurate and robust wireless localization is a critical enabler for emerging 5G/6G applications, including autonomous driving, extended reality, and smart manufacturing. Despite its importance, achieving precise localization across diverse environments remains challenging due to the complex nature of wireless signals and their sensitivity to environmental changes. Existing data-driven approaches often suffer from limited generalization capability, requiring extensive labeled data and struggling to adapt to new scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose SigMap, a multimodal foundation model that introduces two key innovations: (1) A cycle-adaptive masking strategy that dynamically adjusts masking patterns based on channel periodicity characteristics to learn robust wireless representations; (2) A novel "map-as-prompt" framework that integrates 3D geographic information through lightweight soft prompts for effective cross-scenario adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple localization tasks while exhibiting strong zero-shot generalization in unseen environments, significantly outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines by considerable margins.

中文摘要翻译: 精确且鲁棒的无线定位是支撑新兴5G/6G应用（包括自动驾驶、扩展现实和智能制造）的关键使能技术。然而，由于无线信号的复杂特性及其对环境变化的敏感性，在多样化环境中实现精确定位仍然充满挑战。现有数据驱动方法通常泛化能力有限，需要大量标注数据且难以适应新场景。为解决这些局限，本文提出SigMap——一种多模态基础模型，其包含两项关键创新：(1) 循环自适应掩码策略，根据信道周期性特征动态调整掩码模式，以学习鲁棒的无线表示；(2) 新颖的“地图即提示”框架，通过轻量软提示将3D地理信息集成进来，实现有效的跨场景适应。大量实验表明，我们的模型在多个定位任务上均达到最优性能，同时在未见环境中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，显著超越了监督和自监督基线方法。

</details>


### [164] [Energy-Efficient Target-Aware Hybrid Beamforming for THz Near-Field ISAC with Sparse Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15716)
*Nusaibah A. Alshorman,Chong Han,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种反向设计方法，通过有意构造稀疏阵列产生的栅瓣作为可控辅助照明波束，实现太赫兹ISAC系统中扩展目标的单次全孔径感知，同时支持多用户下行通信，并在140 GHz仿真中验证了接近全数字的感知精度和节能效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 太赫兹ISAC面临关键瓶颈：高方向性波束无法在单波束内覆盖扩展目标，传统顺序波束扫描会降低感知精度并增加能耗；同时，常规稀疏阵列中栅瓣被视为需要抑制的有害伪影。本文试图扭转这一思路，将栅瓣变废为宝。

Method: 提出一种稀疏连接的混合波束赋形架构，通过开关控制的稀疏射频网络有意设计栅瓣，使其作为结构化辅助照明波束，实现扩展目标的单次全孔径照明；采用协方差驱动的交替最小化框架联合优化数字预编码器、量化移相器和天线-射频开关。

Result: 在140 GHz仿真下，该方法实现了接近全数字的克拉美-罗感知精度，在低秩太赫兹信道中具有竞争力的通信性能，快速收敛，并显著节省硬件和能耗。

Conclusion: 结构化稀疏连接是一种可扩展且节能的扩展目标太赫兹ISAC解决方案，建立了稀疏连接作为太赫兹ISAC实用架构的可行性。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) at terahertz (THz) frequencies enables ultra-high-resolution perception while facing a key limitation: highly directional THz beams cannot illuminate extended targets within a single beam. Conventional solutions rely on sequential beam scanning, reducing sensing accuracy and increasing energy consumption. Moreover, in conventional sparse-array, grating lobes are generally treated as undesirable artifacts that should be suppressed to avoid ambiguity and interference. In contrast, this paper adopts a reverse design philosophy by intentionally engineering sparsity-induced grating lobes as controllable auxiliary illumination beams for extended-target sensing. This paper exploits grating lobes and proposes a sparse-connected hybrid beamforming architecture that intentionally engineers and exploits grating lobes to enable single-shot, full-aperture illumination of extended targets while supporting multi-user downlink communication. A switch-controlled sparse RF network preserves the array aperture and generates a dominant main lobe with structured secondary lobes covering the entire target extent. A covariance-driven alternating-minimization framework jointly optimizes digital precoders, quantized phase shifters, and antenna-RF switching. Simulations at 140 GHz demonstrate near fully-digital Cramer-Rao sensing accuracy, competitive communication performance in low-rank THz channels, rapid convergence, and significant hardware and energy savings, establishing structured sparse connectivity as a scalable and energy-efficient solution for extended-target THz ISAC.

中文摘要翻译: 太赫兹（THz）频段的集成感知与通信（ISAC）能够实现超高分辨率感知，但面临一个关键限制：高方向性的太赫兹波束无法在单波束内照亮扩展目标。传统方案依赖顺序波束扫描，这会降低感知精度并增加能耗。此外，在常规稀疏阵列中，栅瓣通常被视为应加以抑制以避免模糊和干扰的不良伪影。相比之下，本文采用反向设计理念，有意利用稀疏性诱发的栅瓣作为扩展目标感知的可控辅助照明波束。本文利用栅瓣，提出一种稀疏连接的混合波束赋形架构，通过有意设计和利用栅瓣，实现对扩展目标的单次全孔径照明，同时支持多用户下行通信。开关控制的稀疏射频网络保留了阵列孔径，并生成一个主导主瓣和覆盖整个目标范围的结构化次级瓣。一种协方差驱动的交替最小化框架联合优化数字预编码器、量化移相器和天线-射频开关。140 GHz下的仿真表明，该方法实现了接近全数字的克拉美-罗感知精度、在低秩太赫兹信道中具有竞争力的通信性能、快速收敛以及显著的硬件和能耗节省，确立了结构化稀疏连接作为可扩展且节能的扩展目标太赫兹ISAC解决方案。

</details>


### [165] [Multibit Quantized Precoding for MU-mMIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15959)
*Getuar Rexhepi,Shreesal Shrestha,Christoph Studer,Giuseppe Thadeu Freitas de Abreu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多用户大规模MIMO下行链路、支持低分辨率DAC的多比特量化预编码方法（MQP），通过l0范数惩罚与分数规划凸化实现有限字母约束，并利用高斯置信传播降低复杂度，性能优于或接近现有最先进方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有量化预编码方法大多局限于1比特量化，难以支持多比特低分辨率DAC场景；同时超参数调优缺乏系统性和可复现性，且计算复杂度较高，亟需一种可扩展至任意比特精度且高效的多比特量化预编码方案。

Method: 提出MQP方法：通过l0范数惩罚近似有限字母约束，利用平滑代理函数重构问题，再经分数规划凸化；结合差异原则与渐进非凸性延续策略实现超参数自动化选取，推导出闭式最小二乘型迭代更新公式；进一步引入高斯置信传播（GaBP）将每次迭代的计算复杂度降为线性时间。

Result: 在不同规模系统下的仿真表明，MQP方法（含GaBP和不含GaBP两种变体）在各种信道条件下均能取得与现有最先进量化预编码算法相当或更优的误码率性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MQP方法成功将量化预编码拓展至多比特场景，兼顾了性能、计算效率与参数调优的规范性，是低分辨率DAC下行大规模MIMO系统中具有竞争力的预编码方案。

Original Abstract: We propose a novel multibit quantized precoding method for the downlink of multi-user massive MIMO systems with low-resolution digital-to-analog converters. The new method, termed multibit quantized precoding (MQP), enforces the finite-alphabet constraint through an l0-norm penalty, approximated by a smooth surrogate so as to yield a reformulated problem, which is then convexized via fractional programming, ultimately extending quantized precoding beyond 1-bit alphabets. The regularization parameter of the proposed method is selected via a discrepancy principle integrated with graduated non-convexity continuation, resulting in a principled and reproducible hyperparameter tuning method and an efficient iterative algorithm with a closed-form, least-squares-type update per iteration. In order to further reduce the computational complexity of the method, we include a Gaussian belief propagation (GaBP) step for turning the least-squares update in linear-time. Simulations performed for systems with different sizes demonstrate that both methods, namely the MQP with and without GaBP, achieve competitive or superior error-rate performance compared to state-of-the-art quantized precoding algorithms under various channel conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对配备低分辨率数模转换器的多用户大规模MIMO下行链路，提出了一种新颖的多比特量化预编码方法。新方法称为多比特量化预编码（MQP），通过l0范数惩罚来强制有限字母约束，该惩罚由光滑代理函数近似以得到重构问题，随后通过分数规划进行凸化，最终将量化预编码拓展至1比特字母表之外。所提方法的正则化参数通过结合差异原则与渐进非凸性延续策略进行选取，从而形成一种有原则且可复现的超参数调优方法，以及一种每步迭代具有闭式最小二乘型更新的高效迭代算法。为进一步降低方法的计算复杂度，我们引入高斯置信传播（GaBP）步骤，将最小二乘更新降至线性时间。针对不同规模系统的仿真表明，两种方法（即含GaBP和不含GaBP的MQP）在各种信道条件下均能取得与现有最先进量化预编码算法相当或更优的误码率性能。

</details>


### [166] [Inertial Human Motion Capture: From Biomechanics to Recent Sensor Fusion Methods and Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16000)
*Manon Kok,Ive Weygers,Hassan Osman,Daniel Weber,Ruiyuan Li,Thomas Seel,Ajay Seth*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教程式综述，聚焦惯性测量单元（IMU）用于人体运动捕捉的 kinematics 估计问题，识别了三大基本挑战（无直接关节角度、无自姿态测量、环境与动态影响可靠性），提出四个关键方面（目标、环境、主体属性、运动特征）来指导问题转化，并分享了利用人体运动链约束等新方法，旨在桥接传感器融合与生物力学两个领域。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管IMU在人体运动捕捉方面前景广阔，但从IMU测量中获取有意义的生物力学量仍然困难。现有方法在实际应用中存在严重局限性，如需要传感器-节段校准、磁场干扰降低关节角度精度等，导致许多用例在精度和可靠性上未充分发挥潜力。本文旨在弥合开发人体运动捕捉方法的传感器融合社区与需要准确、易用、可靠方法研究实验室外人体运动的生物力学社区之间的差距。

Method: 本文采用教程式综述方法，系统性地介绍了从IMU测量进行kinematics估计的四个关键方面（IMU人体运动捕捉目标、环境条件、主体与属性、运动特征），用于指导如何将生物力学问题转化为合适的传感器融合公式。文章识别了三大基本挑战，分析了现有方法的局限性，并分享了利用人体运动链约束等新方法的见解。还提供了与四个关键方面相关的引导性问题，以下肢关节角度估计为例展示了传统工作流与三种替代方案的对比，并开放了代码。

Result: 本文没有进行实验，而是通过分析指出：现有方法虽被广泛使用但在实际应用中存在严重局限，而利用人体运动链约束等新方法有潜力克服这些局限。以下肢关节角度估计为例，展示了传统工作流与三种替代方案的对比，并提供了开源代码。

Conclusion: 本文提出的四个关键方面框架和引导性问题可以有效地帮助研究者将生物力学问题转化为合适的IMU传感器融合公式。利用人体运动链约束等新方法有望克服现有方法的局限性，提升IMU人体运动捕捉的精度和可靠性，从而桥接传感器融合与生物力学两个社区。

Original Abstract: Inertial measurement units (IMUs) are a promising means to capture human motion, yet obtaining meaningful biomechanical quantities from IMU measurements remains non-trivial. This tutorial-style review focuses on kinematics and introduces four key aspects (inertial human motion capture objective, environmental conditions, subject & attributes, and motion characteristics) to determine how to translate biomechanical problems into adequate formulations for the fusion of inertial sensor measurements. We identify three fundamental challenges for kinematics estimation from IMUs: IMUs do not provide direct information about the joint angle, IMUs do not measure their own orientation, and real-world environments and dynamics compromise sensor reliability. Though there exist widely-used methods to overcome these challenges, they suffer from severe limitations in real-life applications, e.g., the need for sensor-to-segment calibration, and the fact that magnetic field disturbances degrade joint angle accuracy. The full potential for many use-cases hence remains untapped in terms of accuracy and reliability. We share insights into recently proposed methods, e.g. exploiting the human body's kinematic chain constraints, having the potential to overcome these limitations. We also present guiding questions related to the four key aspects and illustrate their use for navigating the methodological landscape for the use-case of lower-extremity joint angle estimation, for which we share open-access code and compare the traditional workflow with three alternatives. Our aim is to bridge the gap between the sensor fusion community developing methods for human motion capture and the biomechanics community in need of accurate, easy-to-use, and reliable methods to study human motion outside of the laboratory.

中文摘要翻译: 惯性测量单元（IMU）是一种捕捉人体运动的有前景的手段，然而从IMU测量中获取有意义的生物力学量仍然具有挑战性。本教程式综述聚焦于运动学，介绍了四个关键方面（惯性人体运动捕捉目标、环境条件、主体与属性、运动特征），以确定如何将生物力学问题转化为适合惯性传感器测量融合的公式。我们识别出从IMU进行运动学估计的三个基本挑战：IMU不提供关节角度的直接信息、IMU不测量自身姿态、以及真实世界的环境和动态会降低传感器可靠性。尽管存在广泛使用的方法来克服这些挑战，但它们在实际应用中存在严重局限性，例如需要传感器-节段校准，以及磁场扰动会降低关节角度精度。因此，许多用例在精度和可靠性方面的全部潜力仍未得到发挥。我们分享了最近提出的方法的见解，例如利用人体运动链约束的方法，这些方法有潜力克服上述局限性。我们还提出了与四个关键方面相关的引导性问题，并以下肢关节角度估计为例说明其在导航方法论版图中的使用，为此我们分享了开源代码，并将传统工作流与三种替代方案进行了比较。我们的目标是桥接开发人体运动捕捉方法的传感器融合社区与需要准确、易用且可靠的方法来研究实验室外人体运动的生物力学社区之间的差距。

</details>


### [167] [A Kalman Filter-Assisted Data-Predictive SAR ADC With Reduced Switching Energy for Low-Power Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16139)
*Xiyuan Feng,Yuxiang Zhao,Jie Xiong,Dian Lin,Yunlei Zhong,Wei Liu,Zhongheng Ji,Ruiyu Tian,Chenhao Zhuo,Yue Yin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种卡尔曼滤波辅助的数据预测型SAR ADC，用递归状态估计替代前4位比较器决策，实现MSB电容并行切换，减少50%开关能耗并缩短4个时钟周期量化时间。在180nm CMOS工艺下，20 MS/s、1.8V时功耗降低50.3%，SNR/SFDR达57.88/74.51 dB。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: IoT设备与可穿戴健康监测器对超低功耗ADC的需求迫切。传统SAR ADC中，高位MSB转换能耗占主导，且固定的N周期逐次逼近流程限制了每样本延迟的下界，亟需突破能效瓶颈。

Method: 卡尔曼滤波器利用完整转换历史在每周期开始前预测4位MSB，实现MSB电容的并行同时切换，消除冗余CDAC转换；结合优化的4位MSB开关方案进一步抑制硬件级残余开关；支持传统与预测双模式切换以保证鲁棒性。

Result: 180nm CMOS工艺，20 MS/s采样率，1.8V电源电压下，预测模式总功耗从1.96 mW降至0.975 mW（降低50.3%），在504 kHz输入频率下测得SNR 57.88 dB、SFDR 74.51 dB。

Conclusion: 所提卡尔曼滤波辅助数据预测SAR ADC显著提升了能效，在不显著牺牲动态性能的前提下实现了约50%的功耗降低和量化周期缩短，适用于能量受限的无线传感器网络。

Original Abstract: The proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) devices and wearable health monitors has created an urgent demand for ultra-low-power analog-to-digital converters (ADCs). Successive approximation register (SAR) ADCs are widely used in such applications, yet their energy efficiency remains constrained by the sequential bit-by-bit switching of the capacitive DAC (CDAC). The high-weight most significant bit (MSB) transitions dominate the total switching energy, and the rigid N -cycle conversion flow imposes a hard lower bound on latency per sample.This paper presents a Kalman filter-assisted data-predictive SAR ADC that replaces the first four comparator-driven decisions with a recursive state estimator. The Kalman filter predicts the 4 MSBs from the complete conversion history before each cycle begins, enabling simultaneous parallel switching of the MSB capacitors. This eliminates redundant CDAC transitions, shortens the quantization cycle by four clock periods, and reduces switching energy by approximately 50%. An optimized 4-bit MSB switching scheme further suppresses residual switching at the hardware level. The ADC, designed in a 180-nm CMOS process, supports configurable dual-mode operation, toggling between a conventional mode and the Kalman-driven predictive mode for robustness under erratic inputs. At 20 MS/s and a 1.8-V supply, the predictive mode reduces total power consumption by 50.3% (from 1.96 mW to 0.975 mW), with a measured SNR/SFDR of 57.88/74.51 dB at 504 kHz, confirming its suitability for energy-constrained wireless sensor networks.

中文摘要翻译: 物联网设备与可穿戴健康监测器的普及对超低功耗模数转换器（ADC）产生了迫切需求。逐次逼近寄存器（SAR）型ADC在此类应用中被广泛使用，但其能效仍受限于电容式DAC（CDAC）的逐位切换过程。高权重的最高有效位（MSB）转换主导了总开关能耗，且固定的N周期转换流程对每样本的延迟设定了硬性下界。本文提出一种卡尔曼滤波辅助的数据预测型SAR ADC，将前四次比较器驱动的决策替换为递归状态估计器。卡尔曼滤波器在每个周期开始前根据完整转换历史预测4位MSB，从而实现MSB电容的并行同时切换。这消除了冗余的CDAC转换，将量化周期缩短了四个时钟周期，并将开关能耗降低约50%。一种优化的4位MSB开关方案进一步在硬件层面抑制了残余开关。该ADC采用180-nm CMOS工艺设计，支持可配置的双模式操作，可在常规模式与卡尔曼驱动的预测模式之间切换，以应对不规则的输入信号。在20 MS/s采样率与1.8 V电源电压下，预测模式将总功耗降低了50.3%（从1.96 mW降至0.975 mW），在504 kHz输入频率下测得的SNR/SFDR为57.88/74.51 dB，证实了其适用于能量受限的无线传感器网络。

</details>
